### [1] [Uncertainty quantification of fatigue initiation life for powder bed fusion metal additive manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03163)
*Yulin Guo,Veera Sundararaghavan,Boris Kramer*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个将微观结构和缺陷不确定性直接传播到疲劳起裂寿命分布的框架，用于激光粉末床熔融增材制造部件的合格性鉴定。框架基于EBSD和微CT表征数据，通过三个物理仿真获得影响疲劳的量，并考虑其相关性推导出疲劳起裂寿命的闭式概率密度函数。应用于316L不锈钢部件，发现晶粒尺寸和孔隙分布均影响疲劳寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光粉末床熔融增材制造关键部件需要进行疲劳寿命预测且要求量化不确定性，但现有方法多采用保守的确定性预测，难以支撑知情决策。

Method: 从EBSD和微CT扫描获取微观结构和缺陷表征，驱动三个基于物理的仿真分别计算弹性应变能释放率、裂纹路径表面能和疲劳指示参数；考虑这些量共享微观结构导致的高相关性，推导疲劳起裂寿命的闭式概率密度函数。

Result: 将框架应用于EOS M290机器制造的316L不锈钢部件，结果表明晶粒尺寸和孔隙分布均影响疲劳起裂寿命分布：固定总孔隙体积分数时，较大晶粒导致疲劳寿命略微降低；相较于少量大孔隙，大量小孔隙更有利于疲劳寿命。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了疲劳起裂寿命的解析分布而非保守的确定性预测，有助于增材制造部件的合格性鉴定与部署决策，实现更明智的工程判断。

Abstract: Predicting fatigue life with quantified uncertainties is essential for the qualification of critical components produced by laser-based powder bed fusion additive manufacturing. We present a framework that propagates microstructure and defect uncertainties directly to a fatigue initiation life distribution for a specific part. In particular, microstructure and defect characterizations are obtained from electron backscatter diffraction and micro-computed tomography scan data, which in turn inform three physics-based simulations yielding the fatigue-affecting quantities: the elastic energy release rate, the surface energy along the crack path, and the fatigue indicator parameter. Accounting for the uncertainties in these quantities and the high correlations among them due to the shared underlying microstructure, we derive a closed-form probability density function for the fatigue initiation life. This provides an analytical distribution instead of conservative deterministic predictions and enables more informed decision making for the qualification and deployment of additively manufactured components. Applying the framework to 316L stainless steel parts produced by an EOS M290 laser powder bed fusion machine, we find that both grain sizes and void distributions influence the fatigue initiation life distribution. Specifically, for a fixed total void volume fraction, larger grain sizes cause a marginal reduction in fatigue initiation life, and a population of many small voids is more favorable for fatigue life than fewer, larger voids of equivalent total volume.

</details>


### [2] [Natural Disasters and the Nonprofit Sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04136)
*Mayleen Cortez-Rodriguez*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用1991至2021年的面板数据集和适配面板数据的因果推断方法，研究自然灾害破坏对县级非营利组织产出的因果影响。与以往研究发现灾害破坏与非营利组织收入或资产呈小规模正相关的结论相反，本文未发现任何因果效应的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然灾害对心理健康、贫困、经济活动等领域的影响已有大量研究，但灾害对非营利部门的影响研究不足，尽管非营利部门在地方或国家经济以及灾害响应与恢复中扮演着重要角色。

Method: 使用1991至2021年覆盖县级层面的面板数据集，采用专为面板数据设计的因果推断方法（如合成控制法或类似双重差分扩展方法）来分析自然灾害破坏对不同县级非营利产出的因果效应。

Result: 与以往研究发现灾害破坏与非营利组织收入或资产之间存在小规模正相关关系的结论相反，本文未发现自然灾害破坏对县级非营利组织产出存在因果效应的证据。

Conclusion: 本文对先前关于灾害与非营利部门关系的关联性研究提出了挑战，表明先前的正相关发现可能并非因果关系，这对灾害响应政策及非营利资源分配具有重要启示。

Abstract: When natural disasters strike, individuals, communities, and even entire countries can suffer. Researchers have studied the impacts of disasters on various factors of interest, from mental health, to poverty, to economic activity. However, the impact of disasters on the nonprofit sector is understudied despite the nonprofit sector's perhaps surprising role in local or national economies as well as its role in disaster response and recovery. Thus, we study the effect of natural disaster damage on different county-level nonprofit outcomes using a panel dataset spanning 1991 to 2021 and causal inference methods tailored to panel data. Contrary to prior work, which found small but positive associations between disaster damage and nonprofit revenue or assets, we find no evidence of a causal effect.

</details>

### [3] [Buckling Prediction for Nonlinear Elastic Beams with Soft Inclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02956)
*Thien Tran-Duc,J. E. Bunder,A. J. Roberts*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的多尺度计算框架，用于预测含周期性软夹杂弹性梁的屈曲及后屈曲行为。该框架在先前线性弹性多尺度patch计算均质化的基础上引入非线性，能够准确预测屈曲起裂点和后续后屈曲响应。微观计算仅在稀疏的patch子域进行，宏观行为通过patch耦合算法恢复。结果表明降低夹杂刚度会降低临界屈曲应变和应力，同时产生更缓和的后屈曲响应。与全域模拟相比，该框架在显著降低计算成本的同时准确预测了屈曲阈值和后屈曲行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多尺度计算均质化框架仅适用于线性弹性问题，无法预测含软夹杂周期性梁结构的屈曲和后屈曲行为，而这些结构在实际工程中常因压缩载荷而失稳，需要高效且准确的预测工具。

Method: 将先前针对线性弹性的多尺度patch计算均质化框架扩展至非线性领域；微观尺度仅在一组稀疏的小子域（patches）中执行计算，宏观尺度通过经过验证的patch耦合算法恢复；通过Jacobian矩阵的特征值分析精确预测失稳起裂点、临界应变和临界应力。

Result: 针对夹杂与基体杨氏模量比从0.001到1的梁进行了四分之一和半域patch计算评估。结果表明：降低夹杂刚度会降低临界屈曲应变和应力（增加失稳敏感性），同时产生更缓和的后屈曲响应（更小的横向挠度和应力下降）。特征值分析准确预测了失稳起裂及对应的临界值；并给出了压缩载荷下非线性屈曲构型的分岔图及插值阶数影响分析。与全域模拟相比，该框架在保持精度同时大幅降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: 该框架能够准确且高效地预测复合梁的屈曲起裂及后屈曲行为，且方法可方便地扩展至非均质梁、板、壳体及其他工程结构，具有良好的通用性和工程应用前景。

Abstract: We develop an efficient and accurate multiscale computational framework for predicting the buckling and post-buckling behaviour of elastic beams containing periodically distributed soft inclusions. The framework extends our previous multiscale, patch, computational homogenisation for linear elasticity by incorporating nonlinearity, and thus enables accurate prediction of both the buckling onset and the subsequent post-buckling response. Microscale computations are performed only within a sparse set of small subdomains (patches), while the macroscale behaviour is recovered through a proven patch-coupling algorithm. The scheme is assessed through quarter- and half-domain patch computations for beams with inclusion-to-matrix Young's modulus ratios ranging from 0.001 to 1. The results show that reducing the inclusion stiffness lowers both the critical buckling strain and the critical buckling stress, indicating an increased susceptibility to instability, while producing a milder post-buckling response with smaller transverse deflections and stress drops. Eigenvalue analysis of the Jacobian matrix accurately predicts the onset of instability and the corresponding critical strain and stress. Bifurcation diagrams of the nonlinear buckled configurations under compressive loading, and a quantitative analysis of the effect of the interpolation order on the predicted buckling and post-buckling responses, are also presented. Comparisons with full-domain simulations demonstrate that the proposed framework accurately predicts both the buckling threshold and the post-buckling behaviour while substantially reducing the computational cost. The methodology is readily extendable to heterogeneous beams, plates, shells, and other engineering structures.

</details>


### [4] [Dimensional hyperreduction of nonlinear finite element models via empirical cubature with manifold-adaptive weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03068)
*Joaquín A. Hernández,S. Ares de Parga,Riccardo Rossi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于参数化有限元问题的非线性流形降阶模型框架，核心创新在于允许超降阶（hyperreduction）的采样权重随潜在线性坐标连续非线性变化，而非传统方法中固定不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前采样加权超降阶方法中，权重在整个解流形上保持固定不变，这种限制未能充分挖掘超降阶的潜力——若让权重随潜在线标连续且非线性地变化，可进一步减少采样空间实体的数量，从而提升计算效率。

Method: 提出流形自适应权重经验积分法（MAW-ECM）：从可行的固定权重ECM规则出发，通过贪心剪枝策略移除采样实体，每次移除通过凸二次权重再分配问题实现，同时满足局部条件和权重非负性。非线性流形通过初始线性压缩加输入感知的潜坐标识别构建，使用模态幅度的线性组合（必要时融入图信息）以寻找解流形的内在维度。

Result: 在两个非线性基准问题（具有负增量刚度的超材料单胞均质化问题、历史相关连续损伤问题）上，非线性流形与MAW-ECM相结合使采样积分点数量相比标准线性降阶模型减少两个数量级以上；相比固定权重流形模型，自适应权重在均质化问题中消除约80%的剩余点，在损伤问题中消除超过97%的剩余点，且精度基本保持不变。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，允许权重随解流形非线性变化能够大幅缩短采样空间实体数量，有效提升非线性流形降阶模型的计算效率，同时保持精度，为参数化有限元问题的高效求解提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Nonlinear-manifold reduced-order models for parametrized finite element problems can achieve substantial compression both in the number of generalized (latent) coordinates and, through sampling-and-weighting hyperreduction, in the number of sampled elements/integration points. Yet current sampling-and-weighting approaches employ weights that remain fixed over the solution manifold. We contend that this restriction leaves hyperreduction potential untapped: allowing the weights to vary continuously and nonlinearly with the latent coordinates can further decrease the number of sampled spatial entities. To exploit this possibility, we propose the Manifold-Adaptive-Weight Empirical Cubature Method (MAW-ECM). Starting from a feasible fixed-weight ECM rule, a greedy pruning strategy removes sampled entities through convex quadratic weight-redistribution problems enforcing local conditions and positivity. The method is assessed on two nonlinear benchmarks: homogenization of a metamaterial unit cell exhibiting negative incremental stiffness, and a history-dependent continuum-damage problem. In both cases, the nonlinear manifold is constructed from an initial linear compression followed by an input-informed identification of the latent coordinates as general linear combinations of the retained modal amplitudes, incorporating graph information when relevant to seek the intrinsic dimensionality of the solution manifold. We show that combining the nonlinear-manifold representation with MAW-ECM reduces the number of sampled integration points by more than two orders of magnitude relative to the corresponding standard linear reduced model. Relative to the fixed-weight manifold models alone, the adaptive weights eliminate approximately 80% of the remaining points in the homogenization benchmark and more than 97% in the damage benchmark, while essentially preserving their accuracy.

</details>


### [5] [A discontinuous Petrov-Galerkin finite-element framework for the simulation of microwave-heated flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03155)
*Oreste Marquis,Matthias Maier,Bruno Blais*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于微波加热流模拟的高阶多物理场求解器，其将不连续Petrov-Galerkin（DPG）有限元方法用于时谐Maxwell方程，并与连续Galerkin有限元方法分别耦合于热方程和不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程，解决了微波辅助化学过程中的多物理场耦合模拟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微波辅助化学过程涉及电磁场、热传导和流体流动的强耦合，现有求解器难以同时高效处理具有奇异几何特征的多物理场问题，需要一种具备自适应网格细化能力和良好可扩展性的高阶多物理场数值框架。

Method: 采用DPG有限元法求解时谐Maxwell方程（利用其内置误差估计器实现自适应网格细化），结合连续Galerkin有限元方法求解热方程与不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程；电磁求解器采用共轭梯度（CG）迭代法，流体求解器采用无矩阵（matrix-free）实现以降低内存占用并提升并行可扩展性。

Result: 电磁求解器在矩形波导波传播、含奇异解的腔体问题及微波加热障碍物问题等多个基准测试中与文献中的数值及实验数据一致，验证了实现的有效性及自适应网格细化的能力；多物理场框架成功模拟了具有奇异几何特征的障碍物周围的微波加热流动。

Conclusion: 所提出的高阶多物理场求解器在准确性、自适应能力和内存效率方面均表现优越，具备低内存占用和可扩展性，适合大规模并行模拟，为微波辅助化学过程的模拟与优化提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We present a high-order multiphysics solver for the simulation of microwave-heated flows. The solver couples a discontinuous Petrov-Galerkin (DPG) finite element method for the time-harmonic Maxwell equations with continuous Galerkin finite element methods for the heat equation and the incompressible Navier-Stokes equations. We validate the electromagnetic solver against multiple benchmark problems: wave propagation in a rectangular waveguide, a cavity problem with a singular solution, and a microwave-heated obstacle problem, comparing our results against numerical and experimental data from the literature. The results confirm the validity of the implementation and demonstrate its ability to perform adaptive mesh refinement using the DPG method's built-in error estimator. The final part of the study showcases the capabilities of the multiphysics framework through simulations of microwave-heated flow around obstacles with singular geometric features. These results highlight the potential of the proposed framework for the simulation and optimization of microwave-assisted chemical processes. Finally, the developed high-order multiphysics solver has a low memory footprint, since the electromagnetic solver relies on a Conjugate Gradient (CG) iterative solver and the fluid solver is implemented in a matrix-free fashion, making the overall approach scalable and well-suited for large-scale parallel simulations.

</details>


### [6] [Coupled Tensor-Tensor Completion Method with Applications in Drug Repurposing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03190)
*Maryam Bagherian,Albert Hung,Ivo Dinov,Joshua Welch*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CTTC（Coupled Tensor-Tensor Completion）的新框架，用于在张量补全问题中引入张量形式的辅助信息（而非传统的矩阵形式），通过挖掘多模态张量间的隐藏连接来提升补全性能，并在两个药物效应基准数据集上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多生物医学问题可建模为张量补全问题，利用已观测条目补全缺失值。现有张量补全方法大多只能以矩阵形式引入辅助信息（如基因-基因相似性），难以充分利用以张量形式存在的更复杂的辅助信息，限制了补全性能的提升。

Method: 提出CTTC（Coupled Tensor-Tensor Completion）框架，通过耦合多个模态张量间的隐藏连接来引入张量形式的辅助信息；在理论上基于距离度量学习和群论建立基础；推导了交替优化算法求解CTTC的优化问题，并证明了算法收敛到驻点。

Result: 在两个基准数据集（DTD和LINCS）上，与HaLRTC、CTRC、Cell和NTDDR等其他张量补全方法相比，CTTC在运行时间和RSE（相对平方误差）张量补全精度方面均表现更优；在预测药物效应方面超过了最先进的张量补全方法。

Conclusion: CTTC作为一种能够整合张量形式辅助信息的张量补全新框架，兼具理论保证（距离度量学习与群论基础、收敛性证明）和实际优越性，为生物医学中的药物效应预测等张量补全任务提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Many biomedical challenges can be posed as tensor completion problems where the observed entries of a multidimensional array (a tensor) are used to impute the missing values. In such settings, incorporating side information about the modes of the tensor, such as gene-gene similarity, can significantly enhance the solutions of the completion problem. Most existing tensor completion methods can only incorporate side information in the form of matrices. In this study, we introduce a novel framework to incorporate side information in the form of tensors. Our new approach, called Coupled Tensor-Tensor Completion (CTTC), leverages the hidden connections among multimodal tensors to improve tensor completion performance. In addition to practical utility, CTTC has theoretical foundations in distance metric learning and group theory. We derive an alternating algorithm to solve the CTTC optimization problem and establish its convergence to a stationary point. Finally, we show that CTTC outperforms state-of-the-art tensor completion methods at predicting drug effects.
  Results: Compared with other tensor completion methods, including HaLRTC, CTRC, Cell, and NTDDR, CTTC demonstrates superior run-time and RSE tensor completion accuracy on two benchmark datasets, DTD and LINCS.

</details>


### [7] [A novel parallel approach for solving some free boundary value problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03214)
*Peeyush Singh,Amboru Yalmanda*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新颖高效的并行直接和间接迭代方法，用于求解一般的障碍问题与自由边界问题，并给出了理论收敛性分析及数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 障碍问题和自由边界问题在科学和工程中广泛存在，但传统方法在高维情形下面临计算复杂度高、收敛性缺乏理论保证等挑战，亟需高效且具有严格理论支撑的并行求解方案。

Method: 提出两类并行方案：(1) 直接并行方法，基于SPIKE算法求解一维障碍问题，并推广出快速递归版本；(2) 间接迭代方法，通过对凸集上的离散能量泛函进行逐阶段最小化来实现线性近似过程的收敛，涵盖光滑与非光滑情形。对于高维问题，采用方向分裂法将每个方向子问题转化为一维障碍最小化问题；将能量泛函最小化表述为抛物型时间依赖问题，并结合Armijo时间步长规则进行求解更新。此外，还探索了将该框架扩展为约束二次规划优化求解器的可能性，并应用于图像去模糊。

Result: 在模型问题产生M-矩阵的假设下，直接并行方法解的唯一性得以建立；在Ferris和Mangasarian框架下，对光滑与非光滑能量泛函在凸集上的收敛性给出了理论证明。数值实验验证了理论结果，并在图像去模糊任务中成功恢复原始图像。

Conclusion: 本研究为一般障碍和自由边界问题提供了一套兼具理论保证与计算效率的并行求解框架，其理论收敛性、加速策略以及向约束二次规划优化器和图像处理等应用的拓展表明该方法具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel, efficient class of parallel direct and indirect iterative schemes to solve general obstacle and free boundary value problems. The uniqueness of the solution for the direct parallel method is established under the assumption that the model problem yields an $M$-matrix. The convergence analysis of the indirect approach is predicated on minimizing the discrete energy functional at each iterative stage of the linear approximation process. Under the aforementioned setting, we establish theoretical convergence results for both smooth and nonsmooth energy functionals defined over a convex set, in the sense of Ferris and Mangasarian \cite{ferris1994parallel}. For the numerical computation of the one-dimensional obstacle problem, we adopt a direct generalized parallel approach based on the SPIKE algorithm \cite{polizzi2006parallel}. To accelerate convergence and reduce computational complexity, a fast recursive version of the scheme is generalized to the nonsmooth case. For two- and three-dimensional obstacle problems, we employ a directional splitting method that treats each directional subproblem as a one-dimensional obstacle minimization problem. By framing the energy functional minimization as a parabolic time-dependent problem and utilizing an Armijo time-stepping rule during the solution update process, we successfully obtain results for higher-dimensional obstacles. Additionally, this study explores the possibility of extending the algorithm into a constrained quadratic programming optimization solver. We also evaluate the framework on image deblurring phenomena under the aforementioned setting, successfully recovering the original images. Finally, numerical illustrations are provided to validate the theoretical results.

</details>


### [8] [Learning Informative Prior with Infinite-Dimensional Continuous Normalizing Flow for Bayesian Inverse Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03343)

For both regularity-adapted frequency distributions and uniform distributions on growing frequency windows, the resulting rates range from super-exponential to algebraic, depending on the regularity of the target. Second, we establish abstract error estimates for strong- and weak-form RFM discretizations, thereby converting the preceding approximation bounds into convergence estimates for multidimensional second-order elliptic boundary value and eigenvalue problems. Finally, for random feature matrices (RFMtxs), we prove super-exponential singular-value decay with Fourier features and exponential decay with $\tanh$ features, together with corresponding condition-number lower bounds. The analysis identifies a common mechanism: the same spectral approximation that yields high accuracy also drives severe ill-conditioning.

Abstract: We study the semi-discrete quadratic Wasserstein energy. The energy is nonsmooth at collisions of sites. Its value function is a classical interior solution of the exploratory Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation; the Laplacian of the value function is locally $C^1$, which yields a locally Lipschitz optimal temperature feedback. Independently of this optimal-control result, for every fixed Borel temperature rule bounded away from zero, and every sufficiently small step size, the associated Gaussian Euler chain is geometrically ergodic with a full-support invariant law. The raw iterates do not converge, whereas the best-so-far energy converges almost surely to the global minimum and the running record approaches the set of global minimizers.

Abstract: A common approach to representing disk-like and sphere-like geometries in isogeometric analysis is to use collapsed-edge singularities in the underlying parameterization. We also provide numerical experiments that validate the theory and illustrate the practical behavior of the construction with problems where preserving the structure is mandatory. These include a study of spurious harmonics that highlights how cohomology-breaking refinements differ between the polar and tensor-product settings, a discretization of a Maxwell eigenvalue problem on a locally-refined polar mesh, and an adaptive convergence study for a Hodge--Laplace problem.

Conclusion: Mini单元混合有限元法与非嵌套增广子空间算法在近不可压缩极限下均表现可靠高效，为该类问题的数值求解提供了理论保障与实用工具。

Abstract: This paper addresses the numerical solution of nearly incompressible linear elasticity boundary value problems and their associated eigenvalue problems. A mixed finite element formulation based on Mini element is proposed to circumvent the locking phenomenon that plagues standard low-order elements in the nearly incompressible limit. For the boundary value problem, we establish the well-posedness of mixed variational formulation and derive a priori error estimates that are uniform with respect to the Lamé constant $\underlineλ$, thereby proving the method's locking-free property. For the eigenvalue problem, we develop an efficient non-nested augmented subspace algorithm designed within the mixed finite element framework. A comprehensive convergence analysis is provided for the discrete eigenvalue approximation and the proposed iterative solver, demonstrating that the convergence rates remain independent of $\underlineλ$. Numerical experiments on both problems confirm the theoretical conclusions, showing optimal convergence rates and robustness as $\underlineλ \to \infty$. The results validate the effectiveness of Mini element and proposed augmented subspace algorithm for reliable and efficient computation in the nearly incompressible regime.

Conclusion: 所提出的有限元-代数通量校正组合方案能有效处理时间分数阶对流-扩散-反应方程，在保证离散极大值原理的同时维持收敛精度，对具有层状结构的解也具有良好表现。

Abstract: We study a time fractional convection-diffusion-reaction equation in a bounded domain $Ω\subset\mathbb{R}^2$. A stabilized numerical scheme satisfying a discrete maximum principle is constructed by combining the conforming linear finite element method with the algebraic flux correction method. The resulting semi-discrete scheme is nonlinear, and its well-posedness is established. Assuming nonsmooth initial data, we derive error estimates for the semi-discrete scheme using energy arguments. For the temporal discretization, we employ the L1 method, obtaining a fully discrete scheme for which we prove well-posedness and the discrete maximum principle. We also present numerical experiments that validate the order of convergence as well as we test our schemes to solutions that possess layers.

</details>

*Yuwen Li,Zhiyuan Yang*

TL;DR: 本文针对三角网格上基于最长边二分及最长边传播路径闭包的自适应细化算法，证明了累积闭包估计：#T_L - #T_0 ≲ Σ #M_ℓ，即全部细化产生的网格单元总数与累积标记数成线性增长关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应网格细化是偏微分方程数值求解的重要工具，其复杂度估计（即最终网格规模与标记数之间的线性关系）是保证自适应算法最优性的关键。此前对最长边二分算法缺乏类似DRB策略的累积复杂度分析。

Method: 证明分两个关键步骤。第一步利用平面三角剖分最长边二分的有限相似类定理，将可能直径集合转为有限个√2-几何格的并，从而证明每一步非终态步骤直径以统一严格因子增长，得到单标记创建的三角形的代际与空间局部性。第二步采用Binev–Dahmen–DeVore型收费论证，将单标记局部性转化为整个序列的累积估计。平局由终态优先级解决：若共享边是相邻三角形的最大边，则该对为终态并二分该边。

Result: 证明了对于任何自适应网格序列T_0, T_1, …, T_L及标记子集序列M_0,…,M_{L-1}，有累积闭包估计#T_L - #T_0 ≲ Σ_{ℓ=0}^{L-1} #M_ℓ，即总网格增长量关于累积标记数呈线性上界。

Conclusion: 该结果首次为最长边二分传播闭包策略提供了严格的累积复杂度分析，表明该算法的整体工作量与标记规模成正比，从而在复杂度意义上具有线性最优性，为自适应有限元方法的最优收敛性理论提供了重要的理论支撑。

Abstract: On triangular meshes, we analyze local mesh refinement based on longest-edge bisection equipped with the serial longest-edge propagation-path closure. Ties are resolved by terminal priority: if the incoming shared edge is a longest edge of the neighboring triangle, the pair is declared terminal and that edge is bisected. For every adaptive mesh sequence $\mathcal{T}_0, \mathcal{T}_1, \ldots, \mathcal{T}_L$ with marked subset sequence $\mathcal{M}_0, \mathcal{M}_1, \ldots, \mathcal{M}_{L-1}$, we prove the cumulative closure estimate $\#\mathcal{T}_L - \#\mathcal{T}_0 \lesssim \sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\#\mathcal{M}_\ell$. The proof has two ingredients. First, the finite-similarity-class theorem for planar longest-edge bisection turns the set of possible diameters into a finite union of $\sqrt{2}$-geometric lattices. Hence diameters grow by a uniform strict factor at every nonterminal step. This yields generation and spatial locality for all triangles created by a single mark. Second, a Binev--Dahmen--DeVore type charging argument converts this single-mark locality into the cumulative estimate.

</details>

*Massimiliano Fasi,Behnam Hashemi*

TL;DR: 本文部分回答了Nick Higham提出的关于Sylvester方程和Lyapunov方程解的适定性（conditioning）的开放问题。研究证明即使系数矩阵A、B、C及Kronecker和矩阵均具有完美适定性，Sylvester方程的解仍可能任意地病态；文中给出了解条件数的一般先验上界、A和B可对角化情形下的界，以及涉及矩阵指数和Zolotarev数的下界，并为两种重要Lyapunov方程设定提供了上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源于Sylvester和Lyapunov方程数值算法的向后稳定性分析。理解解的条件数对于评估算法数值稳定性至关重要，而Higham提出的这一开放问题此前尚未得到完全解答，需要更深入的理论刻画。

Method: 通过构造反例证明Sylvester方程解可任意病态（即使A、B、C及Kronecker和矩阵均适定）；利用矩阵理论推导条件数的一般先验上界，并对A和B可对角化情形给出更精确的界；通过引入矩阵指数和Zolotarev数建立下界；针对Lyapunov方程在（i）A对称正定且C对称负定及（ii）A严格耗散且C对称正定两种设定下分别获得条件数上界。

Result: 证明了Sylvester方程解的病态性并不依赖于系数矩阵或Kronecker和矩阵的适定性，即存在反例表明前者可任意的病态而后者均完美适定；建立了条件数的一般先验上界和对角化情形下的改进界，同时给出了基于矩阵指数和Zolotarev数的下界；在Lyapunov方程的两种重要设定下获得了条件数的上界估计。

Conclusion: 该工作对Nick Higham提出的开放问题给出了部分解答，深化了对Sylvester和Lyapunov方程解的适定性理论的理解，为相关数值算法的稳定性分析提供了理论基础；但问题的完全解答仍有待进一步研究。

Abstract: We partially answer an open problem, posed by Nick Higham, concerning the conditioning of solutions to Sylvester and Lyapunov equations. The question arises in the backward stability analysis of numerical algorithms for these equations. We first show that the solution to the Sylvester equation $AX-XB = C$ can be arbitrarily ill-conditioned even if $A, B, C$ and the Kronecker sum $I \otimes A - B^T \otimes I$ are all perfectly conditioned. We then derive general a priori bounds on the condition number of the solution, as well as bounds for the Sylvester equation when $A$ and $B$ are diagonalizable. We also provide lower bounds involving matrix exponentials and Zolotarev numbers. For the Lyapunov equation $AX+XA^T = -C$, we obtain upper bounds in two settings: (i) when $A$ is symmetric positive definite while $C$ is symmetric negative definite, and (ii) when $A$ is strictly dissipative and $C$ is symmetric positive definite.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a convergence theory for geometric multigrid with vertex-patch smoothers applied to cut finite element discretizations of the Poisson problem. The framework addresses non-inherited level forms and the mismatch between the physical and active domains. Using the discrete extension property, we prove two-level convergence bounds uniform in the mesh size and the cut geometry, and W-cycle bounds under an additional smallness assumption on the two-level rate. The numerical experiments intentionally use the stronger V-cycle, for which no convergence bound is claimed here.
  The convergence constants degrade with the degree $p$. Lowering the ghost penalty improves iteration counts. An aligned two-cell model exhibits a semidefiniteness threshold of order $p^{-2}$, whereas the visibility scale of a degree-$p$ cut mode decreases exponentially. Experiments at the model threshold reduce the iteration counts, but do not establish an assembled-operator threshold.

</details>

### [19] [AnyGS2Mesh: Feed-Forward Mesh Reconstruction from 3D Gaussian Splatting with Arbitrary-Resolution Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03304)
*Yuxuan Song,Fan Gao,Yibo Zhao,Jiarui Wen,Youcheng Cai,Ligang Liu*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: AnyGS2Mesh是首个前馈式3D网格重建框架，直接从3D高斯泼溅表示重建3D网格，支持任意输入图像分辨率，通过高斯引导的Transformer架构实现近实时的高质量网格重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从高斯场景表示进行3D网格重建的方法主要依赖迭代优化，导致推理速度慢且难以扩展到高分辨率输入，缺乏高效的前馈式解决方案。

Method: 提出高斯引导的Transformer架构，包含三个关键组件：(1)高斯引导空间推理Transformer，将高斯基元表示为结构化3D token，联合推理高斯与图像特征；(2)流式分块几何编码器，顺序处理原始分辨率视图并跨可变长度视图集聚合信息；(3)尺度对齐混合深度细化器，采用PatchFusion风格的编码器-解码器融合RGB条件预测深度与高斯渲染度量深度。细化后的深度图经TSDF融合和Marching Cubes提取网格。

Result: 实验表明AnyGS2Mesh在重建质量上达到最先进水平，同时相比基于优化的基线显著减少推理时间，实现了近实时的网格重建。

Conclusion: 验证了将高斯表示与前馈Transformer架构结合用于可扩展3D几何重建的潜力。

Abstract: Existing 3D mesh reconstruction methods from Gaussian scene representations predominantly rely on iterative optimization, resulting in slow inference and limited scalability to high-resolution inputs. In this paper, we present AnyGS2Mesh, the first feed-forward framework for directly reconstructing 3D meshes from 3D Gaussian Splatting representations with support for arbitrary input image resolutions. Our approach incorporates a Gaussian-Guided Transformer architecture that exploits explicit 3D geometric priors for efficient mesh generation. We introduce three key components: (1) a Gaussian-Guided Spatial Reasoning Transformer represents Gaussian primitives as structured 3D tokens and jointly reasons over Gaussian and image features; (2) a Streaming and Patchwise Geometry Encoder processes native-resolution views sequentially and aggregates information across variable-length view sets; (3) a Scale-Aligned Hybrid Depth Refiner uses a PatchFusion-style encoder--decoder to fuse RGB-conditioned predicted depth with Gaussian-rendered metric depth, combining fine local structures with globally consistent metric scale. The refined depth maps are integrated through TSDF fusion, followed by Marching Cubes for deterministic mesh extraction. Extensive experiments show that AnyGS2Mesh achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction quality while significantly reducing inference time compared with optimization-based baselines, enabling near-real-time, high-quality mesh reconstruction. Our results demonstrate the potential of combining Gaussian representations and feed-forward Transformer architectures for scalable 3D geometry reconstruction. The code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>

### [20] [Seeing Less Is Not Seeing Safely: Privacy Leakage from Task-Scoped Robot Perception Exports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03055)
*Yuqiao Xu,Erman Ayday*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出任务功能感知蒸馏（TFPD）框架，用于在家庭机器人中按任务范围导出结构化表示，同时量化并降低隐私风险。通过在120个AI2-THOR场景上的导航、碰撞检测和目标执行实验表明，任务等价性与隐私不等价性同时存在，且没有通用隐私排序，需进行任务特定的多风险评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管原始传感器数据保留在本地，下游规划器、云服务、日志或学习管线接收的结构化表示仍可能通过语义、几何、空间结构和任务目标泄露家庭信息。现有方法缺乏系统评估表示导出中任务效用与多种推测性隐私风险之间权衡的框架。

Method: 引入TFPD（任务功能感知蒸馏）框架，将丰富的感知结果保留在本地，而根据任务效用、直接暴露以及多种残差推断风险来评估下游导出。使用120个AI2-THOR场景，场景不相交的训练/验证/测试划分，冻结攻击者选择和表示感知的留存攻击，评估导航、碰撞检测和目标目标执行。

Result: 三种导航导出实现相同的成功率（1.000）和平均路径比（0.898），但表示层链接性从0.532到0.970不等。用目标区域替代显式目标标签可将目标类别宏F1从1.000降至0.077，同时保持0.995的成功率；几何粗化将对象类别宏F1从0.704降至0.556，但带来可测量的碰撞效用代价。ProcTHOR复现实验保留了任务等价性/隐私不等价性发现，但改变了归一化与拓扑导出的相对排序。

Conclusion: 字段移除或更强抽象均无法产生通用的隐私排序，表明需要对完整的公共表示进行任务特定、多风险评估，以在家庭机器人中实现隐私保护的表示导出。

Abstract: Domestic robots rely on rich perception to operate in private homes, but privacy risk persists even when raw sensor data remain local. Structured representations exported to downstream planners, cloud services, logs, or learning pipelines can still reveal household information through semantics, geometry, spatial structure, and task targets. We introduce Task-Functional Perception Distillation (TFPD), a task-scoped representation-export framework that keeps rich perception local and profiles downstream exports according to task utility, direct exposure, and multiple residual inference risks. Using 120 AI2-THOR scenes with scene-disjoint train/validation/test splits, frozen attacker selection, and representation-aware held-out attacks, we evaluate navigation, collision checking, and object-goal execution. Three navigation exports achieve identical success (1.000) and mean path ratio (0.898), yet representation-level linkability ranges from 0.532 to 0.970. Replacing an explicit target label with a target region reduces target-category macro-F1 from 1.000 to 0.077 while preserving success at 0.995, while geometric coarsening reduces object-category macro-F1 from 0.704 to 0.556 at a measurable collision-utility cost. A ProcTHOR replication preserves the navigation task-equivalence/privacy-inequivalence finding while changing the relative ordering of normalized and topological exports. These results show that neither field removal nor stronger abstraction induces a universal privacy ordering and motivate task-specific, multi-risk evaluation of the complete public representation.

</details>


### [21] [GPU-Accelerated Astrodynamics World Models for Spacecraft Rendezvous and Proximity Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03067)
*Duncan Eddy,Isaac R. Ward,Grace Ra Kim,Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次将世界模型（World Model）范式应用于航天器交会与近距操作领域，提出了基于JAX的ISS对接仿真环境和新颖的Out-of-this-World-Model（基于Transformer与流匹配的世界模型），在自主对接任务中展现出优于强化学习基线的样本效率、任务表现和分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在机器人和游戏环境中已有显著成果，但尚未被应用于航天领域。作者旨在将这一表示学习范式引入航天器交会与近距操作任务，以利用世界模型在多步规划和不确定性估计方面的优势，解决传统强化学习方法在该任务中样本效率低和泛化能力不足的问题。

Method: 作者首先构建了一个开源的、基于JAX的ISS对接仿真环境，利用GPU并行模拟航天器轨道与姿态动力学，以生成大规模状态-动作转移数据；随后提出了Out-of-this-World-Model，一种基于Transformer的世界模型，将相对运动状态与星载相机图像编码为潜状态，并通过单步流匹配（one-step flow matching）预测其在指令推力/力矩作用下的演化，从而输出未来观测的分布并刻画随机动力学和逐时间步不确定性。

Result: 在胶囊体自主对接ISS任务中，该方法在保持禁区内约束下实现了53%对29%的对接成功率（优于强化学习基线）、更好的样本效率和训练参数效率（少于DreamerV3式基线），在未见的对接口上分布外泛化能力显著提升（40%对17%），并对接近过程中遇到的异常物体实现了98%的分类准确率。

Conclusion: 世界模型范式在航天器交会与近距操作中展现出显著潜力，不仅在任务性能和样本效率上超越强化学习方法，还具备良好的分布外泛化与异常检测能力。作者开源了仿真环境和模型架构，以促进该范式在航天领域的进一步研究。

Abstract: World models are an emerging paradigm in representation learning in which an agent jointly learns state-action dynamics and observation models from offline trajectory data, enabling multi-step planning and trajectory prediction with uncertainty estimates. They have shown strong results in robotics and game environments, but, to the best of our knowledge, have not previously been applied to the space domain. This paper introduces a world model-based approach to cooperative and non-cooperative spacecraft rendezvous and proximity operations. First, we introduce an open-source, JAX-based International Space Station (ISS) docking environment supporting parallel GPU simulation of spacecraft orbit and attitude dynamics, generating the thousands of state-action transitions that world model training requires. Second, we introduce Out-of-this-World-Model, a transformer-based world model that encodes relative kinematic states and body-fixed camera imagery into a latent state and predicts its evolution under commanded thrusts and torques using one-step flow matching. It produces a distribution over future observations, capturing stochastic dynamics and per-timestep uncertainty, and outperforms DreamerV3-style posterior-correction baselines with fewer trainable parameters and hyperparameters. Third, we apply the approach to a capsule autonomously docking with the ISS under keep-out-zone constraints, demonstrating improved sample efficiency and task performance over reinforcement learning baselines (53% versus 29% docking success across ports), better out-of-distribution generalization (on held-out ports the world model more than doubles baseline success, 40% versus 17%), and detection of anomalous objects encountered during approach with 98% classification accuracy. We open-source the simulation environment and model architecture to enable further study of this paradigm.

</details>

*Yue Yang,Diego Romeres,Chiori Hori,Gedas Bertasius,Daniel Szafir,Siddarth Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种称为"模态纠缠"的失败模式，即VLA策略在多模态传感器训练中过度依赖传感器间的虚假相关性而非任务相关信号。作者提出证据门控正则化（EGR）训练目标，通过逐帧、逐传感器的任务相关性信号来门控两个状态条件一致性目标，在不增加推理开销的情况下提升模型在遮挡和干扰物下的鲁棒性。EGR在BEHAVIOR-1K基准及两个异构真实机器人平台上均显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在有限且同质的机器人演示数据上训练时，模型会利用传感器间的虚假相关性（即模态纠缠），而非任务相关的真实信号。这导致在真实环境中遇到遮挡或干扰物时，模型对无信息传感器的损坏高度敏感，且当仅剩单一信息传感器时性能严重不足。

Method: 提出Evidence-Gated Regularization (EGR)，一种与模态无关的训练目标。EGR推导逐帧逐传感器的任务相关性（证据）信号，以此门控两个状态条件一致性目标：(1) 对低证据传感器施加不变性约束；(2) 对高证据传感器施加单传感器充分性约束。EGR在推理时零额外开销。

Result: 在BEHAVIOR-1K基准上：全模态成功率从12.5%提升至16.4%（+31%）；无信息传感器损坏时从9.4%提升至16.5%（+75%）；单传感器回退时从2.8%提升至6.1%（+120%）。在真实机器人上，双臂平台在有物理干扰物时SR从30%提升至85%（+183%），触觉平台从55%提升至70%（+27%）。

Conclusion: EGR有效缓解了模态纠缠问题，通过在训练时利用动态证据信号区分任务相关与不相关的传感器信息，显著提升VLA策略在遮挡、传感器损坏及干扰物场景下的鲁棒性和成功率，且不增加任何推理负担，具有良好的泛化性和实用性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies fuse multimodal sensory inputs, but training on limited and homogeneous robot demonstrations encourages spurious inter-sensor correlations rather than task-relevant signal, a failure we term modality entanglement. Under real-world occlusions and distractors, this manifests as nuisance sensitivity to corruption of uninformative sensors and single-modality insufficiency when only one informative sensor remains intact. We propose Evidence-Gated Regularization (EGR), a modality-agnostic training objective that introduces zero inference-time overhead. EGR derives a per-frame and per-sensor task-relevance signal to gate two state-conditional consistency objectives: invariance on low-evidence sensors, and single-sensor sufficiency on high-evidence ones. We introduce a benchmark based on BEHAVIOR-1K, comprising a fast inference-only diagnostic suite and 47 rollout-based skills targeting modality entanglement. We validate EGR on this benchmark and on two real-robot setups with fundamentally different embodiments: a bi-manual setup with two Kinova arms and three RGB cameras, and a single-arm MELFA ASSISTA setup combining vision and GelSight tactile sensors. EGR improves simulation success rates (SR) from 12.5% to 16.4% under full modalities (+31%), from 9.4% to 16.5% under uninformative-sensor corruption (+75%), and from 2.8% to 6.1% under single-sensor fallback (+120%). Under physical-object distractors, EGR boosts SR from 30% to 85% on the bi-manual setup (+183%) and from 55% to 70% on the tactile setup (+27%).

*Jiahe Wang,Eron Ristich,Sultan Haidar Ali,Eric weissman,Lei Zhang,Wanxin Jin,Yi Ren,Jiefeng Sun*

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Koopman模型预测控制的框架，将全局与局部可观测量结合，实现多段软体机械臂的实时形状控制。数值实验验证了最多10段独立驱动段的可扩展性，物理实验验证了3段和5段机械臂的实时形状控制（尖端速度达0.6 m/s）、无需重训练的鲁棒跟踪（含400g末端负载和7N侧向扰动恢复）以及受限空间巡检应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多段软体机械臂的形状控制比尖端控制更具挑战性，因为连续变形具有高维度和非线性动力学特性。现有工作仅以全局坐标系下的形状误差作为控制目标，对多段软体臂而言不足——段间耦合、重力加载和惯性效应会随段数增加而显著，导致控制精度严重下降。

Method: 提出Koopman-based模型预测控制（MPC）框架，通过结合全局和局部可观测量来同时表征形变的整体构型与段间细节，克服coordination维度和非线性带来的建模困难，实现实时在线优化控制。

Result: 数值实验：在最多10个独立致动段的机器人上实现形状控制，验证可扩展性。物理实验：(1) 3段和5段机械臂实时形状控制，尖端速度达0.6 m/s；(2) 无需重训练的鲁棒跟踪，包括400g远端负载和7N侧向扰动后的恢复；(3) 受限空间巡检演示验证了实际应用潜力。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了多段软体机械臂的动态、可扩展且精确的实时形状控制，为受限空间下的实际应用（如巡检）提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Multi-segment soft robotic arms can continuously reconfigure their body shapes for safe interaction, but tip control alone is insufficient for constrained-space tasks. Therefore, shape control is a more important task for multi-segment soft arms than tip control, but remains challenging due to the high dimensionality and nonlinear dynamics of continuum deformation. In existing work, shape control accuracy is defined by the error in the global frame (global shape error). For multi-segment soft arms, using only global shape error as the control objective is insufficient, as segment coupling, gravity-induced loading, and inertial effects become more significant. This difficulty increases with the number of segments. In this paper, we present a Koopman-based model predictive control framework that combines global and local observables, enabling real-time shape control on multi-segment soft robotic arms. The framework is evaluated through numerical and physical experiments. Numerical experiments demonstrate the scalability of the proposed controller by achieving shape control on robots with up to 10 independently actuated segments. The physical experiments demonstrate that the controller is capable of (1) real-time shape control of 3- and 5-segment robotic arms with tip speeds up to 0.6 m/s, (2) robust tracking without retraining, including distal payloads up to 400~g and recovery from a 7~N lateral disturbance, and (3) the potential for future inspection applications through a confined-space demonstration. These results demonstrate that the proposed framework enables dynamic, scalable, and accurate real-time shape control on multi-segment soft robotic arms.

</details>


### [24] [Following a Unique Path: A Fast Certifier Applied to Outlier-Robust Pose Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03222)
*Connor Holmes,Abhishek Goudar,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CP-Cert（中心路径认证器），一种针对存在退化现象的SDP松弛问题的可认证求解方法。通过从候选解出发寻找可行域中靠近中心路径的区域来获取有效证书，利用间接线性代数技巧、问题稀疏性和并行性提升效率。应用于矩阵加权位姿配准和点云数据关联（后者提出了新的SDP松弛），在模拟和真实数据上均验证了其速度和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可认证方法采用"先局部求解再认证"的思路，利用凸半定规划（SDP）松弛保证非凸问题解的全局最优性。然而，许多机器人学中的问题在松弛中表现出某种退化形式，阻碍了这种高效的两阶段方法，使得不得不使用昂贵的松弛优化作为唯一手段。因此需要一种专门针对此类退化问题设计的高效可认证方法。

Method: CP-Cert以候选解为起点，在可行空间中搜索靠近中心路径（central path）的区域，在此区域中可以方便地获得有效证书。关键技术创新包括：利用间接线性代数技术处理退化问题、利用问题稀疏性和并行计算保持效率。此外，论文还提出了点云数据关联问题的新颖SDP松弛。

Result: 在模拟数据上，CP-Cert展现出比最先进的直接求解器快多达三个数量级的运行时间，并展示了所提出新SDP松弛的性质。最后，论文将这些贡献整合为可认证的、对离群点鲁棒的位姿估计流水线，并在真实数据上进行了验证。

Conclusion: CP-Cert为存在松弛退化问题的可认证方法提供了有效的替代方案，显著提升了此类问题的求解效率。结合新颖的点云数据关联SDP松弛，形成了完整的可认证鲁棒位姿估计流水线，在实际应用中具有很好的前景。

Abstract: Certifiable methods have arisen as a means to guarantee global optimality of solutions to non-convex problems using convex semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxations. The most performant of these methods use a local solver to obtain the candidate solution, and then certify its optimality using efficient linear algebra techniques. However, for many problems of interest in robotics, this local-solve-then-certify approach is impeded by a form of degeneracy in the relaxation, leaving a costly optimization of the relaxation as the only recourse. In this paper, we introduce our Central-Path Certifier (CP-Cert), a certifiable method explicitly tailored to certify candidate optima to problems that exhibit this form of degeneracy. Using a candidate as a starting point, our approach seeks a nearby region of the feasible space -- known as the central path -- where a valid certificate can be readily obtained. The approach is kept efficient by exploiting indirect linear algebra techniques, problem sparsity, and parallelism. We apply CP-Cert to both matrix-weighted pose registration and pointcloud data association, whose novel SDP relaxation is of independent interest. On simulated examples, we explore the properties of this novel relaxation and show that CP-Cert is fast and scalable, achieving runtimes that are up to three orders of magnitude faster than state-of-the-art direct solvers. Finally, we combine these contributions into a certifiable, outlier-robust pose-estimation pipeline, which we apply to real-world data.

</details>


### [25] [Long-Horizon Consistent and Interaction-Aware World Models for Multi-Style End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03225)
*Yuxuan Han,Kunyuan Wu,Liyunong Yang,Zilu Wang,Cansen Jiang,Yi Xiao,Liang Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了StyleDrive，一个基于世界模型强化学习的端到端自动驾驶框架，通过时间一致性正则化、状态解耦和群体相对策略优化，在Bench2Drive基准上取得88.44的驾驶分数和66.82%的成功率，超越此前最佳世界模型方法，并在真实AGV平台上验证了sim-to-real迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于世界模型的端到端自动驾驶方法存在三大局限：长时程想象rollout中的时间不一致性、对自我与环境交互建模不充分、以及对多样化驾驶风格的适应能力有限。这些问题制约了世界模型方法的想象学习效率和最终驾驶性能。

Method: StyleDrive包含三个核心组件：(1) 时间一致性正则化：通过门控交叉注意力整合历史潜在状态，稳定长时程想象rollout并缓解误差累积；(2) 显式状态解耦模块：将自我相关与自我无关的交互状态分离，提升复杂交通场景中决策的可解释性和效率；(3) 通过群体相对策略优化支持多样驾驶风格，以轨迹级相对优势替代逐步骤奖励优化，降低奖励方差并支持多风格驾驶而无需重新训练。

Result: 在Bench2Drive闭环驾驶基准上，StyleDrive取得88.44的驾驶分数（较此前最佳世界模型方法提升17.08）和66.82%的成功率（提升16.58）。此外在真实自动导引车平台上验证了动态驾驶场景中良好的sim-to-real迁移能力。

Conclusion: StyleDrive通过统一的学习范式同时解决长时程一致性、交互状态解耦和多样驾驶风格三大问题，显著超越了现有世界模型驱动的自动驾驶方法，并展现出实际部署的可行性，为基于世界模型的自动驾驶学习提供了有效的前进方向。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has increasingly adopted world model-based reinforcement learning frameworks to improve learning efficiency through \textit{imagined rollouts}. However, existing world models suffer from three key limitations: temporal inconsistency in long-horizon imagined rollouts, inadequate modeling of ego-environment interactions, and limited adaptability to diverse driving styles. To address these challenges, we propose \textit{StyleDrive}, a world-model-based learning framework that jointly enforces long-horizon consistency, explicitly disentangles interactive traffic states, and supports multi-style policy optimization within a unified learning paradigm. First, we introduce a temporal consistency regularization that integrates historical latent states through gated cross-attention, stabilizing long-horizon imagined rollouts and mitigating error accumulation. Second, we design an explicit state disentanglement module that separates ego-relevant from ego-irrelevant interactive states, enabling more interpretable and efficient decision-making in complex traffic scenarios. Third, we enable multi-style driving behaviors through Group Relative Policy Optimization, which replaces per-step reward optimization with trajectory-wise relative advantages, reducing reward variance and supporting diverse driving styles without retraining. We evaluate StyleDrive on the Bench2Drive closed-loop driving benchmark, achieving a driving score of 88.44 (+17.08 over the previous best world model-based method) and a success rate of 66.82 (+16.58). Furthermore, we deploy StyleDrive on a real automated guided vehicle platform and demonstrate promising sim-to-real transfer capability in dynamic driving scenarios.

</details>

Abstract: This paper presents an experimental design for constructing a multimodal dataset to analyze user engagement in human-robot interaction (HRI). Prior studies have mainly relied on observable behavioral cues, with limited frameworks integrating physiological signals. We therefore propose a structured data-collection protocol to build a multimodal dataset that includes wearable physiological signals, behavioral data, and self-report measures under different levels of task complexity defined in this experiment.

</details>


### [27] [R2S-Eval: Robot Evaluation with Real-to-Sim Calibration via Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03276)
*Yidi Wang,Feixiang Ruan,Ruoqu Chen,Jie Yin,Yang Yu,Mengdi Xu,Kaifeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出R2S-Eval评估管线，将真实到模拟（real-to-sim）校准与视觉语言模型（VLM）偏好评估相结合，以自动化、统计稳定且质量感知的方式评估机器人操作策略，取代传统人工成功率计数评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统真实世界机器人评估成本高、不稳定且信息量有限。其需要重复硬件试验、人工场景重置和持续监控，不同次评估可能产生不同的策略排名，且仅依赖成功率等二元指标，无法反映执行质量。而人类评估机器人时是通过观察和比较完整行为，而非仅看二元成功结果。

Method: R2S-Eval包含两个核心组件：1）real-to-sim校准组件，在标定到真实评估场景的模拟器中高效生成rollout视频，减少重复硬件试验需求；2）VLM评估器，评估rollout视频的执行质量并生成成对偏好，随后聚合成策略排名。此外还引入了一套协议，用于评估该管线是否得出经过验证的策略结论，同时缓解传统真实评估的关键挑战。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的实验表明：R2S-Eval能产生可靠且稳定的策略结论，与人类偏好达成一致，大幅减少重复硬件操作工作量，并能揭示二元成功标签无法捕捉的行为质量差异。

Conclusion: R2S-Eval推动机器人评估从人工成功率计数迈向自动化、统计稳定、质量感知的行为评估范式。

Abstract: Evaluating robot manipulation policies is becoming increasingly important as generalist models, particularly vision-language-action (VLA) models, are deployed on physical robots. However, conventional real-world evaluation remains labor-intensive, unstable, and insufficiently informative. It requires repeated hardware trials, manual scene resets, and continuous operator monitoring, may produce different policy rankings across repeated evaluations, and primarily relies on success-rate metrics that provide limited information about execution quality. In contrast, humans assess robot performance by observing and comparing complete behaviors rather than relying solely on binary success outcomes. To this end, we propose R2S-Eval, an evaluation pipeline that combines real-to-sim calibration with vision-language model (VLM) preference evaluation. The real-to-sim component efficiently generates rollout videos in a simulator calibrated to the real-world evaluation setting, thereby reducing the need for repeated hardware trials. The VLM evaluator assesses the execution quality of rollout videos and produces pairwise preferences, which are subsequently aggregated into policy rankings. We further introduce a protocol to assess whether the proposed evaluation pipeline yields validated policy conclusions while mitigating the key challenges of conventional real-world evaluation. Experiments in both simulation and real-world settings demonstrate that R2S-Eval produces reliable and stable policy conclusions, achieves agreement with human preferences, substantially reduces repeated hardware-operation effort, and reveals behavior-quality differences that are not captured by binary success labels. In general, R2S-Eval advances robot evaluation from manual success counting toward automated, statistically stable, and quality-aware evaluation of robot behavior. Project page: https://r2s-eval.github.io.

</details>


### [28] [ARTiS: An Adaptive Robotic Gripper for Enhanced Tool Manipulation in Disassembly Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03362)
*Roman Mykhailyshyn,Domae Yukiyasu,Harada Kensuke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ARTiS（自适应机器人工具抓取器），一种结合软体抓取器适应性、仿人手指灵活性与刚性机构鲁棒性的新型抓手，通过软掌心主动堵塞与指尖鳍状适应性实现工具的稳固握持，以七自由度设计实现高灵活性，适用于拆卸系统中的自动化与协作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具握持与使用在装配和拆卸过程中对机器人和人都具有挑战性，现有机器人抓取技术在操作工具时往往存在显著局限性，难以兼顾安全性、适应性与效率。

Method: 设计ARTiS新型抓手：结合软体掌心和指尖的适应性（主动堵塞+鳍状仿生效应的fin-ray适应）、仿人手的灵活性（七自由度设计使指尖能朝向任意表面）以及刚性机构的鲁棒性，用于在多样化情境中安全地握持工具。

Result: 使用一系列常规拆卸工具进行了全面评估，验证了抓手的顺应性、耐久性与功能多样性。

Conclusion: ARTiS通过刚-软混合设计有效弥合了软体抓手的适应性与刚性抓手的鲁棒性之间的差距，为拆卸系统中的工具握持提供了可行的自动化与协作解决方案。

Abstract: Grasping and holding tools while using them presents a considerable challenge not only for robots but also for humans. Such a challenge is particularly noticeable in processes involving assembly and disassembly, where efficiency and consistency depend on performing rapidly adaptive tasks. Nonetheless, contemporary robotic grasping technologies that can securely manipulate tools during operation frequently have significant constraints. In this paper, introduce ARTiS (Adaptive Robotic Tool Gripper in Disassembly Systems), a novel gripper that combines the adaptability of soft grippers, the dexterity of anthropomorphic hands, and the robustness of rigid mechanisms with a soft palm and fingertips. This unique combination makes it possible to hold tools securely in a variety of situations through using active jamming in the palm and fin-ray adaptation in fingertips. Furthermore, high finger dexterity is achieved through the seven degrees of freedom design, which enables the fingertips to orient to any surface, both for automated solutions and collaborative tasks. A comprehensive evaluation was conducted using a range of conventional disassembly tools to assess the gripper's compliance, durability, and functional versatility. More information, hardware instructions, and videos at https://romanmykhailyshyn.github.io/artis/

</details>

Abstract: Highly varying production sets increasing challenges for robotic manufacturing and indoor logistics. New capabilities for agility, flexibility, and robustness are needed. Robot skills, integrating motions, tool operations, and sensor perceptions consistently provide an execution mechanism for a versatile set of tasks with varying parameters. In this paper, easy-to-use CAD-model based programming and execution system for parametrized skills and skill monitors is showcased. The execution control structure is dynamic and parametrized, based on a modified Behavior Tree, where only event based communication is used. A human-robot-crane collaborative skill is shown as a test example, where a human instructs an overhead crane and a manipulator in inserting a heavy object supported by the crane, and guided by the manipulator, into the goal.

</details>


### [30] [Air-Ground Collaborative Vision-and-Language Navigation via Shared Bird's-Eye Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03483)
*Shuning Zhang,Liang Li,Yunheng Wang,Tao Wang,Yihang Kang,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次提出空地协同视觉语言导航（AGC-VLN）的无训练基线方法。核心思路是将无训练导航分解为基于VLM的语义推理和确定性几何执行，通过UAV的全局俯视图叠加UGV位置与目标标记构建共享鸟瞰图，UGV据此进行路径规划与闭环执行，同时UAV采用3D-SPF空间搜索升级算法定位并飞向目标。在CARLA-Air的Town10HD场景100个闭环回合中达到77.0%的联合成功率，比较弱的单智能体UAV（50.0%）提升27个百分点，比最强已发布单智能体基线超出24个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空地协同VLN（UAV具备全局俯视视角、UGV具备局部第一人称视角）这一设置此前基本未被探索：现有无训练方法只能解决单智能体任务、缺乏协同机制，而近期CARLA-Air评测发现五种最先进的VLA模型均未表现出稳定的协同行为；朴素语义通信或双向耦合甚至会降低性能。因此亟需建立空地协同VLN的有效协作框架。

Method: AGC-VLN将无训练导航分解为VLM语义推理与确定性几何执行两部分，揭示出协作接口：UAV在其全局视图上渲染UGV报告的位置以及VLM锚定的目标作为CAR/GOAL标记并附距离标签，形成共享的鸟瞰图。UGV从该图获取第一人称视角无法提供的全局空间上下文，用冻结的VLM规划沿道路行驶的路径并在闭环控制下执行；同时UAV运行3D-SPF（SPF的空间搜索升级版），在俯视图中定位目标并朝其飞行。

Result: 在CARLA-Air的Town10HD场景100个闭环回合上，AGC-VLN达到77.0%的联合成功率，较弱的单智能体（UAV，50.0%）获得27.0%的协作增益，且比最强已发布的单智能体基线（Travel UAV，53.0%）高出24个百分点。增益来源于UAV全局视角与UGV道路循迹执行的互补性。

Conclusion: AGC-VLN是首个面向空地协同VLN的无训练基线，证明了通过UAV全局视图渲染的共享鸟瞰图能够有效弥合两类异构智能体视角差异，实现稳定的协作导航行为，显著超越单智能体方法。

Abstract: Air-ground collaborative Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) pairs an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) with a global bird's-eye view and an unmanned ground vehicle (UGV) with a local first-person view, yet the setting remains largely unexplored: existing training-free methods solve single-agent tasks but offer no collaboration mechanism, and a recent CARLA-Air evaluation found no stable cooperative behavior across five state-of-the-art VLA models; naive semantic communication or bidirectional coupling even degrades performance. We establish AGC-VLN (Air-Ground Collaborative VLN), the first training-free baseline for air-ground collaborative VLN. The key insight is that training-free methods decompose navigation into VLM-based semantic reasoning and deterministic geometric execution, exposing a collaboration interface: the UAV's global view, over which it renders the UGV's reported pose and the VLM-anchored target as CAR/GOAL markers with distance labels, yielding a shared bird's-eye map. From this map, the UGV acquires global spatial context its first-person view cannot provide, plans a road-following path with a frozen VLM, and executes it under closed-loop control; in parallel, the UAV runs 3D-SPF, a spatial-search upgrade of SPF that localizes the target in the downward view and flies toward it. On 100 closed-loop episodes in CARLA-Air's Town10HD scene, AGC-VLN reaches a 77.0% joint success rate, a collaboration gain of +27.0% over the weaker individual agent (the UAV, 50.0%), and exceeds the strongest published single-agent baseline (Travel UAV, 53.0%) by 24.0 points, stemming from the complementarity of the UAV's global view and the UGV's road-following execution. Project page: https://github.com/ZSN2024/AGC-VLN.

</details>


### [31] [BRIDGE: An Open-Source Humanoid Platform via Morphology-Control Co-Design for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03497)
*Jianren Wang,Letian Qian,Zikai Wang,Weiwei Wu,Junjie Zong,Abhinav Gupta,Deepak Pathak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的形态-控制协同设计框架，用于优化人形机器人的形态以匹配人类运动，并引入了一个联合考虑运动重定向保真度和动态跟踪性能的新型评价指标。基于该框架实现了Bridge人形机器人平台（88cm高），在基础运动、鲁棒平衡和高速动态操作等任务上均优于Bumi、K1和Toddlerbot等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人形机器人开发采用硬件设计与全身控制相分离的范式，导致系统整体欠优，无法实现类似人类的流畅性和敏捷性。为突破这一瓶颈，需要将人类行为数据引入形态设计过程，实现形态与控制的联合优化。

Method: 提出数据驱动的形态-控制协同设计框架，在优化过程中将人形机器人形态与全身控制策略联合优化；同时引入一种新颖的形态保真度量化指标，该指标同时考虑对人类运动的运动学重定向保真度以及动态跟踪性能。

Result: 该框架在所有指标上均达到SOTA性能，超越Bumi、K1和Toddlerbot等基线人形机器人。所实现的Bridge平台（88cm高的开源人形机器人，配套控制策略）展现了优越的人类运动数据保真度，在基础运动、鲁棒平衡和高度动态操作中均表现卓越。

Conclusion: 形态-控制协同设计范式能够有效弥合硬件设计与全身控制之间的鸿沟，使基于人类行为数据驱动的人形机器人开发成为可能。Bridge平台证明了该方法结合开源发布可推动通用具身智能机器人的发展。

Abstract: Developing humanoid robots capable of leveraging human behavioral data is essential for general-purpose embodiment, yet conventional development remains bottlenecked by a decoupled paradigm that isolates hardware design from whole-body control. This approach leads to suboptimal systems that compromise human-like fluidity and agility. To bridge this gap, we introduce a data-driven morphology-control co-design framework that optimizes humanoid morphology for human-like movement. To quantify morphological fidelity, we also introduce a novel metric that jointly considers kinematic retargeting fidelity to human motion and dynamic tracking performance. Our framework achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance across all metrics compared to baseline humanoids (Bumi, K1, and Toddlerbot). Finally, we realize this design in Bridge, an open-source, 88cm-tall humanoid platform released alongside its control policy. We demonstrate that Bridge captures human motion data with superior fidelity, exhibiting exceptional performance across foundational locomotion, robust balance, and highly dynamic maneuvers. Videos and open-source materials: https://sites.google.com/view/bridgerobot.

</details>


### [32] [TRaIL-Odom: Tightly Coupled Continuous Time Radar-IMU-LiDAR Odometry with Adaptive Doppler Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03561)
*Chiyun Noh,Turcan Tuna,William Talbot,Marco Hutter,Laurent Kneip,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TRaIL-Odom，一种雷达-IMU-LiDAR紧耦合里程计框架，包含逐点雷达重加权和扫描级雷达增益调度两种退化解感知的自适应加权模块，根据LiDAR几何的退化方向动态调整雷达多普勒贡献权重。在13个序列上取得SOTA性能，在退化场景中优势明显，消融实验相对固定权重基线将RMSE ATE和RTE分别降低86.0%和78.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达-LiDAR融合方法使用固定残差权重，但雷达多普勒和LiDAR几何的信息量随扫描和方向变化，固定权重会导致均匀加权并将多普勒信息错误分配到平移方向，在几何退化场景中性能受限。

Method: 提出两个退化感知的多普勒重加权模块：(1) 逐点雷达重加权——从LiDAR几何中识别弱平移方向（退化子空间），根据各雷达多普勒约束与弱子空间的对齐程度重新加权；(2) 扫描级雷达增益调度——利用LiDAR几何各向异性动态调整整体雷达贡献，当LiDAR可观测性差时增强雷达，当LiDAR约束可靠时抑制雷达。

Result: 在13个评估序列中取得SOTA整体性能，在几何退化场景中优势明显；在3个退化序列的消融实验中，两种自适应加权模块的组合相对固定权重基线将RMSE ATE和RTE分别降低86.0%和78.5%。代码和数据集已开源。

Conclusion: 退化方向感知的自适应雷达加权策略能有效克服固定权重在几何退化场景下的局限性，显著提升雷达-LiDAR融合里程计的鲁棒性和精度。

Abstract: Existing radar-LiDAR fusion methods rely on fixed residual weights, even though the informativeness of radar Doppler and LiDAR geometry is scan- and direction-dependent, leading to uniform radar weighting that misallocates Doppler information across translational directions. To address this limitation, we propose two degeneracy-aware Doppler reweighting modules within a tightly coupled Radar-IMU-LiDAR odometry framework: per-point radar reweighting and scan-wise radar gain scheduling. Since geometric degeneracy is directional, we first identify weak translational directions from the LiDAR geometry and reweight individual radar Doppler constraints based on their alignment with the weak subspace. We further adjust the overall radar contribution using LiDAR geometric anisotropy such that radar is emphasized when LiDAR observability is poor and suppressed when LiDAR constraints are already reliable. Across 13 evaluated sequences, TRaIL-Odom achieves state-of-the-art overall performance, with clear advantages in geometrically degenerate scenes. In ablation experiments on three degenerate sequences, combining the two adaptive weighting modules reduces RMSE ATE and RTE by 86.0% and 78.5% relative to the fixed-weight baseline. We make our code and an accompanying dataset publicly available at https://github.com/ChiyunNoh/TRaIL-Odom.

</details>


### [33] [Toward Physically Grounded JEPA World Models for Goal-Conditioned Robotic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03565)
*Muyuan Liu,Yue Huang,Zheng Liang,Xiang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的JEPA世界模型，在潜在空间预测的基础上结合逆动力学（IDM）和状态对齐（SA），使潜在表示保留与机器人控制相关的物理状态信息，从而提升基于视觉目标的规划成功率。在四个基准任务中达到最优或与最优相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动作条件JEPA世界模型虽然避免了未来像素重建，但仅靠潜在预测本身并不能显式地鼓励学习到的表示保留与机器人控制相关的信息，导致潜在状态可能无法充分反映足以支撑规划决策的物理状态细节。

Method: 构建端到端的JEPA世界模型，在潜在预测（latent prediction）基础上引入两个辅助目标：逆动力学（inverse dynamics, IDM）用于推断产生潜在迁移的动作，抑制潜在坍缩并使潜在迁移携带动作信息；状态对齐（state alignment, SA）用于将连续的表示与对应的物理构型及运动状态相锚定，将潜在空间映射到机器人的物理状态空间。

Result: 在四个基准任务上，模型在TwoRoom（100%）、PushT（98%）和OGBench-Cube（87%）上取得最高成功率，在Reacher上与LeWorldModel性能相当。消融实验显示，在所有任务中，SA的加入均一致地优于仅使用IDM的方案。虽然LeWorldModel在OGBench-Cube上平均straightening更高，但迁移子空间分析显示其迁移能量集中在维度显著更低的子空间中；该状态对齐模型的有效迁移维度更高。

Conclusion: 状态对齐能够作为逆动力学的有效补充，提升潜在表示的信息保留能力与规划成功率，验证了将表示锚定到物理状态对于机器人规划的积极价值。

Abstract: Action-conditioned JEPA world models enable planning toward visually specified goals without reconstructing future pixels, yet latent prediction alone does not explicitly encourage the learned representations to retain information relevant to robotic control. We introduce an end-to-end JEPA world model that augments latent prediction with inverse dynamics (IDM) and state alignment (SA). While inverse dynamics discourages latent collapse and makes latent transitions informative of the actions that produced them, state alignment grounds consecutive representations in their associated physical configuration and motion. Across four benchmark tasks, our model attains the highest success rates on TwoRoom (100%), PushT (98%), and OGBench-Cube (87%), while performing comparably to LeWorldModel on Reacher. Our ablation further shows that adding state alignment consistently improves planning success over IDM alone across all four tasks. Although LeWorldModel, our primary baseline, attains higher average straightening on OGBench-Cube, transition-subspace analysis shows that its transition energy is concentrated in a substantially lower-dimensional subspace. Our state-aligned model exhibits a higher effective transition dimension than LeWorldModel and improves planning over IDM alone, supporting state alignment as an effective complement to inverse dynamics for robotic planning.

</details>


### [34] [Scaling Bimanual Household Manipulation from 1,500 hours of Demonstrations to On-Policy Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03591)
*Jiafeng Xu,Qi Li,Yan Shen,Yiyu Ren,Travis Davies,Shaowen He,Ze Wang,Yifan Yang,Ran Cheng,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发布了1,500小时的双臂操作演示数据集，并训练了XR-2视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过系统性实验验证了模型在任务成功率、训练效率和数据利用方面的优越表现，同时观察到随着专家演示数据和DAgger纠正数据规模的增加，任务成功率呈稳定提升的缩放趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习鲁棒的双臂操作通用策略受限于高质量大规模人类演示数据的稀缺，缺乏足够数据来训练和评估大规模VLA模型，需要构建全面的数据语料库来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 构建了高吞吐量的数据管道和精心设计的多阶段训练范式，利用1,500小时涵盖日常家务的双臂操作演示数据训练XR-2 VLA模型，并研究了两个关键缩放维度：专家演示数据量的变化，以及使用实时人类干预生成的DAgger纠正数据进行后训练。

Result: XR-2在系统性实验中表现出强大的操作性能，同时保持优异的训练效率和数据利用率。在两种数据扩展设置下（专家演示数据和DAgger纠正数据），任务成功率随数据量增加呈稳定且一致的缩放趋势。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了XR-2的学习能力以及所发布数据集的良好缩放特性，通过开源该数据集支持双臂机器人操作学习的可复现研究。

Abstract: Learning generalist policies for robust bimanual manipulation is bottlenecked by the scarcity of high quality large scale human demonstration data. In this work, we release 1,500 hours of diverse bimanual manipulation demonstrations covering everyday household tasks, and use this comprehensive corpus to train XR-2, a powerful vision-language-action (VLA) model. Enabled by a purpose built high throughput data pipeline and a carefully designed multi stage training paradigm, XR-2 attains strong manipulation performance in our systematic experiments while retaining favorable training efficiency and high data utilization. We further study two critical scaling axes: varying the amount of expert demonstration data, and post training on DAgger correction data from real time human interventions. In both settings, task success rate improves steadily over the data ranges we probe, exhibiting a clear consistent scaling trend at our current data scale. These results validate both the learning capacity of XR-2 and the promising scaling properties of the released dataset, which we open source to support reproducible research on bimanual robot manipulation learning.

</details>


### [35] [FailBench: How Reliable are VLMs at Judging Robot Task Success?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03611)
*Zaruhi Navasardyan,Tatul Danielyan,Hrant Davtyan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FailBench基准，用于评估VLM在机器人操作失败检测中的跨域泛化能力。基准包含来自14个公开来源的2,197次操作尝试，其中75%的失败自然发生。评估13种VLM检测器发现最佳模型仅达到0.77平衡准确率，且为失败检测微调的模型普遍不如通用VLM。模型在依赖可观察物体运动的任务上接近饱和，但在接触密集型装配任务上退化至接近随机（<0.60）。错误分析揭示模型在证据模糊时系统性偏向预测成功。输入级干预（空间定位和裁剪相关区域）可在无需额外训练的情况下使最佳检测器提升2.4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然VLMs越来越多地被用于评估机器人操作结果，但现有基准缺乏跨域泛化的证据，无法有效衡量VLM检测器在多样化真实场景中的失败检测能力。

Method: 构建包含2,197次操作尝试的FailBench基准，覆盖14个公开来源（12个真实世界，2个模拟），其中75%的失败自然发生，6个真实世界来源来自非失败检测数据集。评估13种VLM检测器，包括微调模型和通用模型，并进行错误分析以及输入级空间裁剪干预实验。

Result: 最佳检测器仅达到0.77平均平衡准确率；为失败检测微调的模型反而一致性地差于通用VLM及其自身预训练基线；模型在物体运动可观测的任务上接近饱和，但在接触密集型装配任务上退化为近随机水平（<0.60）；模型在模糊证据下存在系统性偏向预测成功的偏差；输入级区域裁剪干预使最佳检测器提升2.4个百分点。

Conclusion: VLM用于机器人失败检测的跨域泛化能力仍有限，尤其难以处理接触密集型任务。任务特异性微调并不能带来优势，输入级空间裁剪提供了一种有效的无训练改进途径。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly used to evaluate robot manipulation outcomes, but existing benchmarks offer limited evidence of cross-domain generalization. We introduce FailBench, a benchmark for robot failure detection comprising 2,197 manipulation attempts across 14 public sources (12 real-world, 2 simulated). In FailBench, 75% of failures occur naturally, and six real-world sources come from non-failure-detection datasets. Evaluating 13 VLM-based detectors, we find the best model achieves only 0.77 mean balanced accuracy. Notably, models fine-tuned for failure detection consistently underperform general-purpose VLMs and their own pretrained baselines. Performance depends heavily on required visual evidence: models approach saturation when outcomes depend on observable object motion, but degrade to near-chance (<0.60 balanced accuracy) on contact-intensive assembly tasks. Error analysis reveals a systematic bias toward predicting success under ambiguous evidence, which persists even with increased reasoning effort. Finally, we show that input-level intervention--spatially localizing and cropping outcome-relevant regions--improves the top detector by 2.4 percentage points without extra training.

</details>

Result: 成功设计并搭建了QLAUN——一个15 kg、12自由度、近乎完全3D打印的扭矩可控四足机器人平台，具备易于更换的模块化腿、高扭矩QDD执行器、宽运动范围的关节以及柔性足部。

Conclusion: QLAUN作为一个开源理念的、经济实惠且坚固的研究平台，面向学术界和机器人研究社区，尤其为资源有限的地区提供了腿式机器人运动研究的可行选择。

Abstract: QLAUN Bot (Quad-Legged Adaptive Unmanned Navigator Robot) is a torque-controlled quadruped robot that is research-oriented, cost-effective, and aimed at achieving simultaneous robustness and agility while being completely 3D-printed. It is a quadruped robot that is aimed at empowering robotics research at universities and research institutes in Lebanon and the MENA region. Using a novel electronics-free leg design strategy, we present a modular robot with interchangeable and easily replaceable legs. The 15 kg robot possesses 12 DoF (Degrees-of-Freedom) with three per leg, each paired with a completely 3D-printed Quasi-Direct Drive (QDD) actuator that consists of a brushless DC motor and a low-ratio gearbox transmission that is connected to a belt transmission system for significantly increasing the torque outputs at the joints. We present legs that have decoupled hip and knee actuators to improve the overall modularity of the robot. QLAUN is almost completely 3D-printed using polylactic acid (PLA) and assembled using off-the-shelf parts to create a robust, agile, and affordable robot for legged robot locomotion research. The legs possess joints with wide ranges of motion, including a continuous hip flexion-extension joint. A compliant foot, printed using TPU-95A is also implemented for alleviating hard impacts and handling terrain uncertainties. This extended abstract aims to introduce QLAUN, a novel platform for robotics research, emphasizing the design concepts and principles that underpin its development to the academic and research communities in the field of robotics.

</details>
