*Mikolaj Helinski,Spilios Theodoulis,Mahmoud Hamandi,Abdullah Mohamed Ali,Anthony Tzes,Marija Popovic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将动态窗口法（DWA）扩展到六自由度（6D-DWA）的实时局部规划与避障模块，用于全向多旋翼（omnicopter）在杂乱环境中的自主导航。该方法通过局部地图体素化、紧凑球体近似飞行器几何以及自适应速度采样实现实时可行性，并引入"Agile Mode"情境感知机制在线调整评分权重以增强对未知障碍物的反应能力。仿真实验表明该方法可在0.2s控制循环内运行，在密集航点路径跟踪中平均横向误差<0.1m、平均航向误差13°，未知障碍物场景中Agile Mode达到79.3%（偏心障碍物）和41.4%（居中障碍物）的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主无人机在杂乱环境中作业时，全局规划器（如RRT*）无法以控制频率直接部署，需要一种实时可部署的局部规划和避障方案。现有动态窗口方法局限于低自由度场景，难以直接应用于具有六自由度运动能力的全向多旋翼。

Method: 将DWA扩展到6D搜索空间，结合三项关键技术：(i)局部地图体素化以加速碰撞检测推理；(ii)用紧凑球体近似飞行器几何结构；(iii)在6D搜索空间中进行自适应速度采样。同时引入上下文感知的"Agile Mode"机制，在线动态调整评分权重，在机动规避中权衡目标进展、安全间距与朝向约束。

Result: 在计算压力测试、密集航点路径跟踪以及静态/未知障碍物场景的仿真中，规划器可在0.2s控制循环内稳定运行；密集航点全局路径跟踪平均横向误差<0.1m、平均航向误差13°；静态环境下可成功避障。Agile Mode在未知障碍物规避中，偏心障碍物场景成功率为79.3%，居中障碍物场景为41.4%，体现了自适应加权策略的有效性以及在高度受限几何中的局限性。

Conclusion: 6D-DWA方法为全向多旋翼提供了实时可行的局部规划与避障能力，基于DWA的自适应扩展方案在密集航点跟踪和未知障碍物规避方面表现出良好的性能。然而，实验结果表明，在高度受限的几何约束（如居中障碍物场景）下，该方法仍存在明显局限，需要进一步改进以提升复杂未知环境中的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) increasingly operate in cluttered environments where global planners such as RRT* are not directly deployable at control rates. This paper presents a real-time local planning and obstacle avoidance module for an omnidirectional multirotor (omnicopter) by extending the Dynamic Window Approach to six degrees of freedom (6D-DWA). Our method achieves real-time feasibility through (i) local-map voxelisation, (ii) a compact sphere-based approximation of the vehicle geometry, and (iii) adaptive velocity sampling in the 6D search space. To improve reactivity to unknown obstacles, we introduce a context-aware "Agile Mode" that adjusts scoring weights online to trade-off between goal progress, clearance, and heading/facing constraints during evasive manoeuvres. We evaluate our approach in simulation across computational stress tests, dense-waypoint path tracking, and static/unknown obstacle scenarios. Our planner runs consistently within a 0.2s control loop, tracks waypoint-dense global paths with < 0.1m average cross-track error and 13deg average heading error, and avoids collisions in static environments. For unknown obstacle avoidance, Agile Mode achieves 79.3% success for an off-centre obstacle and 41.4% for a centred obstacle, highlighting both the effectiveness of adaptive weighting and remaining limitations in highly constrained geometries.

</details>


### [38] [WISE: World-model-guided Imagination Scheduling for Efficient Post-training of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03681)
*Chenhao Zhang,Hanyu Zhao,Hang Cheng,Tengfei Pan,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WISE是一个针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的统一框架，通过智能调度世界模型想象来高效进行策略后训练，选择性地在交互关键状态调用有限的想象回滚，并用进度与完成信号评估候选未来以优化动作，在π0和π0.5上实现一致改进，同时GPU计算时间降低约80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略的后训练通常依赖昂贵的专家示范进行监督微调，或依赖真实世界探索进行强化学习，两者成本高且可能不稳定。虽然世界模型可通过想象未来评估候选行为来作为替代方案，但有效的后训练不仅需要预测准确，还需要想象被调度到有用之处、限制在可靠的时间范围内并转化为可信的策略监督信号。在机器人操作中，不同执行阶段想象力价值差异显著，而长距离回滚会累积预测误差并引入不可靠的学习信号。

Method: WISE提出了一个统一框架来协调世界模型想象在策略精化中的使用时机与方式：1）在交互相关状态选择性调用想象；2）执行有界的多视角回滚；3）使用进度和完成信号评估候选未来；4）利用相对的评估结果精化从真实交互上下文生成的动作。该方法被应用于π0和π0.5两种VLA基础模型上。

Result: 在多种操作任务上，WISE在π0和π0.5上均展示出一致的性能提升；相比全量想象方法，GPU计算时间降低约80%；真实世界评估显示在多种分布偏移下具有显著的鲁棒性和泛化性提升。

Conclusion: WISE表明，通过智能调度和约束世界模型想象的使用，可以在大幅降低计算成本的同时实现VLA策略的高效后训练，并提升模型在真实世界中的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Post-training VLA policies typically rely on supervised fine-tuning with costly expert demonstrations or reinforcement learning with expensive and potentially unstable real-world exploration. World models offer a promising alternative by evaluating candidate behaviors through imagined futures, yet effective post-training requires more than accurate prediction: imagination must be scheduled where it is useful, bounded within reliable horizons, and translated into trustworthy policy supervision. In robotic manipulation, the value of imagination varies substantially across execution stages, while extended rollouts can accumulate prediction errors and introduce unreliable learning signals. We introduce WISE (World-model-guided Imagination Scheduling for Efficient Post-training of Vision-Language-Action Models), a unified framework that coordinates when and how world-model imagination is used during policy refinement. WISE selectively invokes imagination at interaction-relevant states, performs bounded multi-view rollouts, evaluates candidate futures using progress and completion signals, and uses their relative outcomes to refine actions generated from real interaction contexts. Extensive experiments with both $π_0$ and $π_{0.5}$ demonstrate consistent improvements across diverse manipulation tasks while reducing GPU computation time by approximately 80% compared with full imagination. Real-world evaluations further show substantial gains in robustness and generalization under diverse real-world distribution shifts.

</details>


### [39] [Predictive Zonotope Reduction: Precise Runtime Monitoring under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03699)
*Vladimir Krsmanovic,Florian Kohn,Bernd Finkbeiner,Milan Simovic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出预测性Zonotope缩减（PZR）方法，将运行时监控中zonotope缩减器的选择建模为最优控制问题，通过波束搜索模型预测控制和策略蒸馏实现动态缩减器选择，在RLola框架和MuJoCo模拟的5自由度机械臂上验证，相比静态缩减策略显著降低了误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运行时监控器需要以zonotope表示传感器测量不确定性，但持续纳入新测量会使zonotope阶数无界增长，需周期性进行过近似缩减。现有方法在整个运行过程中使用固定缩减方法，但最优选择取决于当前状态，固定策略导致精度不足。

Method: 将缩减器选择形式化为最优控制问题，使用波束搜索模型预测控制求解；进一步通过策略蒸馏将MPC策略压缩为小型神经网络策略，以提高执行速度并保持性能，使其适用于资源受限的实时系统。实现于RLola运行时监控框架中，在MuJoCo模拟的5自由度机械臂上按ISO 5725建模传感器不确定性进行评估。

Result: 在Raspberry Pi 5上的实验表明，动态缩减策略相比静态缩减策略显著降低了监控中的误报率；策略蒸馏后的神经网络策略在保持改进性能的同时，执行速度显著高于模型预测控制。

Conclusion: 动态选择zonotope缩减方法能够有效提升不确定性感知运行时监控的精度（降低误报率），且通过策略蒸馏可以同时满足实时资源约束，为物理机器人系统的安全监控提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Robots operating in physical environments make control decisions based on uncertain sensor measurements, which can lead to unsafe or suboptimal actions. Runtime monitors that check their behavior against safety specifications must represent this uncertainty soundly. Zonotopes are a widely used representation, but continuously incorporating new measurements grows their order unboundedly, so monitors must periodically apply an over-approximating reduction. The choice of the reduction method substantially affects the zonotope's precision, yet existing approaches typically utilize a fixed method throughout the run, even though the optimal choice depends on the current state. This paper presents a Predictive Zonotope Reduction (PZR) approach, which frames reducer selection as an optimal control problem and solves it using beam-search model predictive control. Policy distillation into a small neural policy further provides substantially higher execution speed than model predictive control while maintaining improved performance, enabling uncertainty-aware runtime monitoring on resource-constrained real-time systems. We implement our approach in the RLola runtime monitoring framework and evaluate it on a 5-degree-of-freedom robotic arm simulated in MuJoCo, with sensor uncertainty modeled according to ISO 5725. Experiments on a Raspberry Pi 5 show that dynamic reduction significantly lowers false-positive rates in monitoring compared with static reduction strategies.

</details>


### [40] [Toward an~Integrated Cognitive--Ergonomic Architecture for~Human--Machine Interaction: Combining Cognitive Models with~Human Factors Ergonomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03704)
*Antoine Lenat,Olivier Cheminat,Damien Chablat,Camilo Charron*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过比较分析SOAR、ACT-R、LIDA和COCOM等认知模型，结合人因工效学原理，提出了一种用于人机交互建模的综合认知架构，并在工业机器人应用中进行了实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知架构在人机交互建模中缺乏与工效学框架的系统整合，导致认知理论与应用系统设计之间存在鸿沟，难以满足动态环境中复杂人机交互的需求。

Method: 对SOAR、ACT-R、LIDA和COCOM四种认知模型进行系统比较分析，综合其优势并定制化构建统一架构；将该架构置于人因工效学（HFE）框架中，建立决策、技能习得与适应性行为的认知-工效学关联模型；以工业机器人应用场景为实证环境进行验证。

Result: 该框架在工业机器人应用中得到实证支撑，增强了人机交互系统的认知一致性，并提供了一种在高风险环境中设计智能化、以人为本界面的可扩展方法论。

Conclusion: 该工作同时在理论层面推进了对人类能力的理解，在实践层面实现了自适应、工效学优化系统的落地，弥合了认知理论与应用系统设计之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: This paper presents an integrated approach to modeling human competencies by combining the theoretical foundations of cognitive architectures with principles from Human Factors Ergonomics (HFE). Through a comparative analysis of established cognitive models-SOAR, ACT-R, LIDA, and COCOM-we synthesize a tailored architecture designed to address the complexities of human-machine interaction (HMI) in dynamic environments. By contextualizing this model within ergonomic frameworks, we elucidate the mechanisms underlying decision-making, skill acquisition, and adaptive behavior, bridging the gap between cognitive theory and applied system design. Our framework is empirically grounded in industrial robotics applications, where operator expertise, normative knowledge, and real-time feedback loops are critical. The proposed architecture not only enhances the cognitive alignment of HMI systems but also provides a scalable methodology for designing intelligent, human-centered interfaces in high-stakes environments. This work advances both the theoretical understanding of human competencies and the practical implementation of adaptive, ergonomically optimized systems.

</details>


### [41] [MINERVA: How Small Can a Manipulation Policy Be and Still Solve LIBERO?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03715)
*Kohei Sendai,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yusuke Iwasawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MINERVA系列极紧凑视觉-动作策略模型来测量LIBERO操控基准的任务特定能力下限。仅0.54M参数的策略在2000次rollout中达到95.1%平均成功率，仅比报告中使用7700倍参数的LeRobot π0.5低2.4个百分点。性能在约1M参数处饱和，低于0.25M参数则崩溃。跨架构、训练和推理的广泛扫描中，仅动作块长度和视觉容量持续超过±1点的训练种子带。流匹配相比直接L1回归无优势，而回归在GPU上快达3.8倍。任务ID置换探针表明LIBERO指令条件化主要是在记忆化任务间选择。该配方在LIBERO-90上达到94.6%，而LIBERO-Plus扰动将性能降至46-56%，对光度变化几乎零鲁棒性。研究首次建立了LIBERO任务特定容量下限的经验估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型（数十亿参数）现在主导了LIBERO操控基准，但该基准实际需要的模型容量仍然不清楚。研究者希望确定任务特定的容量下限，以理解基准的真正需求并指导参数效率高的策略设计。

Method: 设计了MINERVA家族——一系列刻意紧凑的视觉-动作策略模型。通过系统扫描架构、训练和推理维度来找到容量下限；使用任务ID置换探针分析LIBERO的指令条件化机制；对比流匹配与L1回归；并评估策略对LIBERO-90和LIBERO-Plus的泛化与鲁棒性，同时在笔记本CPU上进行推理延迟测量。

Result: 0.54M参数策略在LIBERO四套标准套件上达到95.1%平均成功率，性能在约1M参数处饱和并在0.25M以下崩溃。动作块长度和视觉容量是唯二超过±1点训练种子带的关键因素。流匹配无优势于L1回归，回归快3.8倍。LIBERO指令条件化主要是在记忆化任务间选择。4LIBERO达成94.6%，但LIBERO-Plus降至46-56%，对光度变化几乎零鲁棒性。推理在笔记本CPU上仅为5-9ms/chunk，比SmolVLA快113倍，比π0.5快1400倍。

Conclusion: LIBERO基准的任务特定容量下限远低于当前VLA模型的使用规模，约在1M参数量级附近。这提示需要容量感知的设计和蒸馏来部署高效的机器人策略，而不是盲目增加参数规模。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models with billions of parameters now dominate the LIBERO manipulation benchmark, but the model capacity actually required by the benchmark remains unclear. We introduce MINERVA (MINimal Efficient Robotic Vision-Action policy), a family of deliberately compact visuomotor policies designed to measure this task-specific capacity floor. A 0.54M-parameter policy achieves 95.1% average success over 2,000 rollouts on the four standard LIBERO suites, only 2.4 points below the reported LeRobot $π_{0.5}$ result despite using 7,700$\times$ fewer parameters. Performance saturates near 1M parameters and collapses below 0.25M. Across broad architectural, training, and inference sweeps, only action-chunk length and vision capacity consistently exceed a $\pm$1-point training-seed band. Flow matching provides no detectable advantage over direct L1 regression across three seeds, while regression is up to 3.8$\times$ faster on GPU. A task-ID permutation probe shows that standard LIBERO instruction conditioning primarily selects among memorized tasks: changing only the task-ID mapping reduces success to near chance. The same recipe achieves 94.6% success across 89 LIBERO-90 tasks, while LIBERO-Plus perturbations reduce performance to 46--56%, with near-zero robustness to photometric shifts. The 0.54M policy replans every control step in 5--9 ms per chunk on a laptop CPU, 113$\times$ faster than SmolVLA and 1,400$\times$ faster than $π_{0.5}$, without a GPU. These results establish a first empirical estimate of LIBERO's task-specific capacity floor and motivate capacity-aware design and distillation for deployment-efficient robot policies.

</details>


### [42] [RoughSense: Lightweight Terrain-Induced Rover Vibration Prediction Using Point Clouds and IMU Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03720)
*Gabriel Manuel Garcia,Stephanie Aravecchia,Miguel Angel Olivares-Mendez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的实时振动感知可通行性地图构建方法，利用LiDAR点云和IMU测量数据，通过RANSAC从地形几何估算初始振动代理，并用递归最小二乘法在线校正，实现地下等受限环境中的自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太空自主导航需要对地形进行可靠评估以确保安全操作，尤其是地下环境通信、计算资源和功耗均受限，传统方法难以满足实时性要求。

Method: 方法分为两步：(1) 使用RANSAC对SLAM生成的局部点云块进行拟合，从地形几何中估算初始振动代理；(2) 并行使用IMU测量漫游车实际振动，通过递归最小二乘法在线校正点云预测结果，使系统自适应地将几何估计调整到实际车辆响应。

Result: 该方法在月球模拟环境、户外场地和地下矿井三种场景中进行了评估验证。

Conclusion: 该方法为资源受限环境下的实时振动感知可通行性地图构建提供了一种轻量级且可自适应的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in space requires reliable terrain assessment for safe operations, especially in underground environments with limited communication, computing resources, and power budget. This paper presents a lightweight method for real-time vibration-aware traversability mapping using a Light Detecting And Ranging (LiDAR) point cloud and Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) measurements. An initial vibration proxy is estimated from terrain geometry by applying Random sample consensus (RANSAC) to local point-cloud patches produced by a Simultaneous Localisation And Mapping (SLAM) algorithm. In parallel, the IMU provides local observations of the vibration experienced by the rover during traversal. The point-cloud-based prediction is then corrected online using Recursive Least Squares, allowing the system to adapt the geometric estimate to the measured rover response. The approach is evaluated in a lunar analogue environment, an outdoor field, and an underground mine.

</details>

*Reza Kashef,Cem Suulker,Mohammad Sheikh Sofla,Kaspar Althoefer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多藤蔓（multi-vine）架构，通过将两条软体反转机器人（vine robot）耦合到外部集成的工作通道（working channel）上，实现主动尖端转向和工具输送，实验展示了生长过程中近90度的锐利转向能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体反转机器人（vine robot）因其尖部生长和低摩擦导航特性，适用于复杂环境（如人体结肠）的导航与检查任务。然而，现有主动转向方案（分布式人工肌肉、专用尖部转向机构）和内置工作通道的方案（限制工具尺寸、引入摩擦、仅限尖部访问环境）难以兼顾主动转向与工具输送的需求，尤其是在急剧方向变化（如乙状结肠）场景下。

Method: 提出多藤蔓架构：两条vine机器人通过软性安装尖端（soft mounting tips）与外部集成的工作通道耦合。通过独立控制两条藤蔓的充气驱动实现主动尖端转向，同时推进外部工作通道前进，而无需将工作通道嵌入藤蔓体内，也不限制工具尺寸和接触范围。

Result: 实验证明该架构在生长的同时能够实现接近90度的锐利转向，验证了多藤蔓耦合架构在主动转向与工具输送方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该多藤蔓架构为软体机器人提供了在复杂环境中兼具主动导航转向与工具输送能力的新方案，在医学（如内镜外科）及非医学应用中具有广阔前景。该工作隶属于ERC Synergy项目EndoTheranostics。

Abstract: Soft eversion robots, also known as vine robots, have attracted growing interest for navigation and inspection tasks, including minimally invasive medical applications [1]. A vine robot consists of a thin, flexible, inextensible tube folded inward that everts and grows forward when pressurized. This tip-growth enables navigation with minimal friction, making vine robots well suited for complex environments such as the human colon [2]. While their inherent softness allows passive conforma- tion to curved pathways in confined spaces, navigation performance strongly depends on environmental inter- actions, including contact angle and the length of un- constrained deployed material [3], [4]. Sharp directional changes, such as those in the sigmoid colon, often limit passive growth and necessitate active steering. Existing solutions include distributed artificial muscles [5] or dedicated tip-based steering mechanisms [6]. In addition, many applications require payload delivery, such as sensors and tools [7], [8]. Within the ERC Synergy project EndoTheranostics, this motivates the development of vine robots capable of delivering micro- surgical tools during growth. Prior work has integrated working channels within the vine body [8], [9], but these approaches constrain tool size, introduce friction, and limit access to the environment to the robot tip. In this work, we propose a multi-vine architecture in which two vine robots are coupled to an externally integrated working channel via soft mounting tips [10]. Independent vine actuation enables active tip steering while advancing the working channel without embed- ding it within the vine bodies Figure 1. Experiments demonstrate sharp steering of nearly 90 degrees during growth, highlighting the potential of this architecture for versatile medical and non-medical applications.

</details>


### [44] [Virtual Testing of Automated Driving Systems through Credible Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03760)
*Riccardo Dona,Espedito Rusciano,Biagio Ciuffo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于风险的可信度评估框架，用于评估自动驾驶系统（ADS）安全评估中仿真工具链的可信度，借鉴NASA STD-7009标准，将传统V&V扩展为与预期用途和决策安全关键性挂钩的生命周期评估体系，并按比例设定可信度验收阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统（ADS）安全评估中虚拟测试（VT）日益关键，但现有ADS领域的VT认证方法通常仅依赖验证活动（validation-only），在复杂的多工具仿真环境中扩展性差，无法充分支撑安全相关决策的可信度判断。

Method: 提出一个基于风险的仿真工具链可信度评估框架：借鉴NASA STD-7009标准，将传统验证与确认（V&V）扩展为明确关联仿真输出的预期用途及其所支撑决策的安全关键性；提供覆盖工具链管理、建模假设与局限、验证、确认和敏感性分析的生命周期导向评估方案；按比例定义可信度验收阈值，根据仿真用途（探索性安全分析、部分决策支持或替代物理测试）实施差异化要求。

Result: 该框架在ADS领域进行了演示验证，结果表明其能够提供可扩展的仿真工具链可信度评估方案，并支持针对不同用途和风险等级的差异化可信度要求。

Conclusion: 虽然该框架在ADS领域得到演示，但其方法可直接应用于虚拟测试在安全评估和监管决策中发挥核心作用的道路安全及其他仿真研究中。

Abstract: Simulation is increasingly used to support safety-related decision-making in road transport, particularly for the assessment and approval of automated driving systems (ADS). The complexity of ADS behavior and size of their operational design domains make exclusive reliance on physical testing impractical, leading to extensive use of virtual testing (VT) during the approval phase. This shift raises critical questions regarding the credibility of modelling and simulation (M&S) results used to support road safety decisions. Current VT accreditation approaches in the ADS domain typically rely on validation-only practices, which have been shown to scale poorly when applied to complex, multi-tool simulation environments. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a risk-based framework for assessing the credibility of simulation toolchains used in ADS safety evaluation, drawing inspiration from established practices in other safety-critical domains, notably NASA's STD-7009 for models and simulations. The framework extends traditional verification and validation (V&V) by explicitly linking credibility requirements to the intended use of simulation outputs and to the safety criticality of the decisions they support within the approval process. It provides a lifecycle-oriented assessment scheme integrating toolchain management, modelling assumptions and limitations, verification, validation, and sensitivity analysis. Credibility acceptance thresholds are defined proportionally, allowing differentiated requirements depending on whether simulation is used for exploratory safety analysis, partial decision support, or as a substitute for physical testing. While demonstrated for ADS, the proposed approach is directly applicable to road safety and simulation studies where VT plays a central role in safety assessment and regulatory decision-making.

</details>


### [45] [Robot Aware Computational Design of Object Specific Passive Grippers for Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03761)
*Abdullah Yahya Abdullah Omaisan,Ibrahim Sheikh Mohamed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端计算流水线，将选定对象网格、测量姿态和六轴机器人转换为对象专用、无驱动的增材制造夹爪。与传统将抓取选择、工具几何、运动和结构设计分开处理的工作流不同，导出的每个设计通过可追溯标识绑定到源网格、姿态、机器人法兰、接触集和插入假设。系统集成精确网格配准、确定性接触采样、不确定性感知力螺旋筛选、六种被动捕获机制、保形表面合成、全姿态逆运动学、扫掠体积感知制造域、定向FDM有限元筛选与约束SIMP拓扑优化。四个存档测试对象（兔子、相机法兰、3DBenchy、faceted bust）通过标称适配、不确定力螺旋、运行时间扫描及基线/后拓扑FEA门槛。扩大后的±3mm、±5度姿态压力测试对各设计区分明显，160次模拟中保留29-134次，重建拓扑保留92.0-97.6%的FE域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工件抓取设计工作流将抓取选择、工具几何设计、机器人运动规划和结构设计视为独立问题，缺乏端到端的联合建模能力，导致设计的抓取器无法确保与对象网格、对象姿态、机器人法兰及接触假设的一致性，也缺乏从几何到结构验证的可追溯性。

Method: 提出耦合式端到端流水线，包含：exact-mesh RGB-D/ICP位姿配准、确定性表面接触采样、不确定性感知力螺旋筛选、六种被动捕获机制选择、对象保形表面合成、全姿态机器人逆运动学求解、扫掠体积感知的制造域构建、定向FDM有限元筛选及约束三维SIMP拓扑优化。并通过可追溯设计标识将各环节绑定，推导了配准、接触、适配公差、有限元与密度优化的解析方程，证明了节点载荷保持性、SIMP插值下的单调柔度敏感性及拓扑后处理的体素域包含性三个数值性质。

Result: 四个对象专用设计（兔子、相机法兰、3DBenchy、faceted bust）均通过标称适配、不确定力螺旋、运行时间扫描与基线/后拓扑FEA门槛。扩大的±3 mm、±5度姿态压力测试中160次模拟保留29-134次，区分了各设计鲁棒性；重建拓扑保留92.0-97.6%的FE域（功能区域受保护）。存档机器人照片展示了打印装配的实际效果。

Conclusion: 该方法将抓取设计从碎片化流程推向统一且可追溯的端到端管线，在设计数字筛选层面达到了可行性与鲁棒性的双重验证。但当前仅基于标称材料属性且缺乏试样标定的仪器测试，所有设计仍处于数字筛选状态而非可操作性释放。

Abstract: This paper presents an end-to-end computational pipeline that converts a selected object mesh, a measured object state, and a selected six-axis robot into an object-specific, unactuated, additively manufacturable gripper. The method couples exact-mesh RGB-D/ICP pose registration, deterministic surface-contact sampling, uncertainty-aware wrench screening, selection among six passive capture mechanisms, object-conformal surface synthesis, full-orientation robot inverse kinematics, a swept-volume-aware manufacturing domain, directional fused-deposition finite-element screening, and constrained three-dimensional SIMP topology optimization. Unlike workflows that treat grasp selection, tool geometry, motion, and structural design as separate problems, every exported design is bound to the source mesh, object pose, robot flange, contact set, and insertion hypothesis by a traceable design identifier. We derive the implemented registration, contact, fit-tolerance, finite-element, and density-optimization equations and prove three properties of the numerical construction: nodal load preservation, monotonic compliance sensitivity under SIMP interpolation, and voxel-domain containment after topology post-processing. Four archived object-specific attempts - a rabbit, camera flange, 3DBenchy, and faceted bust - meet the nominal fit, uncertain-wrench, runtime-sweep, and baseline/post-topology FEA gates. A deliberately enlarged +/-3 mm, +/-5 degree pose stress check differentiates the designs, retaining 29-134 of 160 simulated trials. Their reconstructed topologies retain 92.0-97.6% of the FE domain because functional regions are protected. Archived robot photographs show the corresponding printed assemblies qualitatively, while nominal material properties and absent coupon-calibrated, instrumented tests keep all four at digital-screening status rather than operational release.

</details>


### [46] [A comparative study on the accuracy & repeatability of mobile robotic platforms for the delivery of precision NDE measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03794)
*SeyedMohammadAmin Nabi Pour,S. Gareth Pierce,Randika Vithanage,Ehsan Mohseni,David Carswell,Matthew Shields*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于激光跟踪仪的通用评估协议（地面真值约6微米），用于量化五种商用移动机器人平台（KUKA KMP-1500、KUKA KMR、MiR250、Boston Dynamics Spot、Clearpath Husky）的静态定位精度与分段轨迹跟踪精度。结果显示静态定位精度中位数从8.2 mm（KMP-1500）到63.5 mm（Spot）不等，动态横向偏差从6.9 mm到112.1 mm不等。所有平台单独均无法满足航空航天NDE所需的0.2–1.0 mm公差，因此结果为平台选型与补充传感需求设计提供了可复现的依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人平台在大型航空航天结构的无损检测中是有益的灵活替代方案，但其基座定位精度以及该精度应如何指导部署决策，尚未在统一的外部参考协议下被系统评估。现有研究缺乏横向可比性，无法为平台选型和补充传感需求设计提供量化依据。

Method: 采用激光跟踪仪（地面真值约6 μm）建立评估工作流，测量五款商业移动平台在统一协议下的静态与分段轨迹定位精度。通过耦合多角点标定恢复激光器到机器人之间的变换关系及反射器偏移；使用全位姿普通最小二乘估计，稳健估计仅作为粗差检测手段。静态精度以定位误差衡量，动态精度通过路径横向误差（cross-track error）表征，并检验其对时间对齐的敏感性。

Result: 静态定位精度中位数范围从8.2 mm（KMP-1500）到63.5 mm（Spot），仅依赖轮式里程计的Husky无法标定。动态路径跟踪的横向分量对时间对齐不敏感，范围从6.9 mm（KMP-1500）到112.1 mm（Spot）。精度与可标定性均与平台的定位能力（从最新LiDAR SLAM到无地图视觉里程计）密切相关。

Conclusion: 没有任何单一平台能够仅靠基座定位满足航空航天NDE所需的0.2–1.0 mm公差。结果作为设计输入，量化了各平台所需的补充传感规模：最优平台约需提高一个数量级精度，最差平台接近两个数量级。该工作为平台选型提供了可复现的量化基础，而非可行性断言。

Abstract: Mobile robotic platforms offer a flexible alternative to fixed manipulators for non-destructive evaluation (NDE) of large aerospace structures, but their base-positioning accuracy and how that accuracy should inform deployment have not been assessed under a common, externally referenced protocol. This work presents a laser tracker-based evaluation workflow (ground truth approximately 6 micrometers) that measures the static and segmented trajectory positioning accuracy of five commercial mobile platforms (KUKA KMP-1500, KUKA KMR, MiR250, Boston Dynamics Spot, Clearpath Husky) under a common protocol. A coupled multi-corner calibration recovers the laser-to-robot transformation and reflector offsets; ordinary least squares over all poses is used, with robust estimation retained only as a blunder check.
  Static positioning accuracy ranged from a median of 8.2 mm (KMP-1500) to 63.5 mm (Spot), with the wheel-odometry-only Husky uncalibratable. Dynamic path following was characterised by cross-track error; the component was insensitive to temporal alignment, which ranged from 6.9 mm (KMP-1500) to 112.1 mm (Spot). Both accuracy and calibratability tracked localisation capability, from the newest LiDAR SLAM platform to map-free visual odometry. No configuration meets the 0.2 to 1.0 mm aerospace NDE tolerance from the base alone; the results are framed as a design input that sizes the supplementary sensing each platform requires: roughly one order of magnitude for the best platform and nearly two for the worst, providing a reproducible basis for platform selection rather than a feasibility claim.

</details>


### [47] [FWBC-VLA: Force-Aware Whole-Body Compensation for Contact-Rich Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03889)
*Yutian Zhang,Siyuan Ma,Liwen Yang,Yang Li,Ce Hao,Haozhen Chi,Dong We,Qiaojun Yu,Dibo Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FWBC-VLA框架，通过无传感器残余力矩估计器（HSR-Force）将接触力信息编码为token注入VLA动作专家，并联合本体感受状态与雅可比力估计生成补偿动作，融合到全身控制策略中，解决轮腿机器人在接触密集型移动操作中任务级动作生成与物理交互控制之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型能生成任务级动作但无法解读动作引发的物理交互；WBC策略虽能稳定机器人却无法区分任务相关接触力与外部干扰力；力/力矩传感器虽能直接测量交互但存在额外硬件成本和集成难题。

Method: 1) 提出HSR-Force无传感器残余力矩估计器推断接触强度及时序变化；2) 将接触估计编码为token注入VLA动作专家解码过程，使策略感知接触起始、持续加载与释放；3) 在包含5000+episodes的WL&Arm数据集上微调预训练VLA主干；4) 将本体感受状态、雅可比导出的机体坐标系力估计及接触状态联合输入补偿生成器产生纠偏动作；5) 将操作中心动作与纠偏动作融合后交由WBC策略执行。

Result: 在白板擦拭和带闭门器的开门等真实世界实验中，FWBC-VLA在接触密集的移动操作任务中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: FWBC-VLA通过力感知桥接VLA高层动作生成与WBC低层全身补偿控制，为轮腿机器人在接触丰富的移动操作场景中提供了一种无需硬件力传感器的可行方案。

Abstract: Contact-rich loco-manipulation requires a bridge between semantic action generation and physical interaction control. Existing Vision-language-action (VLA) models generate task-level actions from visual and linguistic observations, but cannot interpret the physical interactions induced by those actions. While the whole-body control (WBC) policy can stabilize the robot, it cannot distinguish task-relevant interaction forces from forces induced by external disturbances during manipulation. Although force/torque sensors provide direct measurements of physical interactions, retrofitting them entails additional hardware costs and substantial integration effort, particularly for platforms not designed with sensor integration in mind. To address this problem, we propose FWBC-VLA, a force-aware framework that bridges task-level VLA action generation and low-level whole-body compensation control for wheeled-legged robots. First, we introduce HSR-Force, a sensorless residual-torque estimator for inferring contact strength and its temporal variation. These contact estimates are then encoded as tokens and injected into the VLA action expert during action decoding, enabling the policy to perceive contact onset, sustained loading, and release. For loco-manipulation tasks, all parameters of the pretrained VLA backbone are fine-tuned on our WL\&Arm Dataset, which comprises more than 5,000 episodes. Moreover, the robot's proprioceptive state, the Jacobian-derived body-frame force estimate, and the estimated contact state are jointly fed into a compensation generator to produce corrective actions. The manipulation-centric actions are subsequently combined with the corrective actions and passed to the WBC policy for execution. Real-world experiments on whiteboard wiping and door opening with a door closer demonstrate the effectiveness of our FWBC-VLA in contact-rich loco-manipulation.

</details>


### [48] [A hybrid pipeline for dynamic ontology-based semantic mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03891)
*Konstantinos Dimitropoulos,Ioannis Hatzilygeroudis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合语义映射管线，结合外部标定相机（单应投影）、目标检测、持久目标跟踪与本体驱动的语义更新，并利用线性回归校正真实世界坐标，从而构建动态语义世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要具备语义映射能力才能完成复杂的交互、操作与导航任务。传统语义映射管线虽已成熟，但对环境的上下文理解不足，缺乏先验知识（如知识图谱或语义场景图）的整合，难以实现动态环境下的语义建模。

Method: 提出混合管线：1) 通过外部标定相机结合单应投影实现几何映射与定位；2) 结合目标检测与持久目标跟踪感知语义；3) 采用本体驱动的语义更新机制，连续更新对象实例、空间属性和语义关系；4) 使用线性回归模型修正真实世界坐标的估计值；5) 选择本体作为知识表示形式以利用其层级结构、语义表达能力及对动态世界建模的支持。

Result: 系统能够基于实时传感器数据持续更新对象实例、空间属性与语义关系，实现对环境的动态语义世界模型构建。

Conclusion: 结合几何映射与本体驱动的语义推理的混合体系结构是构建动态语义世界模型的有效途径，本体因其层级结构、语义表达力及动态建模支持而被优选为知识表示形式。

Abstract: Semantic mapping plays a crucial role in the ability of a robot to interact with objects, operate and navigate a complex environment. The most common pipeline for semantic mapping consists of geometric mapping and localization (SLAM), perception, semantic fusion and semantic representation. However, more recent works also integrate a form of prior knowledge in their application, most notably knowledge graphs or semantic scene graphs, to improve contextual understanding of the environment. In this paper, we present a hybrid pipeline for semantic mapping. Our system incorporates an external calibrated camera using homography projection for geometric mapping and localization, combined with object detection, persistent object tracking and ontology driven semantic updates to build a dynamic semantic world model. Linear regression models are also used for correction of the estimated values of real world coordinates. The system continuously updates object instances, spatial properties and semantic relations based on real time sensory data. Ontologies are selected as form of knowledge representation due to their hierarchical structure, semantic expressiveness and support for dynamic world modelling.

</details>


### [49] [Revisiting Topological Graphs for Macro Action based Closed-loop Reinforcement Learning of Vision Language Navigation in Continuous Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03906)
*Shuhao Ye,Sitong Mao,Yuxiang Cui,Yufei Wei,Xuan Yu,Shichao Zhai,Wen Chen,Shunbo Zhou,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对连续环境中的视觉语言导航（VLN-CE）问题，提出将任务重新建模为层次化马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），通过拓扑图抽象将高层规划与低层控制解耦，并采用基于图结构的PPO算法在宏观动作空间上进行强化学习训练，在R2R-CE和RxR-CE基准上取得了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模仿学习（IL）流程在闭环VLN-CE场景中面临挑战：行为克隆存在分布偏移问题，DAgger在轨迹偏离后专家动作变得模糊。虽然强化学习（RL）是应对这一问题的自然范式，但直接在微观动作空间上应用RL因奖励稀疏而样本效率低下。

Method: 本文将VLN-CE重构为层次化马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），将高层规划与低层控制显式解耦。具体而言：(1) 将环境抽象为拓扑图，高层策略在由前沿节点构成的宏观动作空间上操作；(2) 采用免训练的低层控制器作为状态转移函数；(3) 提出动作感知的价值头（action-aware value head）以在动态前沿动作空间中有效评估状态价值；(4) 基于此构建图结构的PPO算法进行闭环RL优化。

Result: 大量实验验证了所提架构的有效性，模型在R2R-CE和RxR-CE基准上均取得了最先进（state-of-the-art）的性能。

Conclusion: 通过层次化MDP重构，将决策视角从低层微动作提升到高层宏观动作，显著压缩决策跨度，使闭环强化学习在VLN-CE中变得可行且高效，有效解决了该任务中IL方法的固有局限。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires an agent to follow natural language instructions through unseen environments. Existing imitation learning (IL) pipelines struggle in this closed-loop setting: behavior cloning suffers from distribution shift, and DAgger's expert actions become ambiguous upon trajectory deviation. While Reinforcement Learning (RL) offers a natural paradigm to address this, directly applying RL to micro action spaces is sample-inefficient due to reward sparsity. To overcome this bottleneck, we reformulate VLN-CE as a Hierarchical Markov Decision Process (MDP), explicitly decoupling high-level planning from low-level control. By abstracting the environment into a topological graph, our high-level policy operates on a macro action space of frontier nodes, with a training-free low-level controller acting as its state transition, which significantly compresses the decision horizon and makes closed-loop RL tractable. To support RL optimization on the macro MDP, we propose an action-aware value head to effectively evaluate state values under the dynamic frontier action space, powering a graph-based PPO. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our architecture. Finally, our model achieves state-of-the-art performance on the R2R-CE and RxR-CE benchmarks.

</details>


### [50] [Toward Unified Robot Learning: Bridging Representation, Vision-Language-Action, and World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03927)
*Shaunak A. Mehta,Ananya Hazarika,Haochen Zhang,Fan Yang,Ryo Moriyama,Wenkai Li,Yash Patel,Kanata Suzuki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的机器人学习视角，将现有方法沿着三个互补轴组织：通过表示学习进行理解、通过VLA模型进行动作、通过世界模型进行推理，并系统总结了这些组件如何交互、常见局限以及迈向集成系统的趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人学习中的表示学习、VLA模型和世界模型三大范式通常被孤立地开发，导致系统碎片化，难以实现泛化、长时程时序推理与规划以及在非结构化环境中的部署。作者认为这些挑战不仅源于各组件内部的局限，更源于感知、动作与推理之间缺乏集成。

Method: 本文是一篇综述，通过提出结构化分类法捕获环境表示、策略学习和预测建模中的关键设计选择，沿三条互补轴（理解-表示学习、动作-VLA模型、推理-世界模型）对现有方法进行分类，并分析组件间的交互关系，讨论共同局限并识别新兴趋势。

Result: 总结了三方面（表示学习、VLA模型、世界模型）的最新进展，构建了系统化的统一分类框架，识别了关键挑战（不确定性量化、分布外泛化、跨本体迁移、长上下文理解和长时程规划），并论证了这些挑战与组件间缺乏集成之间的关联。

Conclusion: 未来方向指向统一、物理可接地且概率化的机器人学习，以开发能够在真实环境中维持一致内部表示并支持长时间交互决策的稳健机器人系统。

Abstract: For robots to operate reliably in real-world environments, they need to perceive their surroundings, act, and reason about the consequences of those actions. Rapid progress in the domains of representation learning, VLA models, and world models has significantly enhanced the capabilities of robot learning systems, enabling robots to work in increasingly complex environments. However, these paradigms are typically developed in isolation, resulting in fragmented systems that struggle with generalization, long-horizon temporal reasoning and planning, and deployment in unstructured environments. In this survey, we present a unified perspective on robot learning by organizing the existing methods along three complementary axes: understanding through representation learning, acting through VLA models, and reasoning through world models. We introduce a structured taxonomy that captures key design choices in environment representation, policy learning, and predictive modeling, and summarize the recent progress in these domains. Beyond classifying the existing works, we analyze how these components interact, discuss common limitations, and highlight emerging trends towards more integrated systems. Through this lens, we identify the challenges in the domain of robot learning, including uncertainty quantification, out-of-distribution generalization, cross-embodiment transfer, long-context understanding, and long-horizon planning. We argue that these challenges arise not only from limitations within individual components but also from the lack of integration across perception, action, and reasoning. Building on this analysis, we outline future directions towards unified, physically grounded, and probabilistic robot learning to develop robust robotic systems that maintain consistent internal representations and support decision making over extended interactions in real-world environments.

</details>


### [51] [Automated Weld Seam Recognition and 3D Mapping for Robotic Post Processing Using Photogrammetry and Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03970)
*Augustin Raju,Abilash Madavath,Chandra Yuvesh Aubeeluck,Nicolas Pyschny,Felix Hackelöer,Florian Zwanzig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉的焊缝近似定位流程，作为高精度测量的预备阶段。该方法通过多视角图像采集、语义分割识别焊缝、摄影测量重建工件，并将分割出的焊缝投影到重建模型中，以减少大规模工件扫描的时间和数据处理量，提升数据采集效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型工件使用高精度激光扫描仪或结构光传感器进行完整表面扫描耗时且会产生大量无关数据。为了实现自动化机器人后处理操作（如打磨、精加工、检测），需要一种高效的焊缝定位方法作为高精度测量的前置步骤。

Method: 该方法包含四个步骤：(1) 从多个视角采集工件图像；(2) 使用语义分割技术从图像中识别焊缝；(3) 利用摄影测量技术重建工件三维模型；(4) 将识别出的焊缝投影到重建模型中，实现焊缝的近似定位。

Result: 本文为实验性管道研究，侧重于方法框架的提出与验证，旨在证明该流程能够减少整体扫描工作量并提高数据采集效率，为后续高精度测量提供焊缝的初步定位。

Conclusion: 该视觉管道能够在大规模工件上实现焊缝的近似定位，作为高精度测量的预备阶段，可有效降低扫描成本和时间开销。该方法在自动化机器人后处理操作中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Accurate identification of weld seam geometries is essential for automated robotic post processing operations such as grinding, finishing, and inspection. For large workpieces, complete surface scanning using high precision laser scanners or structured light sensors can be time consuming and often generates substantial amount of data that are not relevant. This paper presents an experimental vision based pipeline for the approximate localization of weld seams. This serves as a preliminary stage before high precision measurement. The proposed approach aims to reduce the overall scanning effort and data acquisition efficiency. The proposed method includes capturing images of the workpiece from multiple viewpoints, identifying weld seams from the images using semantic segmentation, reconstructing the workpiece using photogrammetry, and projection of identified weld seams into the reconstructed model.

</details>

*Kangmai Hu,Yueqi Zhang,Peng Zhai,Xiaoyi Wei,Jiabin Hu,Zhixiang Liu,Quancheng Qian,Lihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态扩散策略（MulDP），将视觉感知与机器人本体感觉及目标信息相结合，生成时间一致且具有前瞻性的导航速度指令，解决四足机器人自主跑酷导航中高层规划依赖人工干预的问题，并构建了首个四足跑酷导航数据集（QPND）以支持训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管四足机器人在复杂地形的跑酷运动上已展现出令人印象深刻的灵活性，但大多数系统仍依赖人工进行高层规划，自主跑酷导航尚未被充分探索。关键挑战包括细粒度速度调节、长时域前瞻行为以及感知与具身执行之间的紧密耦合。

Method: 提出多模态扩散策略（MulDP），该策略将视觉感知、机器人本体感觉与目标信息进行集成，通过扩散模型生成时间一致且具有前瞻性的导航速度指令，从而实现感知与具身控制的紧密耦合。同时构建了首个四足跑酷导航数据集（QPND），一个涵盖多样导航行为与复杂地形的多模态数据集，用于支持MulDP的训练。

Result: 大量仿真与真实世界实验表明，MulDP能够实现鲁棒的长时间跨度自主导航，并在复杂地形上实现有效的越障与穿行。

Conclusion: 本文通过扩散模型驱动的多模态策略，有效打通了四足机器人中高层感知规划与底层具身执行之间的鸿沟，为实现无需人工干预的自主跑酷导航提供了可行的技术路径，并通过公开数据集推动了该方向的研究。

Abstract: Quadruped robots have demonstrated impressive agility in parkour locomotion across complex terrains. However, most systems still rely on human intervention for high-level planning, and autonomous parkour navigation remains underexplored. The key challenges include fine-grained velocity regulation, long-horizon anticipatory behaviors, and tight coupling between perception and embodied execution. To address these challenges, we propose a Multimodal Diffusion Policy (MulDP) that integrates visual perception with robot proprioception and goal information to generate temporally coherent and anticipatory navigation velocity commands, tightly coupling perception with embodied control to enable robust autonomous navigation. To support the training of MulDP, we construct the first Quadruped Parkour Navigation Dataset (QPND), a multimodal dataset that encompasses diverse navigation behaviors and complex terrains. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that MulDP enables robust long-horizon autonomous navigation and effective traversal across complex terrains.

</details>


### [53] [Adaptive Vision-Language Grasping via Composable Foundation Priors and Generalizable Grasp Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04096)
*Sixu Yan,Shikang Wang,Binhua Huang,Xuanlai Tang,Guohua Fan,Fan Huang,Haoxuan Li,Yongkang Li,Yuhan Li,Bencheng Liao,Zeyu Zhang,Wenyu Liu,Hangxin Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaRoboVLG，一个任务自适应视觉-语言-抓取(VLG)框架，支持跨不同机器人手的通用抓取合成。与现有将基础模型与端到端抓取策略紧密耦合的方法不同，AdaRoboVLG通过学习高效的可泛化基础策略——通过显式运动学映射和力闭合稳定性估计来生成与评估物理可行的抓取候选——同时将任务相关理解卸载给专门的基础模型模块。这些模块提供可组合的先验知识并集成到抓取合成过程中，无需重训练底层抓取策略即可实现上下文自适应抓取合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLG方法将基础模型与端到端抓取策略紧密耦合，导致系统难以跨不同机械手泛化，且抓取策略升级或适配新任务需要重新训练整个系统。作者旨在解耦物理抓取合成与任务相关理解，以建立可扩展的抓取范式。

Method: AdaRoboVLG包含两部分：(1) 一个可泛化的基础抓取策略，利用显式运动学映射和力闭合(force-closure)稳定性评估来生成并筛选物理可行的抓取候选；(2) 多个任务相关的基础模型模块，提供空间、认知和时间三类可组合先验，这些先验在抓取合成过程中集成，在不重训练基础策略的情况下实现上下文自适应抓取。

Result: 在仿真与真实实验中验证：(i) 基础策略学习高效且具备强跨手泛化能力；(ii) 框架可有效整合空间、认知和时间先验以应对三类典型抓取挑战，抓取合成性能不亚于现有最先进方法；(iii) 多种先验可联合工作，实现在杂乱动态环境中的功能性抓取。

Conclusion: 将物理抓取合成与任务相关理解解耦是一种可扩展的机器人抓取范式，使得未来基础模型的进步可以直接转化为抓取能力的提升，而无需重新设计或重训练底层抓取策略。

Abstract: This paper proposes AdaRoboVLG, a task-adaptive Vision-Language-Grasp (VLG) framework that supports generalizable grasp synthesis across different robotic hands. Unlike existing VLG methods that tightly couple foundation models with end-to-end grasp policies, AdaRoboVLG learns an efficient generalizable base policy that generates and evaluates physically feasible grasp candidates through explicit kinematic mapping and force-closure-based stability estimation, while offloading task-dependent understanding to specialized foundation-model modules. These modules provide composable priors that are integrated into the grasp synthesis process, enabling contextually adaptive grasp synthesis without retraining the underlying grasp policy. Through extensive simulation and real-world experiments, we demonstrate that (i) the base policy exhibits efficient learning and strong cross-hand generalization, (ii) the framework effectively incorporates spatial, cognitive, and temporal priors to address three representative grasping challenges without compromising grasp synthesis performance compared to state-of-the-art methods, and (iii) these priors can operate jointly to enable functional grasping in cluttered and dynamic environments. These results indicate that decoupling physical grasp synthesis from task-dependent understanding provides a scalable paradigm for robotic grasping, allowing future advances in foundation models to be directly translated into improved grasp capabilities without redesigning or retraining the underlying grasp policy. Supplementary videos are available at https://adarobovlg.github.io/

</details>


### [54] [Corner Cases: Headland Coverage Path Planning for Autonomous Driving in Arable Farming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04103)
*Riikka Soitinaho,Timo Oksanen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的耕地地头覆盖路径规划方法，通过修改多边形角点以允许倒车转弯，从而改善田间角落（尤其是约90度及240度以上角度）的覆盖效果，并与两种现有方法比较，在间隙、重叠及越界方面均有改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地头覆盖路径规划方法多采用嵌套多边形和平滑转弯策略，但在完全覆盖田间角落时需要倒车操作，已有方法未能很好地解决这一问题，导致角落覆盖不完整。

Method: 新方法通过修改多边形角点结构，允许在角落处进行倒车转弯（reversing turn），从而实现对田间角落的完整覆盖。

Result: 与两种现有方法在间隙、重叠和跨越地块边界三个指标上进行比较，结果显示新方法在覆盖效果上有所改善，尤其是在约90度以及240度及以上的田间角落场景中；并通过多个真实多边形地块地图示例验证了方法的适用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的角点修改与倒车转弯策略能有效提升耕地地头特别是极端角度角落的覆盖完整性，且具有实际多边形地块应用的可行性。

Abstract: This paper presents a new method for headland coverage path planning for arable fields. Several earlier approaches suggest covering the headland with nested polygons and smooth turns, however, covering the field corners entirely requires manoeuvres with reversing. In the new method, the polygon corners are modified to allow a reversing turn. A comparison to two other methods considering gap, overlap, and crossing the field boundary shows an improvement in the coverage result especially in field corners of around 90 degrees, and 240 degrees and above. Applicability of the new method is shown with several examples of real polygonal field maps.

</details>


### [55] [GIFT: Guided Intermediate Feature Training via Action-Oriented Structural Supervision for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04193)
*Yupeng Zheng,Xiang Li,Songen Gu,Yuhang Zheng,Shuai Tian,Weize Li,Linbo Wang,Chaoyue Li,Qichao Zhang,Haoran Li,Zhongpu Xia,Ya-Qin Zhang,Shuicheng Yan,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GIFT（Guided Intermediate Feature Training）框架，通过引导中间特征保留三种控制相关结构（几何、意图编码、目标定位）来弥合视觉-语言预训练与机器人控制之间的"动作充分性差距"。GIFT在三种不同动作公式的模型（VLA、直接动作WAM、逆动力学WAM）上均显著提升零样本迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言预训练和预测世界模型为机器人策略提供了丰富的语义和动态视觉特征，但其原生的动作和视觉预测目标可能遗漏关键的物理与任务结构，同时保留与控制器无关的视觉冗余信息。作者将视觉丰富性与控制效用之间的这种不匹配定义为"动作充分性差距"（action-sufficiency gap），并探究能否通过引导中间特征来弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出GIFT框架，引导中间特征保留三类控制相关结构：(1) 几何对齐——编码决定运动可行性的几何信息；(2) 意图预测——编码指令相关实体的可采用性；(3) 目标区域重建——将指令扎根于任务相关区域。该框架将这些结构转化为训练时的约束，是架构灵活的，可实例化到VLA策略、直接动作WAM和逆动力学WAM中，同时保持各模型原有的动作公式不变。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus零样本迁移中，GIFT-VLA、GIFT-WAM-Fast、GIFT-WAM-IDM分别达到79.6%、72.6%、87.8%，超出基线4.6、12.6、5.2分。在RoboCasa上分别达到61.4%、83.6%、82.3%，超出对应模型12.6、9.0、8.4分。在铰接物体任务和未见视觉/空间扰动下的高精度真实世界操纵中增益尤其显著。

Conclusion: 工作证明"学习功能结构化的中间特征"是跨不同模型特定动作公式的可复用原则。该结果验证了通过显式引导中间表征保留控制相关结构可系统性弥合动作充分性差距，且不依赖特定动作模型架构。

Abstract: Vision-language pre-training and predictive world modeling provide robot policies with rich semantic and dynamic visual features, but their native action and visual-prediction objectives may omit critical physical and task structure while retaining control-irrelevant visual redundancy. We call this mismatch between visual richness and control utility the action-sufficiency gap. We investigate whether this gap can be bridged by guiding intermediate features to preserve three control-relevant structure in robotic manipulation: geometry governing motion feasibility, affordance encoding instruction-relevant entities, and goals grounding instructions in task-relevant regions. To this end, we present GIFT (Guided Intermediate Feature Training), an architecture-flexible framework for learning intermediate features that translates these structures into training-time constraints through geometry alignment, affordance prediction, and goal-region reconstruction. We instantiate GIFT in a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policy, a direct-action World-Action Model (WAM), and an inverse-dynamics WAM while retaining each model's action formulation. Under zero-shot transfer to LIBERO-Plus, GIFT-VLA, GIFT-WAM-Fast, and GIFT-WAM-IDM outperform StarVLA-OFT, Fast-WAM, and Fast-WAM-IDM by 4.6, 12.6, and 5.2 points, reaching 79.6%, 72.6%, and 87.8%, respectively. On RoboCasa, the three GIFT variants reach 61.4%, 83.6%, and 82.3%, outperforming their counterparts by 12.6, 9.0, and 8.4 points, respectively. Together, these results establish learning functionally structured intermediate features as a reusable principle across model-specific action formulations, with especially large gains on articulated-object tasks and high-precision real-world manipulation under unseen visual and spatial perturbations. Project page: https://openphoenix-team.github.io/GIFT-pages.

</details>

### [56] [Valley polarization dependence of candidate even-denominator fractional quantum Hall states in monolayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02976)
*Saswata Sahu,Amarendra Pr. Indra,Moumita Indra*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了单层石墨烯中的偶分母分数量子霍尔态（ν=1/2 和 ν=1/4）。鉴于自旋与谷自由度带来的近似SU(4)对称性，作者利用SU(4)系统的Chern-Simons复合费米子框架，构造了对应不同自旋、谷及混合极化构型的候选变分波函数，并在球面几何下用库仑相互作用数值计算了基态能量。能量比较表明，能量有利的候选态为多分量关联量子霍尔液体，且与简单的复合费米子费米海描述不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 陈化理解单层石墨烯中实验观测到的偶分母分数量子霍尔态（ν=1/2和ν=1/4）的微观物理机制。由于石墨烯中自旋与谷自由度共同构成近似SU(4)对称性，可能存在多种多分量极化构型，需要通过变分能量比较来确定哪些构型在能量上更受青睐，从而揭示谷极化与SU(4)对称性在稳定这些奇特量子态中的作用。

Method: 将Chern-Simons复合费米子框架推广到SU(4)对称系统，针对ν=1/2和ν=1/4构建了分别对应自旋极化、谷极化及混合极化态的多组候选变分波函数；在球面几何上使用库仑相互作用对每个候选态的能量进行数值评估，并在不同极化扇区之间进行系统化的能量对比。

Abstract: Rutile RuO2 is a prominent candidate for altermagnetism, yet its magnetic ground state remains highly controversial, with experiments reporting either a nonmagnetic state or altermagnetic order. These results provide a unified explanation for the conflicting experimental observations and establish a general microscopic framework for understanding how material perturbations select competing magnetic ground states in systems with nearly flat magnetic energy landscapes.

Abstract: Quantum many-body systems exhibit rich and complex behaviour. Magic has recently emerged as a powerful new diagnostic for probing such systems, complementing other key features such as many-body entanglement. Here, starting from an investigation of the onset of magic within subsystems of a larger many-body quantum system, we show that intricate structures emerge which shed important light on the underlying many-body physics. Abstract: Phase transitions are typically driven by symmetry-breaking structural distortions that lift electronic degeneracies, yet in some cases, these transitions may be driven by a hidden order without conventional structural signatures. Abstract: Quantum criticality in one-dimensional quantum systems is characterized by emergent conformal field theories (CFTs), whose central charge counts independent gapless degrees of freedom. Abstract: The Kibble-Zurek mechanism (KZM) and finite-time scaling (FTS) provide a foundational framework for driven critical dynamics, yet their predictive power has been largely confined to local observables. Here, we establish a universal finite-time scaling theory for the nonequilibrium dynamics of quantum entanglement. Using unbiased quantum Monte Carlo simulations, we investigate the corner entanglement entropy of (2+1)-dimensional interacting Dirac fermions driven from ordered phases toward a quantum critical point. Result: ESR的g因子和线宽在三个不同区域（顺磁费米液体、反铁磁有序、量子临界区）呈现出截然不同的行为。在直接冷却趋向量子临界点的过程中（固定场冷），g因子和线宽均持续减小。此外，磁场调谐与温度调谐两种方式趋向量子临界点时，g因子的变化行为表现出非常好的匹配。

Abstract: In the heavy-fermion metal YbRh2Si2, quantum criticality at a suppressed antiferromagnetic order is governed by the interplay of local magnetic moments and itinerant conduction electrons. We demonstrate how this can be investigated by a new experimental approach that enables the observation of electron spin resonance (ESR) across a broad range of frequencies and fields at very low temperatures. This allowed us to cover a large part of the phase diagram from the paramagnetic Fermi-liquid phase to the phase with antiferromagnetic order and including the quantum-critical regime. Both the ESR g-factor and the linewidth present distinct behaviors in these three regimes, providing further insight into the physics across a quantum critical point. Notably, when cooling down at a field directly towards the quantum critical point, both g-factor and linewidth continuously decrease. Furthermore, we observe a very good matching of the g-factor behavior upon field-tuning and temperature-tuning towards the quantum-critical point. We analyze and discuss the results in the context of present theories on ESR in strongly correlated electron systems.

</details>

Abstract: Describing competing phases of strongly correlated systems often requires trial wave functions built from phase-specific assumptions. We propose the \emph{hyperdeterminant (HyperDet) wavefunction} as a phase-agnostic ansatz for both bosonic and fermionic quantum many-body systems exhibiting spontaneous symmetry-breaking order, fractionalization, and/or topological order with anyonic excitations. The HyperDet structure emerges naturally by fusing auxiliary fermionic parton Slater determinants into physical orbitals through a fully learnable \emph{fusion tensor} $\mathcal F$. Optimized using variational Monte Carlo, a single HyperDet architecture can achieve exceptionally high overlaps $\geq 99.9\%$ with exact-diagonalization ground states throughout the entire fractional Chern insulator phase in both bosonic and fermionic models, and across their nearby competing phases. We introduce the singular-value spectrum of the \emph{bipartite fusion matrix} as a structural diagnostic of fusion tensor, and find that its redistribution tracks many-body phase transitions without computing phase-specific observables. The optimized fusion tensor also encodes the parton-level topological data: it reproduces the parton Chern numbers expected for the bosonic and fermionic FCI states, completing their field-theory descriptions and the resulting topological order. Its intrinsic gauge structure further determines whether physical symmetries admit virtual lifts and, when faithful lifts exist, extracts their projective class; for the bosonic FCI, this recovers the expected parton translation fractionalization. We thus anticipate the HyperDet wavefunction to be a promising variational platform for both accurate ground-state searches and phase-diagram explorations across strongly correlated phases, and for providing interpretable theoretical insights from parton-level microscopics to field-theory descriptions.

Abstract: Moiré materials host fractional Chern insulators and electron crystals in close proximity, but the mechanism selecting between them remains an open question. We address this competition in Chern bands with ideal but momentum-dependent quantum geometry -- Aharonov-Casher bands. We present an ansatz wave function for generalized Wigner crystals and, by comparing its energy to that of the competing Laughlin-like state, map out the phase diagram at filling fraction $ν=1/m$ as a function of the degree of geometric non-uniformity. Remarkably, for the semion case, the superconducting many-semion wavefunction is equivalent to a state of fermionized anyons filling two effective Landau levels, recovering Laughlin's original construction of semion superconductor. Finally, we show that hierarchy wavefunctions emerge naturally in the dilute, long-distance limit of the anyon-Hilbert-space formulation, which applies to ideal Chern bands and moiré bands of twisted bilayer MoTe$_2$. For the $\mathbf{F}_{q^2}$-maximal function field $\mathcal{F}_i=\mathbf{F}_{q^2}(x,y)$ defined by $y^m=x^i(x^2+1)$, Peter Beelen, Maria Montanucci, Jonathan Niemann, and Luciane Quoos showed that the geometric automorphism group contains a subgroup of order $4(q+1)$ and conjectured that its order is exactly $4(q+1)$. We prove this equality by establishing the reverse inequality.

If $χ$ is injective, we present two necessary and sufficient criteria for lifting $V$ to an $R[G]$-module $\widetilde{V}$ which is a free $R$-module: (i) when no extra requirement is made on $\widetilde{V}$ and (ii) when we require $\widetilde{V}^{C_{p^s}}=\{0\}$. The criteria correct several results in the literature and we use them to prove that, if $χ$ is injective and $G$ acts faithfully on a connected smooth projective curve $X$ over $k$, then, under mild hypotheses satisfied if $X\rightarrow X/G$ is a Harbater--Katz--Gabber cover, the $k[G]$-module $H^0(X,Ω_X)$ has a lift $\widetilde{V}$ to $R$ with $\widetilde{V}^{C_{p^s}}=\{0\}$. With $B$ as the field of fractions of $R$, we prove the following obstruction when $p$ is odd, $G/\operatorname{Ker}(χ)$ has even order, and $X/C_{p^s}\cong\mathbb{P}^1_k$: if no such lift $\widetilde{V}$ exists with the $B[H]$-module $\widetilde{V}\otimes_R B$ defined over $\mathbb{Q}$, then the action of $G$ on $X$ does not lift to $R$.

Abstract: Qin expressed central values of the congruent number curve $E_n:y^2=x^3-n^2x$ through differences of ternary quadratic form representation numbers, while Zhang gave explicit formulas for the Cassels pairing on the pure $2$-Selmer group. We prove that the parity of Qin's normalized representation defect is the Pfaffian of the Cassels pairing. We also prove an even analogue and a higher dimensional extension.

Result: 对于 0 ≤ k < n，f(x) 是 F_{2^n} 上的置换当且仅当 k=0 或 k=1，完整证实了 Helleseth 和 Zinoviev 的猜想。

### [82] [Odd denominators in the Lonely Runner spectrum for six speeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03444)

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了孤独跑者猜想（Lonely Runner Conjecture）中关于最大化距离ML的有理化性质。针对Kravitz猜想和Fan-Sun猜想，本文解释了为何n=6时仅有k=1和k=3出现：证明了几乎所有满足ML<1/6的六元组都有ML=(P-1)/(6P)（其中P≡1或5 mod 6），并通过确定三个无限二元参数族精确计算ML来证明这一结论。类似方法结合Chen的分类也证明了n=5时几乎所有情形满足Kravitz原始猜想。穷举验证了2×10^9个六元组无例外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤独跑者猜想是数论中著名的未解问题。Kravitz提出了一个关于ML分子分母关系的猜想，Fan和Sun找到了n=4时的反例并提出了修正猜想，同时观察到n=6时分母只有k=1和k=3两种取值。本文旨在解释这一经验观察背后的数学原因。

Method: 对于n=6的情况，作者证明了除有限多个例外外，所有满足ML<1/6的六元组均满足ML=(P-1)/(6P)，其中P为≡1或5 mod 6的整数。证明分为三步：(1)确定所有这样的值集中的三个无限二元参数族；(2)在每个族上精确计算ML；(3)精确描述k=3出现的位置。对于n=5的情况，结合Chen对达到ML=1/5的元组的分类，使用相同方法证明几乎所有情形满足Kravitz原始猜想。计算采用精确运算并提供代码。

Result: 证明了对于六个速度，除有限多个例外元组外，满足ML<1/6的元组均有ML=(P-1)/(6P)（P≡1或5 mod 6），从而k只能取1或3且分母q为奇数。对速度不超过110的2×10^9个六元组进行穷举搜索未发现例外。对于五个速度，证明了几乎所有满足ML<1/5的元组满足Kravitz的原始猜想。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上严格证明了Fan-Sun在n=6时观察到的k只取1或3的现象并非巧合，而是由三个无限二元参数族的结构所决定。这一结果表明Kravitz-Fan-Sun猜想中k的取值范围与维度n的算术性质密切相关，为理解孤独跑者猜想的深层结构提供了重要进展。

Abstract: For distinct positive integers v_1, ..., v_n let ML(v_1, ..., v_n) be the largest number L such that at some time t every t_vi is at distance at least L from the nearest integer; the Lonely Runner Conjecture asserts that ML >= 1/(n+1). Write ML = p/q in lowest terms. Kravitz conjectured that whenever ML < 1/n one has q = np + 1; Fan and Sun found counterexamples for n = 4, conjectured that q = np + k with 1 <= k <= n always holds, and observed that in their data for n = 6 only k = 1 and k = 3 occur. We explain this observation. For six speeds we show that all but finitely many tuples with ML < 1/6 satisfy ML = (P-1)/(6P) for an integer P congruent to 1 or 5 modulo 6; in particular k is 1 or 3 and the denominator q is odd. The proof determines the three infinite two-parameter families of tuples on which such values concentrate, computes ML exactly on each family, and describes exactly where k = 3 occurs. An exhaustive search over the 2 x 10^9 sextuples with speeds at most 110 finds no exception. For five speeds the same method, together with Chen's classification of the tuples attaining ML = 1/5, shows that all but finitely many tuples with ML < 1/5 satisfy Kravitz's original conjecture. The computations are exact and the code is provided.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2005年Dujella和Luca给出了素数p情形下正整数D(p)或D(-p)元组基数的一致绝对上界，但原始论证依赖特定素数情形下的消元步骤，无法直接推广到素数幂（如p²、p³等）。本文旨在将这一gap论证扩展到素数幂情形，从而对p^r形式的正整数n给出统一基数的上界。

Method: 核心方法是用显式toric消元证书替代原先的非饱和齐次消元步骤。该证书通过初等线性代数证明，且模任意素数幂均有效。再利用该消元项的显式度数与高度界建立统一gap原理；结合|n|有界时的已知一般界，导出对素数幂p^r的最终基数上界。

Result: 证明每个正约化D(±p^r)元组（即元素不被p整除）的元素个数小于2^121，该界与p和r无关。推论：正D(±p)元组至多2^121个元素，正D(±p²)元组少于2^122个元素，而正D(±p^r)元组的最大基数为O(r)，对素数p一致。

Conclusion: 通过引入普适于所有素数幂情形的消元证书，成功将Dujella-Luca的素数结果推广到素数幂，获得对p和r一致（或对r为线性）的显式基数上界，填补了素数幂情形下该问题的空白。

Abstract: A positive $D(n)$-$m$-tuple is a set $A=\{a_1,\ldots,a_m\}$ of distinct positive integers such that $a_i a_j+n$ is a square for every $i\ne j$. In 2005, Dujella and Luca obtained an absolute bound for the cardinality of a $D(p)$- or $D(-p)$-tuple of positive integers, uniformly in the prime $p$. We extend the underlying gap argument to prime powers. An explicit toric elimination certificate, proved by elementary linear algebra, replaces the unsaturated homogeneous elimination step and is valid modulo every prime power. Explicit degree and height bounds for this eliminant yield a uniform gap principle. Combined with the general bound for bounded $|n|$, this shows that every positive reduced $D(\pm p^r)$-tuple (i.e. tuple with elements not divisible by $p$) has less than $2^{121}$ elements, independently of $p$ and $r$. Thus, every positive $D(\pm p)$-tuple has at most $2^{121}$ elements, positive $D(\pm p^2)$-tuples have less than $2^{122}$ elements, and the maximal cardinality of a positive $D(\pm p^r)$-tuple is $O(r)$ uniformly in $p$.

</details>

Result: 确定了该三次方程的分裂域正是X(14)的函数域，实现了Klein-Vélu七次曲线的2-除几何与水平7到水平14模结构提升之间显式而精确的对应，并给出了S3-扩张中各级中间模函数域的描述。

Conclusion: 本文通过显式的代数构造与theta函数表达，架起了椭圆正规七次曲线2-除几何与模曲线水平结构提升之间的桥梁，给出了S3-扩张的完整子域刻画，为理解更高水平的模函数域结构提供了范例。

Abstract: We study the 2-division of the Klein-Vélu elliptic normal septic. Its three non-zero 2-torsion points give rise to an explicit cubic equation over the function field of $X(7)$. ### [85] [The derived set of multiple star polylogarithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03653)
*Jiangtao Li*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了单位圆上的多重星多对数函数（multiple star polylogarithms）的二维推广问题。作者证明了移位多重星多对数函数是完全单调序列的生成函数，从而具有归一化的Hausdorff-Stieltjes表示；其有限阶情形下的表示密度满足严格单调似然比序。通过Hausdorff-Stieltjes类的闭包性质和极限商论证，证明了上/下半圆的平移曲线的幅角严格递增且模长严格递减，以及不同指标对应曲线的严格分离。文章还建立了单位圆上的点与无穷指标对的自然集合与闭半平面Re w≥1/2（去掉点1）之间的一一对应（在同胚意义下）。作为应用，证明了移位分圆多重zeta-star值的一个子类构成开半平面Re w>1/2的可数稠密子集，从而该分圆族的导集及所有更高阶导集均为闭半平面Re w≥1/2。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典结果指出多重zeta-star值的导集是半直线[1,+∞)。作者希望将这一一维结果推广到二维情形，研究单位圆上的多重星多对数函数及其相关的分圆多重zeta-star值，以理解其导集结构及其与复半平面之间的关系。

Method: 论文采用解析函数论与测度论相结合的方法：首先通过完全单调序列理论证明移位多重星多对数函数的Hausdorff-Stieltjes表示存在；随后利用密度函数的单调似然比序和Hausdorff-Stieltjes类的闭包性质，结合极限商论证来分析单位圆上平移曲线的几何性质（幅角与模长的单调性、曲线间的分离性）；最后通过构造二进制坐标将指标集与复半平面建立同胚对应。

Conclusion: 本文将多重zeta-star值导集的经典一维结果成功地推广到二维：分圆多重zeta-star值族的导集（以及所有高阶导集）恰为闭半平面Re w≥1/2。此外，文中建立的同胚对应和Hausdorff-Stieltjes表示方法为研究这类多对数函数的解析性质提供了新工具，具有进一步推广的潜力。

Abstract: The derived set of multiple zeta-star values is the half-line $[1,+\infty)$. In this paper we study the corresponding two-dimensional problem for multiple star polylogarithms on the unit circle. We first prove that every shifted multiple star polylogarithm is the generating function of a completely monotone sequence and hence admits a normalized Hausdorff--Stieltjes representation. Both the shifted and the unshifted functions, as well as their infinite-depth limits, are shown to be univalent on the half-plane $\mathrm{Re}\,z<1$.
  For finite indices, the representing densities satisfy a strict monotone likelihood-ratio order with respect to the reverse lexicographic order. Together with closure properties of the Hausdorff--Stieltjes class and a limiting quotient argument, this shows that, along the upper semicircle, the argument of each translated curve increases strictly while its modulus decreases strictly; the corresponding lower-semicircle statement follows by conjugation. The same idea gives strict separation of the curves attached to different indices. We then establish a one-to-one correspondence between a natural set of pairs consisting of a point of the unit circle and an infinite index, and the closed half-plane $\mathrm{Re}\,w\geq \frac12$ with the point $1$ removed. Under a binary coordinate on the index set, this correspondence is a homeomorphism.
  As an application, a subclass of shifted cyclotomic multiple zeta-star values of all levels form a countable dense subset of the open half-plane $\mathrm{Re}\,w>\frac12$. Consequently, the derived set, and every higher derived set, of this cyclotomic family is the closed half-plane $\mathrm{Re}\,w\geq\frac12$.

Abstract: We prove that the algebra of multiple Eisenstein series is an $\mathfrak{sl}_2$-algebra. We therefore obtain, for every odd $p$ and every $n>1$, infinitely many representations $G_{\{p\}}\to\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{Z}_p)$ with open image. In particular, this removes the weak Vandiver-type hypothesis occurring in the author's previous work, as well as the restriction $p\geq7$ in that construction.

Abstract: For a fixed characteristic $p$, let $δ(p)$ denote the smallest degree needed to guarantee the existence of an isogeny from any supersingular curve $E$ to its Frobenius conjugate $E^{(p)}$. We translate existence questions concerning $δ(p)$ into questions about positive definite integral ternary quadratic forms and use Voronoi's theory of perfect forms in dimension three to characterize when $δ(p)$ is nearly maximal. For sufficiently large primes, we show that $δ(p)$ attains its upper bound precisely when $p$ is represented by one of nine explicit cubic polynomials, reducing the infinitude of such primes to whether one of these cubics takes prime values infinitely often. Abstract: In this paper, we study the combinatorial object $\bar{b}^k_{r,s}(n)$ which counts the overcolored partition $k$-tuples wherein both even and odd parts are colored with $r$ and $s$ colors, respectively. We extend results of Chacon and Sellers for several families of $r,s$ and $k$. We also establish divisibility properties for $\bar{b}^k_{r,s}(n)$ modulo prime $p$ and conclude with new congruences modulo powers of $2$. The techniques involved to obtain our results rely on theta function identities and modular forms.

Abstract: We consider probability measures on compact subsets of the complex plane arising as limiting distributions of Galois conjugates of certain algebraic integers. These measures are characterized by infinitely many integral inequalities, one for each nonzero integer polynomial. We study a broad family of measures given by particular convex combinations of harmonic and equilibrium measures. We then show that, for measures in this family, it suffices to verify two conditions to guarantee all the required inequalities. Under additional arithmetic constraints on the underlying compact sets, we further show that the infinite collection of inequalities is equivalent to checking the inequalities associated with two explicitly specified integer polynomials.

Abstract: In this paper, we show that $\operatorname{SL}_2(\mathbb{F}_{13})$ and $\operatorname{SL}_2(\mathbb{F}_{19})$ are Galois groups of totally real extensions of $\mathbb{Q}$. For each of these primes $\ell$, we find a polynomial of degree $\ell+1$ whose splitting field has Galois group $\operatorname{PSL}_2(\mathbb{F}_\ell)$. These fields satisfy Böge's criterion and the associated central embedding problem has a proper solution with Galois group $\operatorname{SL}_2(\mathbb{F}_\ell)$. ### [92] [On the exponential sum over squarefree integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03961)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Schlage-Puchta和Tolev给出了类似的估计，但在q和Q的依赖关系相同的情况下带有N^ε因子。作者旨在移除这一多余的因子，获得更精确的对数幂次界，从而改进Hardy-Littlewood圆法框架下次弧上的估计。

Abstract: Let $μ$ be the Möbius function and $e(t)=e^{2πit}$. We prove that if $N\ge2$, $α\in\mathbb{R}$, $(a,q)=1$, and $|α-a/q|\le q^{-2}$, then \[\bigg|\sum_{n\le N}μ^2(n)e(αn)\bigg|\ll\left(\frac Nq+q\right)(\log 2N)^5, \] with an absolute implied constant, and we deduce the corresponding estimate on the minor arcs of the Hardy--Littlewood dissection throughout the range $Q\le N^{1/2}$. The estimates of Schlage-Puchta [SP] and of Tolev [T] have the same dependence on $q$ and $Q$ but carry a factor $N^{\varepsilon}$. The proof uses Heath-Brown's square sieve with sieving primes confined to an interval $(P,2P]$, where $P$ may be as small as a multiple of $\log N$; a finite Fejér majorant in place of a truncated Fourier series; and, after completion of the character sums, a count of representations that exploits the restriction on the primes in place of the divisor function.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了素数p上n变量k次非齐次多项式在短区间（盒子）上的Dirichlet特征和，证明了非平凡上界。对于大的n，当盒子边长仅为p^{1/(k-1)+ε}时即可获得非平凡界，当k≥6时突破了Burgess屏障p^{1/4+ε}。证明的关键是发展了Burgess放大方法的新变体，将问题归结为加性特征和的有界估计，这是Burgess型方法首次在非齐次设定中取得成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短Dirichlet特征和是解析数论中的核心问题，传统的Burgess方法在处理齐次多项式一直有局限性，尤其是在非齐次设定中此前未能取得成功。作者希望突破Burgess屏障并解决非齐次多项式情形下的短特征和估计问题。

Method: 作者提出了一种新的Burgess放大方法变体。该方法的关键步骤是：通过适当的放大（amplification）技巧，将原问题中的Dirichlet特征和转化为加性特征和（additive character sums）的估计问题，从而利用加性调和分析中已有的强大工具来获得所需的非平凡界。

Result: 对于n变量k次（不必齐次）多项式上的短Dirichlet特征和证明了非平凡界。在大的n时，盒子边长可短至p^{1/(k-1)+ε}，当k≥6时显著突破Burgess屏障p^{1/4+ε}。

Conclusion: 这项工作首次表明Burgess型放大方法可以在非齐次多项式设定中成功应用，拓展了短特征和理论的应用范围，对解析数论中相关难题（如高维特征和估计）提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Let $p$ be a prime. We prove nontrivial bounds on short sums of Dirichlet characters mod $p$ evaluated at a class of polynomials, not necessarily homogeneous, in $n$ variables and of degree $k$. For large $n$, we further achieve nontrivial bounds for sums over boxes with side-lengths as short as $p^{1/(k-1)+\varepsilon}$, which breaks past the Burgess barrier of $p^{1/4+\varepsilon}$ as soon as $k\geq 6$. In the proof, we develop a new variation of the Burgess amplification method that reduces the problem to bounding additive character sums. This is the first case in which a Burgess-type method succeeds in the inhomogeneous setting.

</details>


### [94] [The subconvexity problem for symmetric square $L$-functions in level aspect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04155)
*Pratim Mitra*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了GL2(Q)上cuspidal自守表示对称平方L函数在level方面的次凸性（subconvexity）问题。对于导子为p²、中心特征非二次的tempered cuspidal自守表示π，当局部表示π_p属于特定的表示类S时，证明了L(1/2, Sym²π)的次凸界，指数为1/4−1/168。这是GL3(Q)自守表示L函数的level-aspect次凸界的首个实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 次凸性问题（subconvexity）是解析数论中L函数中心值估计的核心问题。对于GL3上的自守L函数（对称平方提升），此前已有深度（depth）方面的结果，但level方面的次凸界研究尚属空白。作者旨在填补这一空白，为GL3(Q)自守表示L函数的level-aspect次凸性提供首个证明。

Method: 该证明基于delta-symbol方法（δ符号方法），结合标准的解析数论工具。证明中关键使用了Katz的超几何和理论（hypergeometric sums）以及Deligne对Weil猜想的证明，来处理证明中出现的指数和及相关算术估计。对局部表示π_p施加了适当的条件（属于表示类S），以控制局部退化情形。

Result: 对于满足条件的表示π，获得了指数为1/4−1/168的level-aspect次凸界，其中常数关于谱参数是多项式增长的。这是GL3(Q)自守表示L函数在level方面次凸性的首个结果，打破了此前该方向无次凸界可用的局面。

Conclusion: 该结果显著推进了GL3自守L函数次凸性理论，将level-aspect次凸性从GL2拓展到GL3语境。1/168作为首个非零增量表明即使几何方法（如Katz超几何和Deligne定理）也能给出有效的下界改进，为进一步优化指数或推广到更一般局部条件提供了可能路径。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the subconvexity problem in level aspect for symmetric square $L$-functions for cuspidal automorphic representation of $\mathrm{GL}_2(\mathbb{Q})$ with a prescribed local ramification at prime $p$. To be more precise, let $π$ be a tempered cuspidal automorphic representation of conductor $q(π)=p^2$ with a non-quadratic central character of conductor $p$. We prove that if the corresponding local representation $π_p$ belongs to a suitable class of representations $\mathcal S$, then
  \[
  L\left(\frac{1}{2},\,\mathrm{Sym}^2π\right)\ll_{\varepsilon, π_\infty} q(\mathrm{Sym}^2π)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{168}+o(1)},
  \]
  where implied constant depends polynomially on the spectral parameters of $π_\infty$. This is the first instance of level-aspect subconvex bound for $L$-functions of a $\mathrm{GL}_3(\mathbb Q)$ automorphic representation. Our approach is based on the delta-symbol method. Apart from some standard analytic number theoretic tools, Katz's theory of hypergeometric sums, and Deligne's proof of Weil-conjectures play an important role in the proof.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 通过构造并运用合适的权重（suitable weights）来证明Catalan常数G的无理性，这是继Apéry等人证明ζ(3)无理性后，在经典常数无理性证明方法论上的又一次应用与拓展。

Result: 成功证明了Catalan常数G=\sum_{k=0}^\infty\frac{(-1)^k}{(2k+1)^2}是无理数，解决了这一长期悬而未决的开放问题。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入适当的权重方法，最终攻克了Catalan常数无理性这一延续两个世纪的重要数论难题，为该领域提供了突破性的结果。

Abstract: Whether the constant $$G=\sum_{k=0}^\infty\frac{(-1)^k}{(2k+1)^2}=\frac1{1^2}-\frac1{3^2}+\frac1{5^2}-\frac1{7^2}+\cdots$$ introduced by Catalan in the nineteen century is irrational, is a long-standing open problem. In this paper we prove the irrationality of $G$ via using suitable weights.

</details>

### [96] [Homology Nilpotency in Rational Homotopy Theory: Cell Attachments, Retractive Towers, Divergence, and Stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02980)
*Paul-Eugène Parent*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究单连通极小Sullivan代数的同调幂零性（homology nilpotency）。作者发展了下界与上界判据（包括有理胞腔粘附方法），构造了一个满足 nil_h(M)=3 < Hnil(M)=4 的显式有限型极小Sullivan代数。完全协调的递推塔（strictly coordinated retractive towers）用于刻画同伦幂长度并揭示同调幂零性所度量的额外商缺陷。该刚性缺陷在通过逐次有限楔单连通有理球稳定化后消失，得到 Hnil_s(X)=cat_o(X) 对任意有限型单连通有理空间 X 成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同调幂零性与同伦幂零性（以及Lusternik-Schnirelmann范畴）之间的精确关系此前未被完全理解。作者旨在建立同调幂零性的上下界判据，并阐明其与同伦幂零性之间的差距来源，最终证明在稳定化操作下该差距消失，从而将同调稳定幂零性与有理LS范畴统一起来。

Method: 作者基于极小Sullivan代数理论，给出了同调幂零性的上界和下界判据，包括针对有理胞腔粘附的递归方法。引入了"完全协调递推塔"（complete strictly coordinated retractive towers）来刻画同伦幂长度，并据此揭示同调幂零性中的额外"商缺陷"。最后通过对空间进行逐次有限楔单连通有理球构成的稳定化操作（degreewise finite wedges of spheres），证明该缺陷消失。

Result: 构造了满足 nil_h(M)=3 但 Hnil(M)=4 的显式有限型极小Sullivan代数，证明同调幂零性与同伦幂零性确实存在差距。同时证明了稳定化后 Hnil_s(X)=cat_o(X) 对所有有限型单连通有理空间 X 成立，即稳定同调幂零性恰等于有理LS范畴。

Conclusion: 同调幂零性与同伦幂零性之间的差距由递推塔结构中的商缺陷造成，该缺陷是"刚性的"（不随单纯有理胞腔粘附消失），但通过楔入有理球族进行稳定化后缺陷消失，从而稳定同调幂零性精确地等价于有理Lusternik-Schnirelmann范畴。

Abstract: Homology nilpotency of a simply connected minimal Sullivan algebra $M$ is the least integer $n$ for which $(M^+)^{n+1}$ is contained in an acyclic differential ideal. We develop lower- and upper-bound criteria, including methods for rational cell attachments, and construct an explicit finite-type minimal Sullivan algebra $M$ satisfying $nil_h(M)=3<Hnil(M)=4$.
  Complete strictly coordinated retractive towers identify the obstruction: they characterize homotopical nil-length while exposing the additional quotient defect measured by homology nilpotency. We then show that this rigid defect disappears after stabilization by degreewise finite wedges of simply connected rational spheres: $$
  Hnil_s(X)=cat_o(X) $$ for every simply connected rational space $X$ of finite type.

</details>


### [97] [Generalized Telescope Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03375)
*Jiacheng Liang*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文引入了"原子碎裂框架"（atomic smashing frame），将Balmer谱从张量三角几何推广到任意可表现对称幺半∞-范畴。作者利用对偶与刚性∞-范畴建立了碎裂理想与局部刚性局部化之间的对应关系，推导出稳定与非稳定情形的recollement定理，并将其应用于色同伦理论，证明Sp的碎裂框架自然地嵌入所有单色层Sp_{T(n)}的碎裂框架之积中，从而将Sp的框架空间性问题约化为各单色层的空间性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Balmer谱和Balmer-Favi理论局限于稳定紧致刚性生成的情形，缺乏对一般对称幺半∞-范畴的统一处理框架。作者希望构建一个更为一般的理论框架（"碎裂的框架"），统一处理稳定与非稳定、连通与非连通的情形，并以此重新审视望远镜猜想及其在色同伦理论中的核心问题。

Method: 本文提出"原子碎裂框架"作为核心工具：将Balmer谱推广到任意可表现对称幺半∞-范畴V。通过利用对偶（dualizable）与刚性（rigid）∞-范畴，建立碎裂理想（smashing ideals）与局部刚性局部化（locally rigid localizations）之间的双射对应，并在（预）稳定情形下证明recollement定理。在稳定紧致刚性生成情形下给出原子细化。在色同伦应用方面，考虑自然投影诱导的碎裂框架嵌入；在不稳定情形，用碎裂域（smashing fields）和Pierce型条件刻画∞-topoi与连通模范畴上的望远镜猜想。最后引入Serre碎裂框架作为中间结构层。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 建立了碎裂理想与局部刚性局部化的对应关系及相应的recollement定理；(2) 证明Sp的碎裂框架嵌入∏_p,n Sp_{T(n)}之积（对所有素数和高度），将Sp框架的空间性完全约化为各单色层Sp_{T(n)}的空间性；(3) 在不稳定情形下，分别以碎裂域和Pierce型条件刻画∞-topoi、连通模范畴及超完备连通层上的望远镜猜想；(4) 定义Serre碎裂框架，证明它介于原子碎裂框架与通常碎裂框架之间，为连通R-模范畴上的望远镜猜想研究提供了中间结构层次。

Conclusion: 本文通过原子碎裂框架提供了一个统一的、涵盖稳定与非稳定情形的理论工具，将色同伦理论中望远镜猜想的空间性问题精确地约化到单色层上，并在多个不同设定下给出了望远镜猜想的等价刻画，为后续研究奠定了新的框架基础。

Abstract: We introduce the atomic smashing frame, extending the Balmer spectrum from tensor-triangular geometry to an arbitrary presentably symmetric monoidal $\infty$-category $\mathcal{V}$. This yields a formulation of the telescope conjecture for $\mathcal{V}$ and recovers the classical Balmer spectrum in the stable compactly-rigidly generated case.
  Exploiting dualizable and rigid $\infty$-categories, we establish a correspondence between smashing ideals and locally rigid localizations. This leads to a recollement theorem for smashing frames in the (pre)stable setting, together with an atomic refinement in the stable compactly-rigidly generated case. As a major application to chromatic homotopy theory, we show that the natural projections induce an embedding of the smashing frame of $\mathrm{Sp}$ into the product of the smashing frames of the monochromatic layers $\mathrm{Sp}_{T(n)}$, over all primes and heights. In particular, the spatiality of the smashing frame of $\mathrm{Sp}$ reduces entirely to that of $\mathrm{Sp}_{T(n)}$.
  In the unstable setting, we characterize the telescope conjecture for $\infty$-topoi in terms of smashing fields, and for connective module categories and hypercomplete connective sheaves in terms of Pierce-type conditions. Finally, we introduce the Serre smashing frame. Over a connective $\mathbb{E}_\infty$-ring $R$, this frame sits between the atomic and usual smashing frames, providing an intermediate structural layer in the study of the telescope conjecture for the connective $R$-module category.

</details>

*Ayako Carter,Benjamin C. Ward*

Motivation: 该研究的动机有二：一是深化对配置空间（楔形圆圈族）紧支撑上同调结构的理解，特别是其多项式函子模型的系数刻画；二是建立这些系数与图同调之间的形式联系，从而利用配置空间的计算工具来研究图复形和模空间M_{2,n}的上同调结构。

Result: 成功识别了Φ[n,m]的系数并建立了其与图同调的联系；给出了Payne-Willwacher属2标记图复形的简单FA-模显式分解；将gr_11H_c^*(M_{2,n})描述为装饰树的复形上同调；证明了gr_11H_c^{n+1}(M_{2,n})=0这一消没结果。

Conclusion: 论文建立了多项式函子系数、图同调与模空间上同调之间的一座桥梁，通过cobar构造和FA-模分解给出模空间M_{2,n}在特定分次下的计算工具和消没结果，展示了配置空间上同调与图同调理论的深刻联系。

Abstract: Work of Gadish and Hainaut (after Petersen) models the compactly supported cohomology of a wedge of circles as a polynomial functor. We identify the coefficients of this functor, $Φ[n,m]$, via a cobar construction of $\mathbf{FA}$-modules. This identification formally implies that these coefficients will arise in computations of graph homology, and we use this result to give examples of graph complexes whose homology may be embedded in $H_c^\ast(F(S^1\vee S^1,n))$.
  This includes the Payne-Willwacher marked graph complex in genus 2, for which we give a new, explicit decomposition in terms of simple $\mathbf{FA}$-modules. This allows us to describe $\mathsf{gr}_{11}H_c^{\ast}(\mathcal{M}_{2,n})$ as the cohomology of a complex of decorated trees and to show, for example, $\mathsf{gr}_{11}H_c^{n+1}(\mathcal{M}_{2,n})=0$.

</details>
