Conclusion: 该文为辫子簇上的量子簇代数建立了完整的显式幺半范畴分类理论，揭示了量子簇幺单项式、实单模与全局基元素之间的深层对应，同时证明了该量子坐标环是坐标环的平坦量子形变，为量子群的表示论与簇代数理论的交叉提供了系统性框架。

Abstract: Let \(G\) be a simple and simply connected algebraic group of simply-laced type. For each positive braid word \(β\), and for the complete strong duality datum attached to a \(Q\)-datum, we construct an explicit based monoidal categorification of the quantum cluster algebra of the braid variety \(X(β)\). We identify this algebra with both a localized quantum Grothendieck ring and a localized level-\(\geq1\) subalgebra of the bosonic extension algebra. Under these identifications, quantum cluster monomials correspond simultaneously to real simple modules and normalized global basis elements. We construct the specialization homomorphism at \(q^{1/2}=1\) and prove that it recovers \(\CC[X(β)]\); in particular, the resulting integral form is a flat quantum deformation. We also give intrinsic Lusztig parameters for cluster variables attached to double strings, identify the corresponding quantum grid minors, and establish generalized quantum \(T\)-systems.

</details>


### [100] [Positive kernels in Lie group representations and Fourier interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03167)
*Peter Vang Uttenthal*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文针对非紧致黎曼对称空间G/K上的离散子空间Γ上的给定函数f，构造了解析、非紧支撑的函数W，使得W在Γ上的限制等于f，并且W还满足一个不变微分方程。核心方法是将W视为希尔伯特空间中某个李群G表示的相干态空间上的正交投影，其中该希尔伯特空间具有严格正定再生核。该技术被应用于G/K上的热核，W以某些线性无关相干态的闭式公式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机是为非紧致黎曼对称空间上离散子空间上的函数提供满足额外约束（不变微分方程）的解析延拓或插值构造，这在调和分析、群表示论及相关应用中具有重要价值。

Method: 将延拓问题转化为相干态空间上的正交投影问题。具体而言，利用李群G在具有严格正定再生核的希尔伯特空间中的表示，将函数W视为在该表示空间中相干态张成子空间上的正交投影。对于热核的具体情形，通过线性无关相干态的闭式公式来显式表达W。

Result: 成功构造了满足W|_Γ=f且满足不变微分方程的解析非紧支撑函数W，并在热核情形下给出了W的显式闭式表达形式。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了从离散数据构造满足不变微分方程的解析函数的一般框架，将问题归结为群表示论中相干态与再生核希尔伯特空间的正交投影技术，为对称空间上的插值/延拓问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: For a given function $f$ on a discrete subspace $Γ$ of a noncompact Riemannian symmetric space $G/K$, we construct analytic, noncompactly supported functions $W$ on $G/K$ with $W|_Γ=f$ that, in addition, satisfy an invariant differential equation on $G/K$. The key step is to regard $W$ as an orthogonal projection onto a space of coherent states in a representation of the Lie group $G$ in a Hilbert space that admits a strictly positive definite reproducing kernel. The techniques are implemented for the heat kernel on $G/K$, and $W$ is expressed in terms of certain closed formulas for linearly independent coherent states.

</details>


### [101] [What is Smoothness?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03246)
*Zachary P Bradshaw*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文提出在非阿贝尔群上通过Cayley图Laplacian对不可约表示进行排序，从而定义L²(G)中函数的"光滑性"概念。光滑性不再是函数自身的固有属性，而是取决于函数与群及生成集的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实数线上，函数的光滑性可通过Fourier变换的衰减来体现。将此想法推广到任意群L²(G)时，需要对不可约表示进行排序，但对非阿贝尔群而言不存在规范的排序。本文旨在寻找一种自然的排序方式，以此定义函数的光滑性。

Method: 给定对称生成集S，构造关联Cayley图的Laplacian算子，该算子在群的对偶上分块对角。通过每块中特征值的均值对不可约表示进行排序，产生一个仅依赖于(G,S)的排序函数ω。作者分析该构造的自由度：在满足自然公理的算子类中，诱导排序恰好是那些在平凡表示处为零且共轭对取值相同的实函数；进一步加入类权非负性和"均匀增量"元素的声明后，可将算子唯一确定为Cayley-Laplacian（差一个正倍数）。作者还将该理论推广到紧群和带有传递群作用的有限集。

Result: 排序函数ω有界于0和2之间，仅在平凡表示处取零，最大界2恰在Cayley图为二部图时达到。在自然公理约束下，诱导排序的完备刻画得到；从共轭类的反转轨道导出的排序构成一组基。在额外约束下，算子被唯一固定到Cayley-Laplacian。

Conclusion: 光滑性是函数与群及生成集三元组共同决定的性质，而非函数自身的固有属性。该框架不仅适用于有限群，也适用于紧群和带有传递群作用的有限集。

Abstract: Smoothness of a function on the real line is reflected in the decay of its Fourier transform, which suggests that smoothness of a function in $L^2(G)$ for a group $G$ should mean concentration of the Fourier coefficients at low frequency. Such a reading presupposes an ordering of the irreducible representations of $G$, but for non-abelian $G$, no ordering is canonical. Given a symmetric generating set $S$, the Laplacian of the associated Cayley graph is block diagonal over the dual, and we order the irreps by the mean of the eigenvalues in each block. This produces an ordering function $ω:\widehat{G}\to\mathbb{R}$ that depends only on the pair $(G,S)$. This function is bounded between zero and two, vanishing only at the trivial representation and achieving the upper bound exactly when the Cayley graph is bipartite. We then ask how much freedom the construction has. Within the class of operators satisfying natural axioms, the induced orderings are exactly the real functions on the dual vanishing at the trivial representation and agreeing on conjugate pairs, and the orderings coming from inversion orbits of conjugacy classes form a basis for them. We cut the freedom down further by requiring two additional inputs: nonnegativity of the class weights and a declaration of which group elements count as uniform incremental changes, which pins the operator to the Cayley-Laplacian up to positive scale. We observe that the construction persists for compact groups even though the Cayley graph does not, and we extend the theory to finite sets carrying a transitive group action, where the acting group selects which frequencies exist and the generating set orders them. The answer to the title question is therefore that smoothness is a property of a function together with a choice of group and generating set, not of the function alone.

</details>


### [102] [The Buckyball Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03292)
*George Henderson-Walshe*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 利用表示论（群论）在Hückel近似下计算Buckminsterfullerene（富勒烯C60）及其他对称共轭体系的π电子能级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称共轭体系（如Buckminsterfullerene）具有高度的对称性，直接计算其π电子能级在计算上较为复杂。作者希望利用表示论这一数学工具，通过分类体系的对称性来简化Hückel近似的计算。

Method: 采用表示论（representation theory）方法，利用体系的对称群对Hückel哈密顿量进行约化，从而系统地计算π电子能级。

Result: 成功计算了Buckminsterfullerene及其他对称共轭体系的π电子能级。

Conclusion: 表示论是处理高对称共轭分子π电子能级的有效数学工具，能够简化Hückel近似的求解过程。

Abstract: We use representation theory to calculate the $π$-electron energy levels of Buckminsterfullerene and other symmetrical conjugated systems according to the Hückel approximation.

</details>


### [103] [Singular equivalences of $n$-adjoint type and standard eventually homological isomorphisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03365)
*Yang Han,Xianqing Wang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文修改了"最终同调同构"的定义，使其更为自然且富有成效，并引入$n$-伴随型的奇异等价概念，将其视为不同层次的标准奇异等价。文章系统发展了$n\ge 2$时$n$-伴随型奇异等价与标准最终同调同构的理论，给出了基于$\otimes$-（余）完美双模复合体的刻画、通过反对称代数和张量积代数的构造、由代数同态与幂等元诱导的例子，并将其应用于简化有限维数猜想、Gorenstein对称性猜想、Auslander-Reiten猜想和Gorenstein投射猜想等同调猜想，以及保持Gorenstein性、Fg条件、Happel性质和Han性质等同调性质。此外，作者证明标准最终同调同构对应于带有典范左伴随的全忠实标准三角函子（在奇点范畴之间），而本质满的标准最终同调同构（在导出范畴之间）对应于3-伴随型奇异等价，从而将前者纳入3-伴随型奇异等价的框架中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望改进"最终同调同构"的定义使其更加自然，并建立一种更便捷的工具——$n$-伴随型奇异等价——来简化各类同调猜想的证明和同调性质的传递，同时统一不同层次的标准奇异等价理论。

Method: 作者通过修改最终同调同构的定义，引入$n$-伴随型奇异等价的概念（$n\ge 2$），利用$\otimes$-（余）完美双模复合体对其进行刻画，通过反对称代数和张量积代数构造具体例子，并由代数同态和幂等元诱导实例，同时建立了这些概念与奇点范畴之间全忠实标准三角函子（带典范左伴随）之间的对应关系。

Result: 得到了$n$-伴随型奇异等价与标准最终同调同构的多种刻画；证明了标准最终同调同构与奇点范畴间带典范左伴随的全忠实函子之间的对应；证明本质满的标准最终同调同构对应于3-伴随型奇异等价，将其纳入该框架；并展示了对多个同调猜想的约简与多种同调性质的保持。

Conclusion: $n$-伴随型奇异等价为标准奇异等价的推广和分层次统一，为同调猜想的约简提供更有效的工具；标准最终同调同构与3-伴随型奇异等价之间的桥梁关系揭示了这两类概念的内在联系，拓展了奇异等价理论在导出范畴和奇点范畴之间的应用。

Abstract: We modify the definition of eventually homological isomorphisms so that it becomes more natural and fruitful. We introduce singular equivalences of $n$-adjoint type which can be viewed as standard singular equivalences of different levels and are more convenient for reducing various homological conjectures and transferring homological properties. We develop the theories of singular equivalences of $n$-adjoint type for $n\ge 2$ and standard eventually homological isomorphisms between derived categories by giving their characterizations in terms of $\otimes$-(co)perfect complexes of bimodules, two constructions via opposite algebras and tensor product algebras, examples induced by homomorphisms of algebras and idempotents, and applications to the reduction of homological conjectures such as the finitistic dimension conjecture, Gorenstein symmetry conjecture, Auslander-Reiten conjecture and Gorenstein projective conjecture, as well as to the preservation of homological properties such as Gorensteinness, the Fg condition, Happel's property and Han's property. Moreover, we show that standard eventually homological isomorphisms correspond to fully faithful standard triangle functors between singularity categories with canonical left adjoint, and essentially surjective standard eventually homological isomorphisms between derived categories correspond to singular equivalences of 3-adjoint type, thereby incorporating essentially surjective standard eventually homological isomorphisms into the framework of singular equivalences of 3-adjoint type.

</details>


### [104] [Exceptional model structures and the induced extriangulated categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03524)
*Chencheng Zhang,Pu Zhang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Hovey三元组中的"例外"情形。作者证明了在弱幂等完备的extriangulated范畴中，Hovey三元组是例外的当且仅当由Nakaoka-Palu定理诱导的extriangulated结构不是"典范三角化"的。该结构与三角化产生的中介extriangulated结构与某个函子范畴的Serre子范畴一一对应。文章还以自入射Nakayama代数为例，给出了其模范畴存在例外Hovey三元组的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hovey三元组在三角范畴和同伦论中具有重要地位，其中遗传（hereditary）的情形已被充分研究。作者注意到存在一类"例外"Hovey三元组，其(C∩F, C∩F∩W)不构成Frobenius对，这类三元组广泛出现但先前未被系统研究。作者旨在揭示例外Hovey三元组特有的结构性质及其与三角化结构之间的关系。

Method: 本文将Hovey三元组划分为三类：遗传、例外、以及非遗传且非例外的。利用Nakaoka-Palu定理获得商范畴(C∩F)/(C∩F∩W)上的三角化结构。作者在弱幂等完备的extriangulated范畴框架下，建立了例外性与诱导extriangulated结构是否典范三角化之间的等价刻画。此外，通过考虑中间extriangulated结构，将它们与函子范畴fp(...,Ab)的Serre子范畴建立一一对应，并使用自入射Nakayama代数kN型（模kC_n/J^t）作为具体例证。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 对自入射Nakayama代数kC_n/J^t，其模范畴存在例外Hovey三元组当且仅当gcd(n,t)≥2且t≥3；(2) 在弱幂等完备extriangulated范畴中，Hovey三元组是例外的当且仅当诱导的extriangulated结构(C∩F)/(C∩F∩W), Ē, s̄不是典范三角化的；(3) 该extriangulated结构与三角化结构之间的中介extriangulated结构与fp((P(C∩F)/(C∩F∩W))^op, Ab)的Serre子范畴一一对应。

Conclusion: 本文系统地引入并研究了例外Hovey三元组的概念，给出了其等价的范畴论刻画，揭示了例外情形下商范畴上extriangulated结构与三角化结构之间的丰富中间层结构，并给出了自入射Nakayama代数上具体的存在性判定条件，表明例外Hovey三元组的同伦范畴展现出新的现象。

Abstract: A Hovey triple $(\mathcal{C}, \mathcal{F}, \mathcal{W})$ is {\it exceptional}, if $(\mathcal{C} \cap \mathcal{F}, \ \mathcal{C} \cap \mathcal{F} \cap \mathcal{W})$ is not a Frobenius pair. One has a disjoint union $\{\text{Hovey triple}\} = \{\text{Hereditary Hovey triple}\} \ \dot\bigcup \ \{\text{Exceptional Hovey triple}\}$ \ $\dot\bigcup \ \{\text{non-hereditary and non-exceptional Hovey triple}\}.$ Exceptional Hovey triples appear widely. For a selfinjective Nakayama algebra $A= kC_n/J^t$, $A\mbox{-}{\rm mod}$ admits exceptional Hovey triples if and only if $\gcd (n, t) \ge 2$ and $t \geq 3$. Their homotopy categories reveal new phenomena. Nakaoka-Palu's Theorem implies that there is a triangulation on $\frac{\mathcal C\cap\mathcal F}{\mathcal C\cap\mathcal F\cap\mathcal W}.$ It is proved that a Hovey triple in a weakly idempotent complete extriangulated category $(\mathcal A, \mathbb E, \mathfrak s)$ is exceptional if and only if the induced extriangulated structure $\bigl(\frac{\mathcal C\cap\mathcal F}{\mathcal C\cap\mathcal F\cap\mathcal W}, \ \overline{\mathbb E}, \ \overline{\mathfrak s}\bigr)$ is not {\it canonically triangulated}. Between this extriangulated structure and the one arising from the triangulation, the intermediate extriangulated structures are in one-to-one correspondence with Serre subcategories of $\mathrm{fp}((\frac{\mathcal P(\mathcal C\cap\mathcal F)}{\mathcal C\cap\mathcal F\cap\mathcal W})^{\mathrm{op}}, \mathrm{Ab})$.

</details>


### [105] [Jantzen filtrations for graded affine Hecke algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03651)
*Dan Ciubotaru*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了几何分次仿射Hecke代数（等参数、实中心特征、主控形变方向）标准模的Jantzen猜想：Jantzen层半单，重数由对应轨道闭包的完全局部交点上同调给出。证明通过将代数标准-反共轭映射识别为收缩横截切片上的余茎到茎映射，利用Hard Lefschetz定理确定初等因子，并通过专用卷积代数的剩余分次证明半单性。文中还给出type A的例子说明主控条件的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jantzen猜想是表示论中的核心问题，涉及标准模的过滤结构与其几何对应物之间的关系。此前在仿射Hecke代数框架下，该猜想仅在受限条件下成立或方法不完整，本文旨在给出完整证明并阐明主控（dominance）条件在证明中的关键作用。

Method: 证明策略分为三步：(1) 将代数侧定义的标准-反共轭映射（standard-to-contragredient map）与几何侧的收缩横截切片上的余茎到茎映射（costalk-to-stalk map）建立识别；(2) 利用Hard Lefschetz定理分析该映射的初等因子，从而获得Jantzen层的重数公式（即完全局部交点上同调重数）；(3) 通过对专用卷积代数上的剩余分次结构分析，证明Jantzen层的半单性。此外，通过type A的反例展示主控方向的不可替代性。

Result: 成功证明了几何分次仿射Hecke代数标准模的Jantzen猜想（等参数、实中心特征、主控形变方向）。Jantzen层被证明是半单的，其重数恰为对应轨道闭包的完全局部交点上同调。type A中的例子表明，若不满足主控方向条件，定理结论可能失效。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了代数表示论（Jantzen过滤）与几何拓扑（交点上同调、Hard Lefschetz）之间更深层的联系，完成了几何分次仿射Hecke代数框架下Jantzen猜想的证明，同时揭示了主控条件是证明中不可或缺的技术前提。

Abstract: We prove the Jantzen conjecture for standard modules of graded affine Hecke algebras with equal parameters, real central character in the dominant deformation direction. The Jantzen layers are shown to be semisimple, and the multiplicities are given by the full local intersection cohomology of the corresponding orbit closures. The proof identifies the algebraic standard-to-contragredient map with the costalk-to-stalk map on a contracting transverse slice. Hard Lefschetz theorem determines its elementary divisors, and a residual grading on the specialized convolution algebra proves semisimplicity. An example in type $A$ is included to show that dominance is necessary.

</details>


### [106] [Enumerating cores of charged multipartitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03738)
*Thomas Gerber,Emily Norton*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对带电多重划分（charged multipartitions）的各种e-核心变体的存在性正性结果，将Granville和Ono关于e-core划分的经典定理推广到更广泛的组合对象上，这些对象与有限典型群的块理论、本原spetses及分圆Hecke代数相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Granville和Ono证明了存在n的e-core划分当且仅当e≥4，该定理等价于对称群在正特征下defect 0块的存在性以及有限一般线性群在正非定义特征下defect 0单本原块的陈述。作者希望将此结果推广到有限典型群的块理论、本原spetses和分圆Hecke代数的类似应用中，因此需要研究带电多重划分的不同e-核心变体。

Method: 本文采用组合方法，对带电多重划分的不同e-核心变体建立正性存在性命题（positivity statements），即证明对每个正整数n，相应的e-核心变体必存在。这是对Granville-Ono经典结果的系统性推广，覆盖了更广义的划分类型。

Result: 作者成功证明了针对带电多重划分的各种e-核心变体的类似正性存在性定理，这些结果在组合层面刻画了相关块理论问题中defect 0对象的存在性条件。

Conclusion: 本文的定理为有限典型群、本原spetses和分圆Hecke代数的块理论提供了组合基础，是Granville-Ono定理在带电多重划分框架下的自然推广，具有重要的表示论应用价值。

Abstract: Granville and Ono proved that there is an $e$-core partition of $n$ for every $n\in\mathbb{N}$ if and only if $e\geq 4$, which translates to a statement about the existence of defect $0$ blocks for symmetric groups in positive characteristic and defect $0$ unipotent blocks of finite general linear groups in positive, non-defining characteristic. Motivated by analogous applications to the block theory of finite classical groups, imprimitive spetses, and cyclotomic Hecke algebras, we prove similar positivity statements about different variants of $e$-cores for charged multipartitions.

</details>


### [107] [Universal Plücker positivity and the Octopus inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03746)
*Haoran Zhu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了Karp和Purbhoo提出的普适Plücker坐标在恰好一个参数为负时的正性定理，其阈值与最大的Plancherel上跃迁概率相关。在临界特殊化下，归一化(2,2)坐标的正性等价于Octopus不等式（Aldous谱间隙猜想证明的关键工具）。通过将不等式与Schubert演算联系，回答了Caputo和Aldous提出的问题。对于具有不同实零点的Wronskian，确定了所有分支平衡坐标正半定的最大公共半线，并给出了Alon、Kozma和Puder两个超图不等式的Plücker理论证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Karp和Purbhoo此前提出了普适Plücker坐标的正性问题，本文旨在证明当恰好一个参数为负时的正性定理，并寻求与Aldous谱间隙猜想相关的Octopus不等式之间的联系。同时希望解答Caputo和Aldous提出的开放问题，以及利用Plücker理论为超图不等式提供新的证明视角。

Method: 本文采用组合与代数几何相结合的方法：通过Plancherel测度的上跃迁概率确定正性阈值；将(2,2)坐标的正性与Octopus不等式建立等价关系；利用Schubert演算（Schubert calculus）将不等式与格拉斯曼流形的相交理论联系起来；并对Wronskian的分支平衡坐标进行正半定性分析，以确定最大公共半线；最后运用Plücker理论给出超图不等式的证明。

Result: 成功证明了单参数为负情形下的普适Plücker坐标正性定理，得到了由最大Plancherel上跃迁概率决定的尖锐均匀阈值。证明了临界情形下归一化(2,2)坐标正性与Octopus不等式的等价性，并通过Schubert演算予以解释，从而回答了Caputo和Aldous的提问。确定了具有不同实零点的Wronskian对应所有分支平衡坐标正半定的最大公共半线，并用Plücker理论给出了Alon-Kozma-Puder超图不等式的两个新证明。

Conclusion: 本文建立的Plücker坐标正性定理连接了普适Plücker坐标理论、Aldous谱间隙猜想（通过Octopus不等式）与Schubert演算三个领域，为Caputo和Aldous提出的问题提供了答案。对Wronskian和超图不等式的处理进一步展示了Plücker理论在多个数学分支中的统一力量。

Abstract: We prove a positivity theorem for the universal Plücker coordinates of Karp and Purbhoo when exactly one parameter is negative, with a sharp uniform threshold governed by the largest Plancherel up-transition probability. At the critical specialisation, positivity of the normalised $(2,2)$-coordinate is equivalent to the Octopus inequality, the main technical tool in the proof of Aldous's spectral gap conjecture. By connecting the inequality with Schubert calculus, this answers questions raised by Caputo and Aldous. For a Wronskian with distinct real zeros, we determine the maximal common half-line on which all branching-balanced coordinates are positive semidefinite. We also give Plücker-theoretic proofs of two hypergraph inequalities of Alon, Kozma, and Puder.

</details>


### [108] [Additive diameters and covering complexity of irreducible representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03882)
*Urban Jezernik,Špela Špenko*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究紧致群G在不可约复表示V上作用时，子空间U的"群加法直径"（需要多少个U的平移才能张成整个V）。平凡下界为dim V / dim U，作者证明直径至多为⌈(dim V/dim U)·ln dim V⌉，即平凡界差一个对数因子。通过覆盖复杂度C(V)（直径与平凡下界的最大比值）刻画各类表示：SL_2的不可约表示C(V)=1；SL_n的对称幂和外幂族及有限Heisenberg群、2-传递群等可真正达到对数级；SL_n共轭表示和SL_3的Sym^k C^3上复杂度有常数上界；而任何固定连通约化群都存在复杂度至少趋向旗簇维数的一族表示。此外，sl_3作用在Sym^k C^3上关于Sym^k X（X为平面）的单形式直径最优，而相应SL_3群直径并非最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于一个群在线性空间上的作用，自然关心子空间平均平移覆盖整个空间所需的最少平移数（即直径）。仅凭维数计数得到平凡下界dim V/dim U，问题在于该下界是否本质正确。这关系到表示论、几何不变量理论中的覆盖与生成问题。作者希望严谨刻画这个直径与维度下界之间的偏差（即覆盖复杂度），并定位各类表示在该偏差范围中的位置，从而揭示群的代数/几何结构如何影响覆盖效率。

Method: 作者先证明了通用上界：对紧致群G的不可约表示V，任意非零子空间U的直径≤⌈(dim V/dim U)·ln dim V⌉。据此定义覆盖复杂度C(V)（介于1和2+ln dim V之间）用以度量与平凡下界的最大偏差，然后通过对不同类别的表示群（SL_2、SL_n的对称/外幂、有限Heisenberg群、PSL_2(F_p)等2-传递群、SL_n共轭表示、SL_3的Sym^k C^3、以及一般连通约化群的若干族表示）逐一分析或构造反例来确定C(V)所处位置。同时对sl_3作用Sym^k C^3的情形，分别从单形式（单项式基）直径与Lie群SL_3直径两个角度比较最优性。

Result: 主要结果为：直径上界为平凡下界的ln dim V倍以内，且1≤C(V)≤2+ln dim V。具体地：SL_2(C)的所有不可约表示满足C(V)=1；随着n变化，SL_n(C)的对称幂与外幂表示族中对数因子真实出现，有限Heisenberg群与PSL_2(F_p)等2-传递群也是如此；SL_n共轭表示与SL_3的Sym^k C^3上C(V)有常数上界；而任意固定连通约化群均存在一族表示使得C(V)趋于至少旗簇维数的下界。sl_3作用Sym^k C^3时，单形式直径对Sym^k X达到最优，而SL_3群直径不然。

Conclusion: 文章精确刻画了群作用下子空间（群加法）直径与维数平凡下界之间的差距，证明了差距至多是对数级别，否定了只能线性增大的可能；但通过大量例子说明该对数因子在多种自然表示族中确实不可消除（SL_n对称/外幂族、Heisenberg群、2-传递群等），也在SL_3的Sym^k C^3及SL_n共轭表示中可被压缩为常数。这揭示了覆盖复杂度从根本上依赖表示的结构与群的约化类型，且对无界维数的固定约化群，存在一族表示的复杂度下界由旗簇维数给出，暗示几何不变量理论的深层联系。

Abstract: Let a group $G$ act linearly on a finite-dimensional complex vector space $V$. The group-additive diameter of a subspace $U \leq V$ is the least number of translates of $U$ whose sum is all of $V$. Counting dimensions, it is at least $\dim V / \dim U$. We show that when $G$ is compact and $V$ is irreducible, the diameter of every nonzero subspace is at most $\lceil (\dim V / \dim U) \ln \dim V \rceil$, so the trivial bound is correct up to a logarithmic factor. We measure the discrepancy by the covering complexity $\mathsf{C}(V)$, the largest ratio between the diameter of any subspace and its trivial lower bound, so that $1 \leq \mathsf{C}(V) \leq 2 + \ln \dim V$, and we determine where in this range various representations lie. Every irreducible representation of $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbf{C})$ has $\mathsf{C}(V) = 1$. The logarithm can be genuinely present along families of symmetric and exterior powers of $\mathrm{SL}_n(\mathbf{C})$ with $n$ varying, and it is present for finite Heisenberg groups and $2$-transitive groups of small order such as $\mathrm{PSL}_2(\mathbf{F}_p)$. The complexity is bounded above by a constant on the conjugation representations of $\mathrm{SL}_n(\mathbf{C})$ and on the representations $\operatorname{Sym}^k \mathbf{C}^3$ of $\mathrm{SL}_3(\mathbf{C})$. On the other hand, every fixed connected reductive group has a family of irreducible representations whose complexities tend to at least the dimension of the flag variety. Finally, for the Lie algebra $\mathfrak{sl}_3(\mathbf{C})$ acting on $\operatorname{Sym}^k \mathbf{C}^3$, the monomial diameter with respect to $\operatorname{Sym}^k X$ for a plane $X \leq \mathbf{C}^3$ is optimal, while the corresponding $\mathrm{SL}_3(\mathbf{C})$ diameter is not.

</details>


### [109] [On the new bases attached to Weyl groups of exceptional type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04089)
*G. Lusztig*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文定义了例外Weyl群中单冪表示的Grothendieck群的新基，对先前论文中的结果进行了修正和精确化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于例外Weyl群中单冪表示Grothendieck群基的研究存在不精确甚至错误之处，需要加以修正并给出更清晰的定义。

Method: 在例外Weyl群中重新定义单冪表示的Grothendieck群的新基，对已有的方法进行改进和纠正。

Result: 给出了例外Weyl群中单冪表示Grothendieck群的一个新的、更精确的基，并修正了此前论文中的错误。

Conclusion: 新基的定义使得例外Weyl群中单冪表示的结构描述更加精确，同时更正了过往研究中的不足。

Abstract: We define the new basis of the Grothendieck group of unipotent representations in an exceptional Weyl group, making more precise and correcting results in previous papers.

</details>


### [110] [Standard Representations of Crystallographic Coxeter Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04179)
*Emma Booth*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究Cartan表示（将某些Coxeter群表示为Kac-Moody代数的Weyl群），给出了这些表示彼此同构、与Coxeter群的反射表示同构以及与它们的对偶表示同构的条件，并在有限特征域上给出部分结果，同时澄清了Coxeter群为晶体的含义及Kac-Moody代数Weyl群成为晶体群的充分条件，并提供示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本研究的动机在于深入理解Cartan表示的结构与分类问题，特别是阐明Coxeter群作为Kac-Moody代数Weyl群的表示何时同构于反射表示或其对偶，并澄清"晶体（crystallographic）"这一概念在Coxeter群及Kac-Moody代数Weyl群语境下的精确含义。

Method: 本文采用表示论与群论的理论研究方法，系统考察Cartan表示之间的同构关系以及它们与Coxeter群反射表示的关系，在特征零域上给出完全条件，在有限特征域上给出部分结果，并通过构造示例加以说明。

Result: 论文给出了Cartan表示间同构及与反射表示、其对偶表示同构的判定条件，完成了特征零情形的定理证明，在有限特征域上获得部分结果，同时为晶体的Coxeter群及Kac-Moody代数的晶体Weyl群给出了精确条件和判定依据。

Conclusion: 本文深化了对Coxeter群与Kac-Moody代数之间关系的理解，明确了Cartan表示的同构分类，并为晶体化条件提供了系统性的理论框架。

Abstract: This dissertation studies Cartan representations: representations of certain Coxeter groups as the Weyl groups of Kac-Moody algebras. It sets out and proves conditions for these representations to be isomorphic to each other, the reflection representation of the Coxeter group, and their dual representations, and gives partial results for the same over fields of finite characteristic, as well as clarifying what it means for a Coxeter group to be crystallographic and giving conditions for the Weyl group of a Kac-Moody algebra to be such. Some illustrative examples are also provided.

</details>

### [111] [Unlocking the QPE Mystery: Star-Disk Collisions in Realistic AGN Disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03011)
*Zhaohuan Zhu,Xiaoshan Huang,Yan-Fei Jiang,Shunquan Huang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过二维多频辐射流体动力学模拟，研究了恒星与活动星系核吸积盘碰撞产生准周期爆发（QPEs）的机制。采用更真实的圆形核盘结构后，发现增厚的盘大气导致弓形激波在不同时间破碎，产生的X射线耀斑时标约为轨道时标的10%，并可解释亮度和周期趋势，表明更真实的盘结构能解释多个观测特征，反之QPEs也能约束盘结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QPEs是低质量星系核中观测到的准周期性软X射线爆发，展现出两个显著趋势：爆发持续时间约为重复时标的10-20%，且更长的爆发更亮。现有模型（恒星与超大质量黑洞周围吸积盘的碰撞）能解释准周期性，但无法阐明如何重现这些观测趋势，亟需数值模拟验证。

Method: 开展二维多频辐射流体动力学（RH）模拟恒星-吸积盘碰撞过程。关键改进在于采用比以往更真实的圆形核盘结构，该结构来自次爱丁顿吸积盘的辐射磁流体力学（Radiation MHD）模拟结果，而非之前的简化盘模型。

Result: 研究发现：1）厚实的膨胀盘大气（延伸至z/r~1）使弓形激波的不同部分在不同时间突破（在z/r~0.7的突破面），产生延长的热辐射；2）X射线耀斑持续时标由激波穿过光学厚盘的传播时间决定，约为轨道时标的10%，重现了观测的占空比；3）更倾斜的恒星-盘相互作用产生更长更亮的耀斑；4）真实AGN盘的面密度Σ∝r²给出碰撞能量E∝P^(2/3)，可解释光度-周期趋势，尤其对较弱的QPEs。

Conclusion: 作者提出，更真实的圆形核盘结构可以解释多个QPE观测趋势，反过来QPEs也可用作约束吸积盘结构的探针。

Abstract: Quasi-periodic eruptions (QPEs) are luminous, recurring soft X-ray outbursts observed in the nuclei of low-mass galaxies. They display two remarkable trends: outburst durations are $\sim$10-20% of the recurrence timescale, and longer bursts are more luminous. A promising theory that naturally explains the quasi-periodicity invokes collisions between a star on an extreme mass-ratio inspiral (EMRI) orbit and the accretion disk around the supermassive black hole. However, it remains unclear how this model reproduces the observed trends. We therefore carry out two-dimensional, multi-frequency radiation hydrodynamic (RH) simulations of star--disk collisions. Crucially, we adopt a more realistic circumnuclear disk structure from previous Radiation MHD simulations of sub-Eddington accretion disks. We find that the thick, puffed-up disk atmosphere, extending to $z/r\sim1$, causes different portions of the bow shock to break out at different times, producing prolonged thermal emission as the shock emerges through the breakout surface at $z/r\sim0.7$. The X-ray flare duration is set by the shock propagation time through the optically thick disk--a $\sim$10% of the orbital timescale, reproducing the observed duty cycle. A more oblique star-disk interaction yields a longer, more luminous flare. The realistic AGN disk models also exhibit a surface density $Σ\propto r^2$, giving a collisional energy $E\propto P^{2/3}$ that may explain the luminosity--period trend, especially for the weaker QPEs. Overall, we suggest that a more realistic circumnuclear disk structure can explain several observed QPE trends-and QPEs may, in turn, constrain the disk structure.

</details>


### [112] [Multi-epoch Detection of an Ultra-fast Inflow in ESP 39607: Evidence for an Accretion Cascade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03019)
*Alessandro Peca,Michael J. Koss,Roberto Serafinelli,C. Megan Urry,Claudio Ricci,Keigo Fukumura,Massimo Gaspari,Elias Kammoun,Alessia Tortosa,Giulia Cerini,Peter G. Boorman,Richard Mushotzky,Quirino D'Amato*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文报告了对Seyfert 2星系ESP 39607（z=0.201）的XRISM、XMM-Newton和NuSTAR联合观测，在~4.7和4.9 keV处探测到红移的Fe XXV/Fe XXVI吸收线特征，表明存在以~0.16c速度内流的超快内流（UFI）。结合此前两期NuSTAR数据，该UFI在2.2年（源静止系）基线上的综合探测显著性达5.3σ，推断其为由吸积"级联"过程持续补给的入流，而非单一长寿云团，质量内流率约为吸积率的0.2-1.0倍，可为活动星系核提供可观的燃料供应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超快内流（UFI）作为活动星系核（AGN）吸积与反馈过程中的重要环节，其存在性、物理性质和长期稳定性对于理解AGN能量供给机制至关重要。此前对该源已有两期NuSTAR探测到类似特征，本文旨在通过多波段、高分辨率联合观测确认该内流的真实性和物理性质，并探究其在多年时间尺度上的持续性及其对吸积的贡献。

Method: 利用XRISM/Resolve的高分辨率光谱识别吸收特征，并结合XMM-Newton和NuSTAR进行宽能段覆盖；采用不同连续谱模型和多种拟合方法交叉验证探测显著性；通过光致电离建模（photoionization modeling）约束吸收体的电离参数（log ξ≈3.7-3.8）和柱密度（log N_H≈23.2-23.8）；结合三期观测数据（含前两期NuSTAR）进行综合显著性分析，并由动力学时间和质量内流率约束推断内流的补给机制。

Result: 在4.7和4.9 keV（观测系）探测到红移的Fe XXV He-α/Fe XXVI Ly-α吸收特征，对应v_in≈0.16c的UFI，单次观测探测显著性~3-3.7σ；光致电离建模给出log ξ≈3.7-3.8，log N_H≈23.2-23.8；三期数据综合显著性达5.3σ，基线≥2.2年（源静止系）；推断UFI位于R≈49-77 R_g处，动力学时间仅数天，因此需要持续补给形成吸积"级联"；质量内流率Ṁ_in≈0.3-1.4 M⊙/yr，Ṁ_in/Ṁ_acc≈0.2-1.0。

Conclusion: 该研究首次通过XRISM等高分辨率X射线观测确证了ESP 39607中持续存在的超快内流，其在长达2.2年以上的时间尺度上稳定存在，不支持单一长寿云团模型，转而支持由吸积"级联"机制持续补给内流物质的图景。该内流可为中央AGN提供其所需吸积物质的20%-100%，表明UFI可能成为AGN的重要供能渠道，对理解超大质量黑洞增长与AGN反馈过程具有重要意义。

Abstract: We present simultaneous XRISM, XMM-Newton, and NuSTAR observations of ESP 39607, a Seyfert 2 galaxy at $z = 0.201$. XRISM/Resolve reveals two absorption features near 4.7 and 4.9 keV in the observed frame, consistent with redshifted Fe XXV He-$α$/Fe XXVI Ly-$α$ absorption from gas inflowing at $v_{\rm in} \simeq 0.16c$. The high velocity identifies the absorber as an ultra-fast inflow (UFI), which is detected at $\sim 3$-$3.7σ$ across different methods and continuum models. Photoionization modeling yields $\log ξ/{\rm erg\,s^{-1}\,cm} \simeq 3.7$-$3.8$ and a column density $\log N_{\rm H,abs}/{\rm cm^{-2}} \simeq 23.2$-$23.8$, the latter depending on the assumed metallicity. The inflow velocity and line properties are consistent with those reported from two earlier NuSTAR epochs, indicating that similar inflowing material was present over a baseline of at least 2.2 yr in the source rest frame. Across all three epochs, the combined detection significance of the UFI is $5.3σ$. Given the dynamical timescale of a few days at the inferred radius, $R \simeq 49$-$77\,R_{\rm g}$, the multi-year evidence favors a scenario in which the inflow is continuously replenished, forming an accretion "cascade", rather than a single long-lived cloud. With an estimated mass inflow rate of $\dot{M}_{\rm in} \simeq 0.3$-$1.4\,M_\odot$ yr$^{-1}$, depending on metallicity, and a ratio $\dot{M}_{\rm in}/\dot{M}_{\rm acc} \simeq 0.2$-$1.0$, the inflow could supply a substantial fraction of the accretion needed to power the central active galactic nucleus.

</details>


### [113] [Single-pulse reanalysis of the 2024 Vela glitch and new observations of PSR~J0437$-$4715 and PSR~J1644$-$4559](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03056)
*Carlos O. Lousto,Ruby Shrestha,Ezequiel Zubieta,Susana B. Araujo Furlan,Guillermo Gancio,Federico García,Santiago del Palacio,Ryan Missel,Linwei Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: PuMA合作组对Vela脉冲星2024年大glitch进行了单脉冲逐脉冲再分析（使用400 MHz带宽的ROACH后端），并将机器学习单脉冲流水线（Isolation Forest离群点剔除、β-VAE去噪、SOM聚类）推广到PSR J1644-4559和毫秒脉冲星PSR J0437-4715。Vela在glitch附近的八天数据中，4/6簇分解复现了窄带观测结论；包含glitch跳变及双指数恢复项后平均轮廓稳定在1%以内。PSR J0437-4715的聚类分解显示族间宽度较平均轮廓窄3.6倍，提示基于聚类的计时精度可提升约3.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vela脉冲星是南天glitch监测的关键目标，PuMA合作组旨在利用宽带后端和机器学习流水线，在高时间分辨率下系统研究glitch前后脉冲轮廓和单个脉冲行为的细微变化，同时将这些方法推广到其他脉冲星类型（含散射主导源和毫秒脉冲星），以检验聚类分解在计时精度提升上的普遍适用性。

Method: 采用脉冲逐脉冲（pulse-per-pulse）再分析方法，用400 MHz带宽的ROACH后端观测；数据处理流水线包括三层机器学习结构：Isolation Forest用于离群点剔除、β-变分自编码器（β-VAE）用于单脉冲去噪、自组织映射（SOM）用于脉冲簇分解。对Vela执行4簇和6簇分解并与先前窄带ETTUS接收机结果对比；对两类新目标执行类似聚类分析，并对PSR J0437-4715按峰值优势分保留前10%脉冲以增强信噪比。

Result: Vela方面：宽带数据以7倍带宽复现了此前窄带发现——高振幅簇在相位上更早达到峰值、更窄、更偏斜且数量更少。包含glitch跳变与双指数恢复项的计时解下，八天平均轮廓稳定在1%以内（宽度变化-0.5±0.9%）；若省略恢复项，折叠频率误差在10^-7量级即可造成高达55%的伪后glitch展宽效应。PSR J1644-4559的簇仅在振幅上有所不同，符合散射主导轮廓预期。PSR J0437-4715保留10%高峰值优势脉冲使S/N加倍，五簇分解得到按相位有序排列的窄簇组，宽度比平均轮廓窄3.6±0.4倍，提示基于聚类的计时精度约可提高3.5倍。

Conclusion: 工作证实了宽带后端配合机器学习流水线可在更高灵敏度下可靠复现并对新脉冲星类型扩展单脉冲聚类分析；正确纳入glitch恢复项对轮廓稳定性测量至关重要，否则会引入显著的伪展宽。SOM聚类对毫秒脉冲星PSR J0437-4715展现出有前景的计时精度增益（~3.5倍），但其相位稳定性和最终计时应用仍需后续论文中的时序验证予以确认。

Abstract: The Pulsar Monitoring in Argentina (PuMA) collaboration systematically monitors southern glitching pulsars, maintaining high-cadence single-pulse records of the Vela pulsar. We present a pulse-per-pulse reanalysis of the 2024 major glitch of Vela (PSR~J0835$-$4510) with the 400~MHz-bandwidth ROACH backend of the Argentine Institute of Radioastronomy, and extend our machine-learning single-pulse pipeline---Isolation Forest outlier rejection, $β$-Variational-AutoEncoder denoising, and Self-Organizing-Map clustering---to new observations of PSR~J1644$-$4559 and the millisecond pulsar PSR~J0437$-$4715. For Vela, the 4- and 6-cluster decompositions of the eight days bracketing the glitch reproduce, with seven times the previous bandwidth, the behavior found with the narrow-band ETTUS receivers: higher-amplitude clusters peak earlier in phase, are narrower, more skewed, and less populated. With the glitch jump and its two exponential recovery terms included in the timing solution, the mean profile is stable to 1\% across all eight days (width change $-0.5\pm0.9$\% from pre- to post-glitch); omitting the recovery terms would mimic a post-glitch broadening of up to 55\% through a folding-frequency error at the $10^{-7}$ level. The clusters of PSR~J1644$-$4559 differ almost exclusively in amplitude, as expected for a scattering-dominated profile. For PSR~J0437$-$4715, retaining the 10\% of pulses with the highest peak-dominance score doubles the signal-to-noise ratio, and a five-cluster decomposition yields narrow, phase-ordered groups a factor $3.6\pm0.4$ narrower than the average profile, suggesting a $\sim$3.5-fold improvement in cluster-based timing precision for this pulsar-timing-array target, to be confirmed in a follow-up paper.

</details>


### [114] [Thermal and non-thermal emission from supermassive black hole circumbinary disks: Disks, Coronae, Streams, and Cavities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03140)
*Chris Nagele,Julian H. Krolik,Brooks E. Kinch,Jeremy D. Schnittman,Scott Noble*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过对HARM3D模拟的双超大质量黑洞(SMBH)系统快照进行后处理，基于稳态辐射转移、热平衡和电离平衡自洽求解了不同黑洞质量下的辐射谱。研究发现约40%的观测光度以软X射线幂律形式辐射，且存在两种方位角变化模式：X射线连续谱因螺旋激波中的气体温度不对称性而约有10%的波动，Fe Kα等值宽度则因內盘视线方向被团块部分遮挡而有约25%的下降，两者同周期且相位差约π/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量模拟来理解双SMBH系统的热光度时间变化，但光谱预测相对稀少，且尚未有超越简单发射模型的预测。本文旨在填补这一空白，为多信使天体物理中的电磁信号搜索提供更可靠的光谱特征。

Method: 对20M间距、0.01爱丁顿吸积率的双SMBH系统HARM3D模拟快照进行后处理。对10^6、10^7和10^8太阳质量的黑洞，自洽地求解基于稳态辐射转移、热平衡与电离平衡的辐射谱，包含所有相关相对论效应及发射和吸收过程。

Result: 虽然大部分热光度由盘的热辐射承担，但双星四极矩排空的低密度区域产生了丰富的X射线，约40%的观测光度呈软X射线幂律(Gamma=2.3)。识别出两种方位角变化模式：X射线连续谱因盘内螺旋激波气体温度不对称性变化约10%；Fe Kα等值宽度在视线到内盘被团块部分遮挡时下降约25%。两者同周期且相位差约π/2，对典型AGN质量和20M间距而言周期为天到周量级。

Conclusion: 双SMBH系统产生的X射线连续谱和Fe Kα线变化具有可区分的特征时变模式，可作为脉冲星计时阵列和LISA引力波探测之外的重要电磁对应信号诊断工具，为多信使观测提供独特的谱学预测。

Abstract: The search for electromagnetic signals from supermassive black hole (SMBH) binary systems is one of the cornerstones of multi-messenger astrophysics, complementing gravitational wave observations of such systems by pulsar timing arrays and LISA. Although extensive simulations have been run to understand the time variability of the bolometric luminosity from accreting binary SMBH systems, comparatively few spectral predictions have been made, and none that go beyond simple emission models. In this paper, we post-process a \texttt{HARM3D} simulation snapshot of a binary at $20 M$ separation accreting at 0.01 Eddington. For black hole masses $10^6$, $10^7$, and $10^8\, M_\odot$, we self-consistently solve for the radiated spectrum on the basis of time-steady radiation transfer, thermal balance, and ionization equilibrium, including all relevant relativistic effects as well as emission and absorption processes. Although most of the bolometric luminosity is radiated thermally by the disk, the low density regions evacuated by the binary's quadrupole moment produce copious X-rays, with $\sim40\%$ of the observed luminosity in a soft X-ray power law ($Γ= 2.3$). We identify two modes of observed azimuthal variation. The X-ray continuum varies by $\sim10\%$ due to an underlying asymmetry in the gas temperature of spiral shocks in the disk. The Fe~K$α$ equivalent width dips by $\sim25\%$ when the line of sight to the inner disk is partially obscured by the lump. These two effects share the same period and are $\sim π/2$ out of phase; the period is order days to weeks for typical AGN masses and a 20$M$ separation.

</details>


### [115] [Observational selection effects on radio pulsars are minimal for masses, but significant for orbits and spins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03157)
*Lisa V. Drummond,Katerina Chatziioannou,Emmanuel Fonseca*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过模拟射电脉冲星双星系统的时序观测，量化了可探测性与可测量性两类观测选择效应，并将其纳入分层贝叶斯群体推断，同时提取脉冲星质量、伴星质量、轨道偏心率、轨道周期和自转周期的内在天体物理分布。结果显示选择效应对质量分布影响极小，但对轨道偏心率/圆轨道比例及轨道与自转周期分布有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射电脉冲星双星的质量分布（双峰结构、~2太阳质量截断）是理解致密物质状态方程与天体形成过程的关键。然而现有质量目录受观测选择效应影响，需要区分内在天体物理分布与观测导致的偏差，才能可靠解读质量分布及相关参数的物理含义。

Method: 作者通过模拟射电脉冲星系统及其时序观测，追踪一个系统最终进入观测数据集的全过程，评估双星和脉冲星参数对选择效应的影响。将选择函数折入分层群体推断（hierarchical population inference），联合推断脉冲星质量、伴星质量、轨道偏心率、轨道周期和自转周期的群体分布。

Result: 选择效应对质量分布影响很小，但对其他参数影响显著：圆轨道与偏心轨道系统的观测比例为1:1，经选择修正后的内在天体物理比例为2:1；真实群体相比观测范围偏向更长的轨道周期和自转周期。

Conclusion: 经选择效应修正后，银河系射电脉冲星质量分布的双峰结构及其~2太阳质量截断是内在可靠的；而其他群体参数（轨道形态比例、周期分布）需大幅修正才能反映真实天体物理分布，说明射电时序观测具有参数依赖的强选择偏差。

Abstract: The masses of Galactic pulsars in binaries measured through radio timing point to structure in the mass distribution with implications for dense matter and astrophysical formation processes: a bimodal shape with peaks at ${\sim} 1.3\,M_\odot$ and ${\sim} 1.6\,M_\odot$, with a cutoff above ${\sim}2\,M_\odot$. However, this observed population of radio pulsars is shaped not only by intrinsic pulsar properties, such as birth masses, binary evolutionary pathways and dense-matter constraints, but also by observational effects. In existing catalogs of pulsar mass measurements, observational selection effects influence (i) which systems are detected in the first place (detectability) and (ii) which systems yield well-measured masses (measurability). In this work, we revisit the radio pulsar population distribution in the context of observational effects associated with pulsar radio timing. Simulating systems of radio pulsars and timing observations, we track the process by which a given system ends up in an observational dataset and estimate the impact of the binary and pulsar parameters. We fold this selection function into hierarchical population inference and extract the intrinsic astrophysical distribution of pulsars observed with radio timing. We jointly infer the populations of pulsar mass, companion mass, orbital eccentricity, orbital period and pulsar spin period. Selection effects have minimal impact on the mass distributions, but more significantly affect other parameters. The ratio of circular-to-eccentric systems shifts from an observed $1\!:\!1$ to an astrophysical, selection-corrected $2\!:\!1$ ratio, while the true population is shifted toward longer orbital and spin periods than observed.

</details>


### [116] [Radiative Efficiency Enhancement by Electromagnetic Energy Dissipation in Strongly Magnetized Supercritical Accretion Flows around Kerr Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03250)
*Hiroyuki R. Takahashi,Ken Ohsuga,Ryohei Kobayashi,Akihiro Inoue,Yuta Asahina,Akira Nukada,Taisuke Boku*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文通过广义相对论辐射磁流体力学模拟，研究了黑洞自转与磁通量对超临界吸积流中电磁能耗散和辐射输运的影响。研究发现高自旋MAD模型中，Blandford--Znajek机制提取的电磁能部分在黑洞附近盘面耗散，显著提升辐射效率至0.60，并大幅缩小有效捕获半径至2.5r_g，表明磁通量积累应作为解释观测光度与谱的关键参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超临界吸积流中黑洞自转与磁通量如何影响电磁能耗散和辐射输运尚未被系统研究，理解这一机制对正确解释观测到的光度与谱至关重要。

Method: 采用广义相对论辐射磁流体力学（GRRMHD）模拟，对MAD（磁滞盘）和SANE（标准正常演化）两种吸积态，结合不同黑洞自转参数进行对比研究。

Result: 高自旋MAD模型中，BZ机制提取的电磁能量在黑洞邻近盘面耗散，贡献辐射能量并增强光度。时间平均辐射效率η_rad=0.60，远高于非自旋情形的0.088和SANE态的0.21；有效捕获半径r_trap=2.5r_g，接近ISCO半径，显著小于非自旋情形的12r_g和SANE态的8.5r_g。

Conclusion: 黑洞上积累的磁通量（MAD vs SANE状态）能显著影响超临界吸积流的辐射性质，在解释观测光度与谱时，除黑洞质量、自转和吸积率外，还应将磁通量作为重要参数纳入考量。

Abstract: We investigate how black hole spin and the amount of magnetic flux affect electromagnetic energy dissipation and radiation transport in supercritical accretion flows. For this purpose, we perform general relativistic radiation magnetohydrodynamic simulations of MAD and SANE accretion flows with different black hole spins. In the high-spin MAD model, we find that a fraction of the electromagnetic energy extracted by the Blandford--Znajek mechanism is dissipated near the disk surface in the vicinity of the black hole. This dissipation significantly contributes to the generation of radiative energy and enhances the luminosity. The time-averaged radiative efficiency reaches $η_{\rm rad}=0.60$, which is much larger than $0.088$ for the non-spinning black hole case and $0.21$ for the weak-magnetic-flux case, corresponding to the SANE state. As a result, the effective trapping radius, defined as the radius at which the outward radiative luminosity becomes equal to the inward radiative luminosity, is $r_{\rm trap}=2.5r_{\rm g}$, comparable to the ISCO radius. This value is significantly smaller than $12r_{\rm g}$ for the non-spinning case and $8.5r_{\rm g}$ for the SANE state. These results suggest that the amount of magnetic flux accumulated on the black hole can affect the radiative properties of supercritical accretion flows and should therefore be considered, in addition to black hole mass, spin, and mass accretion rate, when interpreting observed luminosities and spectra.

</details>


### [117] [The Jet Properties and Accretion Regime for the Blazar Sequence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03435)
*Qingchen Long*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本研究通过编译包含136个FSRQs、64个LBLs、39个IBLs和105个HBLs的耀变体样本，发现这些天体在α_ro-α_ox平面上遵循"∠"形演化序列。沿该序列，5 GHz射电多普勒因子、喷流功率和吸积爱丁顿比均递减。研究表明该分布主要由吸积率和跨波段的相对多普勒聚束效应（尤其δ_i/δ_j）调控，而喷流形状和谱指数起次要作用。FSRQs+LBLs是强喷流耦合辐射有效吸积的源，HBLs是弱喷流耦合辐射无效吸积的源，IBLs为过渡态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耀变体在宽波段谱指数（α_ro-α_ox）平面上的双峰分布现象仍是未解之谜，作者旨在探究其物理起源。

Method: 编译了一个包含136个FSRQs、64个LBLs、39个IBLs和105个HBLs的大型样本，分析各亚类在α_ro-α_ox平面上的分布特征及演化序列，并考察5 GHz射电多普勒因子、喷流功率、吸积爱丁顿比等物理量沿序列的变化，同时对比α_ox、α_ro和α_rx的分布差异。

Result: 发现(FSRQs+LBLs)→IBLs→HBLs在α_ro-α_ox平面上形成"∠"形演化序列，伴随射电多普勒因子、喷流功率和爱丁顿比递减；α_ox分布在亚类间差异小，而α_ro和α_rx差异显著。分布主要由吸积率与跨波段Doppler聚束效应（δ_i/δ_j起主导作用）调控，喷流形状和谱指数贡献较小。

Conclusion: FSRQs+LBLs为强喷流耦合辐射有效吸积源，喷流在到达射电区前仍保持相对论性速度；HBLs为弱喷流耦合辐射无效吸积源，喷流在光学区开始减速并在射电区呈亚相对论速度；IBLs是二者之间的过渡天体。

Abstract: The puzzling bimodality displayed by blazars on the broad-band spectral indices ($α_{\rm ro}-α_{\rm ox}$) plane is still an open question. To investigate its physical origin, we compiled a sample comprising 136 flat-spectrum radio quasars (FSRQs), 64 low-synchrotron-peaked BL Lac objects (LBLs), 39 intermediate-synchrotron-peaked BL Lac objects (IBLs), and 105 high-synchrotron-peaked BL Lac objects (HBLs). Our results show that (FSRQs+LBLs)$\rightarrow$IBLs$\rightarrow$HBLs follow a \reflectbox{$\angle$}-shaped evolutionary sequence on the $α_{\rm ro}-α_{\rm ox}$ plane, with the average 5\,GHz radio Doppler factor, jet power, and accretion Eddington ratio decreasing along this \reflectbox{$\angle$}-shaped track. Additionally, the distributions of $α_{\rm ox}$ exhibit only slight differences among blazar subclasses, whereas those of $α_{\rm ro}$ and $α_{\rm rx}$ differ significantly. Our results can be naturally explained if the distribution of blazars on the $α_{\rm ro}-α_{\rm ox}$ plane is regulated by the accretion rates and the Doppler beaming effect across different bands ($δ_i^{q_i}/δ_j^{q_j}$). However, $δ_i/δ_j$ plays a pivotal role in the distribution, while jet shape ($q=2(3)+α_ν$) and spectral indices ($α_ν$) play a minor role. Our results suggest that FSRQs+LBLs are strong-jet sources coupled with radiatively efficient accretion, and their jets are accelerated to a larger distance from the core and still maintain relativistic speeds down to the radio region; HBLs are weak-jet sources coupled with radiatively inefficient accretion, and their jets may begin to decelerate in the optical region and exhibit sub-relativistic speeds as they propagate to the radio region. IBLs are regarded as transitional objects between FSRQs+LBLs and HBLs.

</details>


### [118] [Gamma-Ray Bursts and Optical Brightness: Insights from Swift Satellite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03452)
*Istvan I. Racz,Yasmin Nehme,Lajos G. Balazs*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文基于Neil Gehrels Swift天文台卫星观测的200多个伽马射线暴（GRB）数据点，使用生存分析（可靠性建模）方法研究光学亮度与伽马射线性质的关系。研究发现，此前报道的持续时间、伽马通量和峰值通量对光学亮度分布的显著影响，在扩大样本后不再成立，表明这些关联可能不如先前认为的那样重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于Swift卫星在许多GRB事件中仅能为光学波段提供上限而非精确测量值，需要使用生存分析来处理包含删失数据的样本。此前研究表明GRB的持续时间、伽马通量和峰值通量对光学亮度分布有实质性影响，推测与中心引擎的射束能量有关，但这一结论需要更大样本的验证。

Method: 采用生存分析（又称可靠性建模/依赖建模）方法分析GRB光学性质数据集。由于部分样本仅存在光学上限约束，传统统计方法不适用，生存分析能够利用这些截尾数据中的有效信息来分析光学亮度分布及其与伽马射线属性（持续时间、伽马通量、峰值通量）之间的关联。

Result: 在分析了超过200个数据点后，研究发现GRB的持续时间、伽马通量和峰值通量与光学亮度分布之间不再存在显著的关联，推翻了此前基于较小样本得到的结论。

Conclusion: 此前报道的伽马射线属性对光学亮度的影响可能并不如先前推导的那样显著，这一分歧表明需要更多研究来深入理解GRB光学亮度与伽马射线性质之间的潜在物理机制。

Abstract: During the examination of optical properties of gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) measured during the life of the Neil Gehrels Swift Observatory satellite, significant discoveries were made. While the satellite measures gamma, X-ray, and optical data simultaneously, in many cases, only an upper limit is established in the optical domain. Hence, survival analysis, a.k.a. dependability modelling, was necessary, serving as a tool for studying samples where some cases are bound only by the upper limit, as mentioned earlier. This method is used to analyse datasets that may have upper or lower bounds rather than precise observations, but nevertheless contain relevant information. It was previously established that the duration, gamma fluence, and peak flux all have a substantial effect on the optical brightness distribution, probably due to the energy of the beam from the GRB's central engine. However, after analysing more than 200 data points, we no longer see this association. This disagreement shows that the previously reported impact may not be as significant as previously derived, needing more research to better understand the underlying processes that determine the optical brightness in connection to gamma-ray properties.

</details>


### [119] [KM3NeT/ARCA stacking search for high-energy neutrino point sources: the case of ultra-luminous infrared galaxies and blazars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03609)
*The KM3NeT Collaboration*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究利用KM3NeT/ARCA探测器336天（19-21个探测单元）的数据，对三类候选天体源（75个超亮红外星系、232个高同步峰BL Lac天体、88个极端同步峰耀变体）进行中微子发射的叠加搜索，基于1544个候选轨道光事件，未发现显著超出大气背景的信号，并给出90%置信水平的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能天体物理中微子的来源尚不明确，需要通过叠加分析的方法提高对潜在较弱源的探测灵敏度，检验是否与超亮红外星系及耀变体等候选源群存在关联。

Method: 采用叠加搜索方法，对三类候选源样本（超亮红外星系采用传统幂律谱建模；两类耀变体样本的预期中微子通量由受多波段观测数据约束的数值工具获得），基于KM3NeT/ARCA共336天的数据（19-21个探测单元配置），分析1544个被选择的轨道光中微子候选事件。

Result: 对三个源目录均未发现统计显著的大气背景之上的过量信号，并设置了90%置信水平的中微子发射上限。

Conclusion: 在三类候选源中未观察到显著的高能天体物理中微子发射，所得上限可用于约束相关源群体的中微子发射模型。

Abstract: A stacking search for high-energy astrophysical neutrino emission is presented using three candidate source populations: ultra-luminous infrared galaxies and two categories of blazars, specifically high-synchrotron-peaked BL Lacs and extreme blazars. The analysis is based on 336 days of data from the Astroparticle Research with Cosmics in the Abyss component of the KM3NeT research infrastructure (KM3NeT/ARCA), collected with detector configurations comprising 19-21 detection units, yielding 1544 selected track-like neutrino candidates. The sample of ultra-luminous infrared galaxies consists of 75 sources, for which conventional power-law spectra are assumed in modelling their signal emission. The blazar samples comprise 232 high-synchrotron-peaked BL Lac objects and 88 extreme synchrotron-peaked emitters, respectively. Their expected neutrino fluxes are obtained from numerical tools constrained by multi-wavelength observational data. No statistically significant excess above the atmospheric background is found for the three catalogues and 90% confidence level upper limits are set.

</details>


### [120] [NuSTAR View of the 2025 Mini-Outburst of the Black Hole X-ray Binary GRS 1739-278: Spectral and Timing Evolution in the Soft State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03624)
*Arshad Hussain,Umananda Dev Goswami*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对银河系黑洞候选体GRS 1739-278在2025年9月18-30日微爆发的衰减阶段的三次NuSTAR观测进行了定时与光谱分析，发现源处于软态、吸积盘主导状态，内盘温度从1.021 keV降至1.002 keV，内半径稳定在约0.95倍ISCO半径，光度从1.35×10^37降至1.15×10^37 erg/s，结果与高自旋黑洞在低吸积率下的标准软态行为一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对银河系黑洞候选体GRS 1739-278在2025年微爆发衰减期的光谱-定时特征进行详细表征，以验证其是否处于标准软态并推断黑洞自旋与吸积几何。

Method: 对三次NuSTAR观测进行联合FPMA+FPMB光谱分析，采用吸收的盘黑体加热康普顿化连续谱模型，结合10-12 keV附近的高显著性（11.7-15.0σ）弯曲特征（作为强盘反射/返回辐射的经验代理）进行光谱拟合；同时进行功率谱和Lomb-Scargle周期图分析以搜索周期性或准周期信号。

Result: 源处于软态、盘主导状态；3-79 keV计数率从112.4降至99.4 cts/s，硬度比约0.14-0.16，分数变化率从3.2%降至1.1%；未检测到周期或准周期信号；光谱拟合χ²/dof=1.04-1.15；内盘温度从1.021±0.001冷却至1.002 keV，内半径R_in≈27.7 km≈0.95倍的ISCO半径，恒定在0.3%以内；吸积未吸收4-30 keV光度从1.35×10^37降至1.15×10^37 erg/s（0.57-0.67% L_Edd）。

Conclusion: GRS 1739-278在2025年微爆发衰减期处于典型软态，吸积盘锚定在ISCO处，光谱-定时结果与高自旋黑洞在低且稳定下降的吸积率下的行为一致。

Abstract: We present a dedicated timing and spectral analysis of three NuSTAR observations of the Galactic black hole candidate GRS 1739-278 obtained during the decay of its 2025 mini-outburst (September 18-30, 2025). The source is found in a soft, disk-dominated state throughout, with a declining $3$-$79$ keV count rate (from $112.4$ to $99.4$ cts s$^{-1}$), a low and only mildly evolving hardness ratio ($\approx0.14$-$0.16$) and a fractional variability that drops from $3.2\%$ to $1.1\%$; no periodic or quasi-periodic signal is detected in the power spectrum or the Lomb-Scargle periodogram of any observation. Joint FPMA+FPMB spectral fits with an absorbed disk-blackbody plus thermal-Comptonization continuum, together with a broad, high-significance ($11.7$-$15.0σ$) curvature feature near $10$-$12$ keV (an empirical proxy for a strong disk-reflection/returning-radiation component) describe the $4$-$30$ keV spectra well ($χ^2/{\rm dof}=1.04$-$1.15$). The inner-disk temperature cools steadily from $1.021\pm0.001$ to $1.002^{+0.003}_{-0.002}$ keV while the disk normalization and hence the derived inner radius, $R_{\rm in}\simeq27.7$ km $\simeq0.95\,R_{ISCO}$ remains constant to within $0.3\%$, consistent with a disk anchored at the innermost stable circular orbit throughout. The unabsorbed $4$-$30$ keV luminosity declines from $1.35\times10^{37}$ to $1.15\times10^{37}$ erg s$^{-1}$ ($0.67\%$ to $0.57 L_{Edd}$). These results of the spectral-timing characterization of the 2025 mini-outburst are broadly consistent with a high-spin black hole accreting at a low, steadily declining rate in the canonical soft state.

</details>


### [121] [The Physics of the Nebular Phase in Black Hole X-ray Binaries: Modelling V404 Cygni](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03714)
*A. Ambrifi,T. Muñoz-Darias,J. A. Fernández-Ontiveros,J. Casares,D. Mata Sánchez,J. H. Matthews*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用光致电离代码Cloudy对银河系黑洞暂现源爆发末期的星云相进行了建模，定量复现了巴尔默减缩和Hα等值宽度等关键观测量，并提出该阶段由团块状、部分电离的喷射物产生，其总质量可能超过爆发期间吸积的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河系极端黑洞暂现源在爆发末期会出现星云相阶段（最早在2015年V404 Cygni爆发中被识别，持续约4天），其特征是极强光学发射线和巴尔默减缩显著增大。理解该阶段对揭示喷射物性质及爆发物理过程具有重要意义，但目前缺乏系统性的定量建模研究。

Method: 采用光致电离代码Cloudy对星云相进行建模。模拟中采用简化的辐照连续谱和发射气体假设，通过广泛改变密度（n_H ~ 10^10.5-10^12.5 cm^-3）和电离参数（logξ ~ -1.5到1）范围，定量复现巴尔默减缩和Hα等值宽度等观测特征，并定性再现观测光谱。此外还使用不同方法约束星云相的一般物理性质。

Result: 模拟成功定量复现了关键观测量（巴尔默减缩和Hα等值宽度），并定性再现了观测光谱。结果显示在跨越数量级的密度和电离参数变化范围内，巴尔默减缩基本稳定在观测范围内，支持星云相可在中等程度喷射物膨胀和冷却过程中持续存在。研究提出星云相由团块状、部分电离的喷射物产生，其总质量可能超过爆发期间的吸积质量。

Conclusion: 该研究通过数值模拟成功再现了黑洞暂现源爆发末期星云相的关键观测特征，确认了星云相源于早前抛射物在X射线辐照减弱后膨胀并复合的物理图像，并对喷射物的质量、结构和电离状态给出了重要约束。

Abstract: Some of the most extreme galactic black hole transients exhibit a nebular phase at the end of their outbursts. This stage was first identified in the 2015 outburst of V404 Cygni, when it dominated the optical spectrum for ~4 days. It is characterised by exceptionally strong optical emission lines, together with a significant increase in the Balmer decrement. The emission features show broad wings reaching velocities consistent with outflow speeds derived from prominent P-Cygni profiles that preceded the nebular phase. They are thus interpreted as arising from previously launched ejecta that expand and recombine as X-ray irradiation declines.
  In this work, we modelled this phase using the photoionisation code Cloudy. Adopting simple assumptions for the irradiating continuum and emitting gas, we quantitatively reproduced key observables, such as the Balmer decrement and the H$α$ equivalent width, and qualitatively reproduced the observed spectra. Our simulations show that the Balmer decrement remains roughly stable within the observed range across orders-of-magnitude changes in the density and ionisation parameter, spanning $n_\mathrm{H} \sim 10^{10.5}-10^{12.5}\,\text{cm}^{-3}$ and $-1.5 \lesssim \logξ\lesssim 1$. This supports the scenario in which the nebular phase can persist through moderate ejecta expansion and cooling, consistent with observations. Finally, we used different approaches to constrain the general properties of the nebular phase, which we propose is produced by clumpy, partially ionised ejecta whose total mass likely exceeds the mass accreted during the outburst.

</details>


### [122] [A catalogue of insights from the fourth LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA observing run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03859)
*Christopher P L Berry,Daniel Williams*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文回顾了LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA（LVK）合作组第四次观测运行（O4）中引力波科学的进展，重点介绍了约200个新的探测事件，包括迄今检测到的最强信号和前所未见的新型源，以及通过群体级分析对致密双星天体物理分布、广义相对论检验和宇宙膨胀率等关键问题取得的推断性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波科学在过去十年中已从单次探测的里程碑时代发展为近乎日常的事件，LVK旗舰科学成果已演变为包含全面分析结果的探测目录。对O4运行的最新发现进行系统回顾，有助于总结该领域在单源发现和群体统计层面的显著进步，并为未来观测计划提供展望。

Method: 本文采用综述性质的研究方法，对LVK合作组第四次观测运行中发布的探测目录进行分析与总结，涵盖两类内容：(1) 单个值得注意的发现，包括迄今最强的引力波信号和新型源； (2) 基于不断增加样本量的群体级分析，用于约束并合致密双星的天体物理分布、检验广义相对论可能存在的偏差，以及推断宇宙膨胀率。

Result: O4运行的最新目录各自包含约100个新探测，累计新增约200个事件。分析揭示了多样化的源亚群存在的证据，为理解大质量恒星演化和并合双星的形成提供了独特探针，并显著提升了对致密双星天体物理分布、广义相对论和宇宙膨胀率施加约束的能力。

Conclusion: 随着观测数据的持续积累，对引力波信号及其源的理解将不断深化。O4最终目录预计于2026年12月发布，未来采用更高探测器灵敏度的观测运行计划将带来更大的目录规模和更多强信号，持续推动引力波科学的前沿发展。

Abstract: Over the last decade, gravitational-wave science has developed from an era where a single detection was a Nobel-winning achievement to an (almost) everyday occurrence. The LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) Collaboration's flagship scientific results are now their catalogues of detections, which include a comprehensive set of analysis results characterising the astrophysical properties of sources, and their implications for fundamental physics and cosmology. We review discoveries from the fourth observing run (O4) of the LVK network. The most recent catalogues each include $\sim100$ new detections. Among these new detections are remarkable single discoveries: the loudest signals detected so far, and sources unlike those seen before. However, significant advances in the field are coming from population-level analyses, leveraging the increasing sample size to infer constraints on the astrophysical distribution of merging compact binaries, potential deviations to general relativity, and the expansion rate of the Universe. Analyses of the latest data have revealed evidence for multiple subpopulations of sources. These observations provide a unique probe into the evolution of massive stars and the formation of coalescing binaries. Our understanding of gravitational-wave signals and their sources will continue to deepen with more observations. The final catalogue from O4}is due for release in December 2026, and future observing runs with even greater detector sensitivity are planned. These will bring even larger catalogues, and additional loud signals, continuing to advance gravitational-wave science.

</details>


### [123] [Ultra-compact twin stars with hybrid equations of state from bosonic dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03964)
*Ishfaq Ahmad Rather,Sarah Louisa Pitz,Jürgen Schaffner-Bielich*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了强一阶相变至夸克物质的致密星与自相互作用玻色暗物质（DM）共存时的性质。发现暗物质的加入显著改变了质量-半径构型的稳定性；消除了双星（twin stars）之间不稳定的分支段，使稳定序列连续；发现了两类超致密天体（UCOs），即DM-halo类（f_DM≳0.9）和DM-core类（f_DM≲0.02），可通过正常物质的表面红移加以区分；还发现了具有相同质量和可见半径但不同暗物质含量的"终极双星"（ultimate twins）混合星解，其潮汐形变度和表面红移不同。未来的X射线和引力波观测可借此探测暗物质及一阶相变的存在与性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上对夸克物质相变致密星的研究往往忽略暗物质的影响，而暗物质在致密星中可能占据显著比例，从而改变星体的稳定性判据、质量-半径关系以及其他可观测性质。该研究旨在探索暗物质与强一阶相变共存时对致密星结构的影响，为未来天文观测提供区分和探测暗物质的途径。

Method: 采用包含强一阶相变（至夸克物质）的致密星模型，并引入自相互作用玻色暗物质作为额外流体。通过求解修改后的多成分TOV方程（或双流体平衡方程）来构建质量-半径关系，并使用一般性稳定性判据来分析构型的稳定性（而非简单的一流体判据）。系统地扫描暗物质质量分数f_DM、总致密度C等参数空间，以分类不同类型的稳定构型，并计算表面红移和潮汐形变度等可观测性质。

Result: (1) 暗物质的加入消除了hadronic分支与hybrid分支之间的不稳定段，使稳定质量-半径序列连续化；(2) 发现了两类稳定的超致密天体（C≥1/3）：DM-halo类（暗物质占总质量比例约90%以上）和DM-core类（约2%以下），分别对应的正常物质表面红移z=0.73-0.77和z<0.45，可作为分类判据；(3) 发现了"终极双星"解——质量和可见半径相同但暗物质含量不同的混合星，导致不同的潮汐形变度和表面红移，可以作为探测暗物质的关键信号。

Conclusion: 研究表明暗物质的存在从根本上改变了强一阶相变致密星的稳定性图景，产生了超出传统中子星范围的超致密天体。若未来的X射线和引力波观测发现质量和半径落在标准中子星允许范围之外的超致密星，将能同时约束暗物质的性质和夸克物质相变的存在。特别是"终极双星"和两类UCOs的独特可观测特征（不同表面红移与潮汐形变度）为探测暗物质提供了独特的观测窗口。

Abstract: The properties of compact stars with a strong first-order phase transition to quark matter and with an additional fluid of self-interacting bosonic dark matter (DM) are studied. We find that the inclusion of DM changes considerably the stability of mass-radius configurations relative to the naive one-fluid criterion. For compact star configurations with similar masses and different radii, so-called twin stars, the presence of DM removes the unstable segment between the hadronic and the hybrid branch, so that the stable mass-radius sequence becomes continuous after the onset of the phase transition to quark matter. We furthermore find stable ultra-compact objects (UCOs), defined by a total compactness $C = M_\text{tot}/R_\text{grav} \ge 1/3$. We observe two distinct classes of UCOs: a DM-halo class with $f_\text{DM} \gtrsim 0.9$, and a DM-core class at $f_\text{DM} \lesssim 0.02$. The two classes can be separated by the surface redshift of the normal matter, which reaches $z=0.73$--$0.77$ for the DM-core class and stays below $0.45$ for the DM-halo class. Finally, we find hybrid star solutions of 'ultimate twins' with similar mass and visible radius, but different dark matter content, leading to different tidal deformabilities and surface redshifts. Future X-ray and gravitational measurements of ultra-compact neutron stars with radii and masses outside the allowed neutron star range can thereby probe the presence and the properties of DM in addition to a first-order phase transition to quark matter.

</details>


### [124] [Sgr A* as a Galactic PeVatron: Multimessenger Signatures of the Magnetic Penrose Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04051)
*Marina Cermeño,Pedro De la Torre Luque,Cristina Fernández-Suárez,Viviana Gammaldi,Enrique Mier-Alonso,María J. Rodríguez,Miguel Á. Sánchez-Conde,Jaume Zuriaga-Puig*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了人马座A*（Sgr A*）中的磁彭罗斯过程（MPP），通过计算吸积流中的中子产生谱并追踪其在Kerr时空中的轨迹，证明Sgr A*可将质子加速至PeV能量，并预测了可由H.E.S.S.、HAWC和SWGO等望远镜观测的伽马射线与中微子信号，将其确立为可观测的致密天体自转能量提取机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究磁彭罗斯过程在银河系中心超大质量黑洞Sgr A*中的可观测性，将视界尺度的物理过程与多信使观测联系起来，并检验其作为银河系PeVatron候选体的可行性。

Method: 从核反应运动学直接计算吸积流中的中子产生谱，在Kerr时空中追踪中子轨迹以确定到达能层并经历MPP的中子群体，从而推导加速质子的能谱，并计算逃逸质子在中央分子云中通过强子相互作用产生的伽马射线和中微子发射。

Result: Sgr A*可通过MPP将质子加速至PeV能量；预测的伽马射线通量具有独特的光谱特征，可对H.E.S.S.和HAWC探测到的高能发射做出不可忽略的贡献；中微子通量低于IceCube推断的弥散银河分量；预测信号落在SWGO的预期灵敏度内，某些模型下仅比CTAO名义灵敏度低数倍。

Conclusion: MPP被确立为一种可观测的黑洞旋转能量提取机制，为视界尺度物理与多信使观测之间建立了直接联系，且该方法可推广至其他磁化黑洞。

Abstract: The magnetic Penrose process (MPP) is one of the most efficient mechanisms for extracting rotational energy from a magnetized Kerr black hole (BH), enabling charged particles to reach very-high energies. We investigate this process in Sagittarius A* (Sgr A*), the supermassive BH at the Galactic center (GC), focusing on its ultra-high-efficiency regime, in which neutrons undergo beta decay inside the BH ergosphere. As a key novelty, we compute the neutron production spectrum in the accretion flow directly from the nuclear reaction kinematics and follow neutron trajectories in the Kerr spacetime to determine the population that reaches the ergosphere and undergoes the MPP. From this population, we derive the spectrum of accelerated protons and show that Sgr A* can accelerate them up to PeV energies, strengthening its interpretation as a candidate Galactic PeVatron. We further compute the gamma-ray and neutrino emission produced through hadronic interactions of the escaping protons in the Central Molecular Zone. The predicted gamma-ray fluxes exhibit distinctive spectral features that could provide an observational signature of the MPP and may constitute a non-negligible contribution to the very-high-energy emission detected by H.E.S.S. and HAWC. The associated neutrino fluxes remain below the diffuse Galactic component inferred by IceCube, but may still contribute to the high-energy emission from the GC. Remarkably, the predicted signals lie within the projected sensitivity of SWGO for all scenarios considered here and, for some models, only a factor of a few below the nominal CTAO sensitivity, while KM3NeT/ARCA and IceCube-Gen2 will provide complementary tests. Our results establish the MPP as an observable mechanism for extracting BH rotational energy, providing a direct connection between horizon-scale physics and multimessenger observations that can be extended to other magnetized BHs.

</details>


### [125] [A targeted search for fast radio bursts from magnetars in elliptical galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04118)
*S. Paine,D. R. Lorimer,S. Sirota,G. M. Doskoch,S. Tabassum,M. Flanagan,J. Lituchy,A. Stone,J. W. Kania,M. Bhardwaj,S. Mehta,M. A. McLaughlin,B. Kharel,D. Agarwal,M. P. Surnis*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报道了利用绿岸望远镜（GBT）的GREENBURST单脉冲搜索管线与VEGAS后端，对五个本地大质量椭圆星系进行L波段FRB高灵敏度搜索。发现FRB 20240817A（位于M49方向），但经前景电子柱密度扣除（银河系+室女座星系团内介质+M49热气体晕）后，判定其为背景事件而非宿主关联。同时发现一组来自M49的低显著性重复脉冲（DM~150-160 cm^-3 pc）。未发现明确宿主定位的爆发，从而对这类系统中活跃磁星源的固有爆发率给出联合上限R0<0.028 hr^-1，较此前限制更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论预测附近巨椭圆星系中的球状星团是探测来自磁星的快速射电暴（FRB）的理想环境，作者旨在通过高灵敏度定向搜索验证这一预测，并约束此类系统中磁星的形成与活跃度。

Method: 使用GBT上的GREENBURST单脉冲搜索管线与VEGAS后端，在1.4 GHz L波段对五个本地大质量椭圆星系进行定向FRB巡测；采用贝叶斯空间关联框架评估宿主归属，并扣除银河系、室女座星系团内介质及目标星系热气体晕的前景电子柱密度贡献；统计各目标观测到的扩展球状星团分布比例以计算爆发率上限。

Result: 发现FRB 20240817A（脉宽15 ms，信噪比13，DM~983 cm^-3 pc），贝叶斯分析初步指向M49为宿主，但计入约300-400 cm^-3 pc的前景贡献后强烈排除物理关联，判定为背景事件；另发现一组DM~150-160 cm^-3 pc的低显著性重复脉冲。综合给出活动源固有爆发率上限R0<0.028 hr^-1，M49积分率上限<1.0 hr^-1。

Conclusion: FRB 20240817A并非来自M49的物理关联事件，而是背景FRB；搜寻未发现明确宿主定位的磁星爆发，联合上限较此前结果有所降低，表明动力学球状星团中的磁星形成效率可能低于此前假设。

Abstract: Motivated by predictions that globular clusters associated with nearby giant elliptical galaxies are prime environments for detecting fast radio bursts (FRBs) from magnetars, we report on a targeted high-sensitivity search across five local massive elliptical galaxies. We utilized the GREENBURST single-pulse search pipeline and the VEGAS backend on the Green Bank Telescope (GBT) to conduct an L-band (1.4 GHz) survey for FRBs in each target environment. We discovered FRB 20240817A in the direction of M49 (NGC 4472) which has a pulse width of 15 ms, a signal-to-noise ratio of 13, and a dispersion measure (DM) of $983~\rm{cm}^{-3}$ pc. Although a Bayesian spatial association framework initially flags M49 as the host, carefully accounting for the foreground electron column contributions from the Milky Way and both the Virgo intracluster medium and M49's hot gaseous halo ($\sim300-400~\rm{cm}^{-3}$ pc combined) strongly disfavours a physical connection. We conclude that FRB 20240817A is a background event. We also identified a repeating cluster of three low-significance pulses from the direction of M49 at a physically plausible DM of $150-160~\rm{cm}^{-3}$ pc. Accounting for the fraction of each target's extended globular cluster distribution observed, the absence of unambiguous, host-localized bursts allows us to place a joint upper limit on the intrinsic burst rate of a single active source within these systems of $R_0 < 0.028~\rm{hr}^{-1}$ (and an integrated rate limit of $<1.0$~hr$^{-1}$ for M49), lowering previous upper limits and suggesting that magnetar formation within dynamical clusters is less efficient than previously assumed.

</details>


### [126] [Low-angular-momentum accretion shocks can power weak-to-moderate X-ray flares from SgrA*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04145)
*Samik Mitra,Bożena Czerny,Michal Zajaček,Zach Sumners*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: X-ray flares from Sgr~A* span broad ranges in duration, fluence, and luminosity, but their origin remains unsettled. We test whether standing shocks in low-angular-momentum magnetized accretion flows can provide a viable energy reservoir to account for these events. Using semi-analytic trans-magnetosonic shock solutions, we estimate the kinetic energy available in the downstream post-shock flow and compare it with the 25-year \textit{Chandra} X-ray flare catalog. For each theoretical solution, we compute the required efficiency $ε=E_{\rm data}/E_{\rm sh}$, where $E_{\rm data}$ is the observed radiated energy for each flare and $E_{\rm sh}$ is the available energy in the shocked flow. Notably, the weak flares require $ε\sim10^{-3}$--$10^{-2}$, while moderate flares require a few percent. We perform the analyses for weakly and highly spinning cases, and the resulting weak-to-moderate flare energy budget remains unchanged. For the fiducial accretion rate and bolometric correction, the kinetic energy reservoir in standing shock is sufficient for the observed weak-to-moderate flare population, whereas the strongest events likely require higher efficiency which can be mediated via additional magnetic energy dissipation channel.

</details>

### [127] [Hadronic Mono-Z Dark Matter Sensitivity with Flow Matching on CMS Open Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02923)
*Hitesh Rasineni,Bhavishya Chebrolu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用CMS Run 2015D HTMHT开放数据（2.256 fb^-1），对强子型mono-Z暗物质产生通道进行了投影灵敏度研究。该研究采用条件流匹配连续归一化流（conditional flow-matching continuous normalizing flow）对背景进行建模，通过哨兵填补（sentinel imputation）处理缺失角特征，并在三个简化模型基准下分别获得2.89σ、7.62σ和7.41σ的预期显著度。消融研究表明，去除额外喷注运动学细节会使预期显著度下降53-71%，表明额外喷注拓扑在该通道中具有重要的判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用CMS开放数据进行暗物质搜索的可行性，并评估强子型mono-Z通道（特别是额外喷注拓扑信息）对暗物质生产的判别能力。

Method: 从CMS Run 2015D HTMHT开放数据中筛选出1,439,523个满足强子型mono-Z选择的事件。背景使用条件流匹配连续归一化流进行建模，在选中的HTMHT事件上训练并在重新加权到完整选中群体的验证集上评估。对未定义的角特征采用哨兵填补以缓解缺失目标特征的人为效应，保留训练/验证分裂索引以避免样本内评分偏差，并设置最小背景产额20的事件作为工作点选择约束。信号侧使用离线触发代理应用于模拟信号后再评分。

Result: 基线分析在三个简化模型基准下分别获得2.89σ、7.62σ和7.41σ的预期显著度。消融实验表明，移除详细额外喷注运动学会使预期显著度降低53-71%，证明额外喷注拓扑在强子型mono-Z通道中携带显著的判别能力。

Conclusion: 强子型mono-Z通道结合额外喷注拓扑信息具有可观的暗物质探测灵敏度，本研究为使用开放数据进行此类分析提供了可复现的方法框架；但结果仅为投影灵敏度，未进行实际数据解盲（unblinding），其局限性与可复现性在文中专门章节讨论。

Abstract: We present a projected sensitivity study for hadronic mono-$Z$ dark-matter production using CMS Run~2015D HTMHT open data corresponding to 2.256382381~\invfb, from which 1{,}439{,}523 events satisfy the hadronic mono-$Z$ selection. Backgrounds are modelled with a conditional flow-matching continuous normalizing flow trained on the selected HTMHT events and evaluated on a held-out validation split reweighted to the full selected population. To mitigate artifacts from missing-object features and avoid in-sample scoring bias we apply sentinel imputation for undefined angular features, persist the train/validation split indices, and enforce a minimum reported background yield of 20 events when selecting the working point. A signal-side offline trigger proxy is applied to the simulated signal before scoring. Under this procedure the baseline analysis yields expected significances of 2.89$σ$, 7.62$σ$, and 7.41$σ$ for three simplified-model benchmarks. An ablation study that removes the detailed extra-jet kinematics reduces the expected significance by 53--71\%, indicating that extra-jet topology carries substantial discriminating power in the hadronic mono-$Z$ channel. These results are projected sensitivities (no unblinding performed); the limitations and reproducibility of the study are discussed in Sections limitations and reproducibility.

</details>


### [128] [Comment on arXiv:2608.29450: "First Measurement of Solar Neutrinos through Elastic Neutrino-Electron Scattering at the keV Scale"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02950)
*Ke Han,Xiangdong Ji,Jianglai Liu,for the PandaX Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文指出XENONnT合作组在最近的太阳pp中微子通量测量结果中，其总结图中存在一个重要遗漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XENONnT合作组近期通过多吨级液氙探测器中的中微子-电子弹性散射，首次测量了太阳pp中微子通量，但作者发现其总结图中存在一处关键遗漏，需要指出并纠正。

Method: 本文并非提出新的实验方法，而是以批判性评论的方式，针对XENONnT合作组发布的太阳pp中微子通量测量结果中的总结图进行分析，指出遗漏的具体内容及其潜在影响。

Result: 指出了XENONnT在总结图中遗漏的重要信息，具体内容需结合论文正文展开。

Conclusion: XENONnT对太阳pp中微子通量的测量结果总结图存在关键信息遗漏，本文予以指出以促进实验结果的完整呈现。

Abstract: The XENONnT Collaboration recently reported a new measurement of the solar \(pp\) neutrino flux via neutrino-electron elastic scattering using a multi-ton-scale liquid xenon detector. We comment here on an important omission in their summary plot.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用LHCb实验在$\sqrt{s}=13\,\text{TeV}$、积分亮度为$5.4\,\text{fb}^{-1}$的$pp$碰撞数据，测量了$\eta$和$\eta'(958)$的异常衰变分支比，包括$\eta\to\mu^+\mu^-$、$\eta'(958)\to\mu^+\mu^-$、$\eta\to\pi^+\pi^-\mu^+\mu^-$和$\eta'(958)\to\pi^+\pi^-\mu^+\mu^-$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究$\eta$和$\eta'(958)$介子的异常衰变（如衰变到轻子对），这些过程对标准模型中子偶素衰变的理论预测检验具有重要意义，并为寻找可能的超出标准模型的新物理提供敏感探针。

Method: 使用LHCb探测器收集的$pp$碰撞数据，以$\phi(1020)\to\mu^+\mu^-$衰变作为归一化通道，测量各信号通道的分支比。对于未观测到显著信号的通道设置90%置信度上界。

Result: 测得$\cal{B}(\eta\to\mu^+\mu^-)=(5.30\pm0.14\pm0.18\pm0.26)\times10^{-6}$，为迄今最精确测量；$\cal{B}(\eta'(958)\to\mu^+\mu^-)<1.9\times10^{-7}$（90% CL）；$\cal{B}(\eta\to\pi^+\pi^-\mu^+\mu^-)<5.4\times10^{-7}$（90% CL）；$\cal{B}(\eta'(958)\to\pi^+\pi^-\mu^+\mu^-)=(2.73\pm0.14\pm0.13\pm0.14)\times10^{-5}$。

Conclusion: 该研究首次完成对$\eta'(958)$介子此类衰变的实验搜索，并给出了$\eta\to\mu^+\mu^-$分支比迄今最精确的测量值，对相关理论预言提供了重要实验约束。

Abstract: Branching fractions of $η$ and $η'(958)$ anomalous decays are measured using $pp$ collision data at a center-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}=13\,\text{TeV}$ collected by the LHCb experiment and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $5.4\,\text{fb}^{-1}$. The measured values and upper limits are \begin{align*} \cal{B}(η\toμ^+μ^-) &= (5.30\pm0.14\pm0.18\pm0.26)\times10^{-6} , \\ \cal{B}(η'(958)\toμ^+μ^-) &<1.9\times10^{-7}\textrm{ at 90% CL} , \\ \cal{B}(η\toπ^+π^-μ^+μ^-) &<5.4\times10^{-7}\textrm{ at 90% CL}, \\ \cal{B}(η'(958)\toπ^+π^-μ^+μ^-) &= (2.73\pm0.14\pm0.13\pm0.14)\times10^{-5} , \end{align*} where the uncertainties are, respectively, statistical, systematic, and due to the external branching fraction of the $φ(1020)\toμ^+μ^-$ decay, which is used for normalization. This marks the first experimental search for the $η'(958)$ decay, and the most precise measurement of the branching fraction $\cal{B}(η\toμ^+μ^-)$ to date.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了利用升级后的HADES探测器对弹性质子-质子散射的系统研究，基于4.53 GeV和1.60 GeV两种动能下质子束轰击液态氢靶的数据。这是升级后的探测器（含为PANDA实验设计的带STT前向探测器）的首次测量，利用弹性散射的两体运动学约束研究了探测器对准、束流对准和束流能量标定，并通过与国际实验数据重叠的动量转移区间归一化确定了实验亮度。在宽范围的四动量转移平方t区间内测定了微分截面，提取出斜率参数B在T=4.53 GeV时为8.98±0.3 (GeV/c)^-2，在T=1.60 GeV时为7.3±0.1 (GeV/c)^-2。该工作为世界弹性质子-质子散射数据库做出贡献，为理论建模和未来实验的亮度标定提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹性质子-质子散射是研究强相互作用基本性质的重要探针，但现有世界数据库在该能量范围内仍不完善。同时，升级后的HADES探测器需要进行首轮物理测量验证，并利用弹性散射的运动学约束来标定探测器与束流参数，为后续实验提供参考基准。

Method: 利用HADES探测器（含为PANDA实验设计的前向探测器STT）采集T=4.53 GeV和1.60 GeV质子束轰击液态氢靶的数据；利用弹性散射两体运动学约束进行探测器对准和束流对准；通过与全球先前实验在t区间重叠处的数据归一化来确定亮度；在大范围t区间测定微分截面并提取衍射斜率参数B。

Result: 成功完成升级后HADES探测器的首次物理测量，确定了束流能量和实验亮度；在两个能量点分别提取了斜率参数B=8.98±0.3 (GeV/c)^-2（T=4.53 GeV）和B=7.3±0.1 (GeV/c)^-2（T=1.60 GeV）。

Conclusion: 该测量补充了世界弹性pp散射数据库在中等能量区间的数据空白，为理论模型（如Regge理论等）提供精确输入，同时为同能量范围内未来实验的亮度标定建立了参考方法。

Abstract: This work presents an investigation of elastic proton-proton scattering based on data collected with the recently upgraded HADES detector using beams of protons with kinetic energies of $T = 4.53$ GeV and $T = 1.60$ GeV impinging on a liquid hydrogen target. This is the first measurement emerging from the upgraded detector setup, including a Forward Detector with straw-tube tracking planes designed for the PANDA experiment. The well-constrained two-body kinematic relations of elastic scattering have been exploited to study the detector alignment, the beam alignment, and to provide the final measurement of the beam energy. In addition, the recorded luminosity of the experiment has been determined by normalizing the HADES data in a range of the squared four-momentum transfer $t$ that overlaps with measurements by previous experiments worldwide. Furthermore, the differential cross sections have been determined over a large range of $t$ from which the slope parameter $B$ is extracted, yielding $B = 8.98 \pm 0.3$ (GeV/$c$)$^{-2}$ and $7.3 \pm 0.1$ (GeV/$c$)$^{-2}$ at $T = 4.53$ GeV and $1.60$ GeV, respectively. This contribution to the world database on elastic proton-proton scattering provides input to theoretical modeling and serves as a reference for luminosity determinations by future experiments in the same energy range.

</details>

Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的质心能量3.773 GeV、积分亮度7.93 fb⁻¹的e⁺e⁻湮灭数据，精确测量了D⁰ → Kₛ⁰ω和D⁰ → K_L⁰ω的绝对分支比，分别为(11.79±0.19±0.26±0.47)×10⁻³和(10.84±0.14±0.23±0.44)×10⁻³，并测定了两者的Kₛ⁰-K_L⁰分支比不对称度为(4.2±1.0±0.9±2.8)%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测量粲介子D⁰衰变到Kₛ⁰ω和K_L⁰ω的分支比及其不对称性，用于检验标准模型框架下粲物理中的CP对称性破缺效应和强相互作用动力学，深入研究K⁰-反K⁰混合及强相位等基本物理问题。

Method: 使用BESIII探测器在ψ(3770)共振峰处收集的7.93 fb⁻¹高统计量数据样本，通过e⁺e⁻ → D⁰D̄⁰过程进行绝对分支比测量。对Kₛ⁰ω和K_L⁰ω两个衰变道分别进行信号重建，统计误差通过似然拟合获得，系统误差通过多种来源的逐项分析评估，并单独量化了非共振D⁰ → π⁺π⁻π⁰Kₛ,L⁰过程与共振信号间的干涉效应所引入的不确定度。

Result: 测得B(D⁰→Kₛ⁰ω)=(11.79±0.19±0.26±0.47)×10⁻³，B(D⁰→K_L⁰ω)=(10.84±0.14±0.23±0.44)×10⁻³（统计/系统/干涉不确定度依次排列），Kₛ⁰-K_L⁰分支比不对称度为R(D⁰,Kₛ,L⁰ω)=(4.2±1.0±0.9±2.8)%。三种不确定度来源中，干涉效应贡献了最大的不确定度分量。

Conclusion: 该测量提供了D⁰介子衰变到Kₛ⁰ω和K_L⁰ω的精确绝对分支比数据，其不对称性与零偏离约在2-3σ水平，可作为检验标准模型及寻找新物理效应的敏感探针。干涉效应的显著不确定性提示未来需更精细地建模非共振背景过程。

Abstract: Based on $e^+ e^-$ annihilation data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 7.93~$fb^{-1}$ collected at a center-of-mass energy of 3.773 GeV with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII collider, the absolute branching fractions of the decays $D^0 \to K_{S}^{0} ω$ and $D^0 \to K_{L}^{0} ω$ are measured to be $(11.79 \pm 0.19 \pm 0.26 \pm 0.47) \times 10^{-3}$ and ($10.84 \pm 0.14 \pm 0.23 \pm 0.44) \times 10^{-3}$, respectively.
  The $K_{S}^{0}- K_{L}^{0}$ branching-fraction asymmetry of these two decays is $R(D^0,K_{S,L}^{0} ω) = \frac{\mathcal{B}(D^0 \to K_{S}^{0} ω) - \mathcal{B}(D^0 \to K_{L}^{0}ω)}{\mathcal{B}(D^0 \to K_{S}^{0} ω) + \mathcal{B}(D^0 \to K_{L}^{0} ω)} =(4.2 \pm 1.0 \pm 0.9 \pm 2.8)\%$.
  Here, the first uncertainties are statistical, the second systematic, and the third arise from the interference between $D^0 \to K_{S,L}^{0} ω$ and the non-resonant $D^0 \to π^+ π^- π^0 K_{S,L}^{0}$ processes.

</details>


### [132] [Multi-tesla operation of high-temperature superconducting cavities for accelerated axion dark matter searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03879)
*Danho Ahn,Jinsu Kim,Seongtae Park,Jiwon Lee,Ohjoon Kwon,Woohyun Chung,HeeSu Byun,Sergey Uchaikin,Arajan Ferdinand van Loo,Yasunobu Nakamura,Dojun Youm,SungWoo Youn,Yannis K. Semertzidis*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种基于应变控制、机械剥离的REBCO高温超导带材构建的三维谐振腔架构，用于轴子暗物质探测。通过在8T磁场下实现Q0达1.4×10^7（比铜腔高两个数量级），并在实际轴子搜索实验中实现180mK噪声温度和约8.4倍的扫描速率加速，克服了传统超导体涡旋损耗和铜腔反常趋肤效应两大致命限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子作为暗物质候选粒子可通过射频腔在强磁场中发生轴子-光子转换来探测。然而，多特斯拉场会在标准超导体中引发严重的涡旋耗散，而铜腔则受限于反常趋肤效应导致Q值难以超过10^5。因此需要一种既能在高磁场下保持高Q值、又便于实用化集成的腔体架构，以加速轴子探测灵敏度。

Method: 采用极对极（pole-to-pole）三维腔体架构，使用应变控制、机械剥离的REBCO高温超导带材构建。关键创新包括：1）选择性去除有损耗的金属基底，同时利用铜稳定层作为"导电背衬"；2）将纵向装配间隙转化为截止波导，抑制跨接缝射频泄漏。采用双轨策略：先以5.4GHz谐振腔验证材料本征高场性能，再工程化设计2.3GHz可调谐、体积最大化的探路腔用于实际实验部署。

Result: 5.4GHz谐振腔在8T磁场下实现Q0=1.4×10^7，比传统铜腔基线高出两个数量级。2.3GHz探路腔在CAPP-PACE的Pilot Axion Cavity Experiment中实现180mK噪声温度、Q值较铜腔提升5倍，累计带来约8.4倍的扫描速率加速。

Conclusion: 该REBCO高温超导腔架构成功解决了高磁场下超导腔涡旋损耗和铜腔Q值受限的双重难题，在保持高灵敏度的同时实现了实际轴子搜索实验的工程集成，显著加速了轴子暗物质探测进程。

Abstract: Axion haloscopes use radio-frequency cavities immersed in a magnetic field to search for dark-matter axions, which could resolve two central puzzles in fundamental physics: the strong charge-parity problem in quantum chromodynamics and the nature of dark matter. Multi-tesla fields trigger axion-to-photon conversion but induce severe vortex dissipation in standard superconductors, whereas copper cavities are limited by the anomalous skin effect ($Q \lesssim 10^5$). Here, we overcome these barriers by introducing a pole-to-pole 3-dimensional cavity architecture constructed from strain-controlled, mechanically delaminated rare-earth barium copper oxide (REBCO) tapes. By selectively stripping the lossy metallic substrate while utilizing the copper stabilizer as a "conductive backing" we convert the longitudinal assembly gaps into waveguides below cutoff, effectively suppressing cross-seam RF leakage. Employing a two-track strategy, we first unveiled the intrinsic high-field potential of the material with a 5.4 GHz resonant cavity, achieving an unloaded quality-factor ($Q_0$) of $1.4 \times 10^7$ in an 8 T magnetic field, exceeding conventional copper baselines by two orders of magnitude. Second, prioritizing practical haloscope integration, we engineered a tunable, volume-maximized 2.3 GHz pathfinder cavity. Deployed in the Pilot Axion Cavity Experiment at the Center for Axion and Precision Physics Research (CAPP-PACE), this system achieved a 180 mK noise temperature and a 5-fold $Q$ enhancement over copper, cumulatively delivering a $\sim$8.4-fold scan-rate acceleration.

</details>
