# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Estimating the asymptotics of integer partitions in intermediate dimensions ($d = 3,4,5,6$)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03034)
*Avinandan Mondal*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过自适应权重学习结合Markov chain Monte Carlo（MCMC）模拟，数值研究了d=3,4,5,6维整数分拆的渐近行为，发现这些中间维度下整数分拆的渐近增长率均严格快于MacMahon数给出的上界，并在各维度给出亚主导项的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作证明d≥7维的整数分拆渐近增长快于MacMahon数，而d=1,2维两者吻合。中间维度d=3,4,5,6的对比尚属开放问题，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用自适应权重学习机制确定合适的采样权重，随后执行标准MCMC模拟，将n推进至N=15000，数值估计各中间维度整数分拆的渐近系数，并采用极限存在假设下的拟合方式提取主导项与部分次主导渐近项。

Result: 数值结果表明，在d=3,4,5,6维度下，整数分拆渐近增长均快于MacMahon数：主导系数分别为1.8196±0.0019、1.7215±0.0045、1.6521±0.0059和1.652±0.021，均大于MacMahon对应系数1.7898、1.6614、1.5737和1.509。另给出各维度部分次主导项的估计。

Conclusion: 本文通过数值模拟揭示了中间维度（d=3,4,5,6）整数分拆的渐近行为同样快于MacMahon数预测，表明该现象在几乎所有维度（除d=1,2）均成立，为后续严格的解析证明提供了数值依据。

Abstract: It was recently shown by Yeliussizov \cite{Yeliussizov} that integer partitions in dimensions $d \geq 7$ asymptotically grow strictly faster than MacMahon numbers. As MacMahon numbers match with integer partitions in dimensions $d = 1,2$, the comparison of asymptotics of integer partitions with MacMahon numbers in intermediate dimensions ($d = 3,4,5,6$) is an open question. In this work, we perform Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) simulations till $N=15000$ by using adaptive weight learning followed by conventional MCMC steps to numerically estimate the asymptotics of integer partitions in these intermediate dimensions. We numerically establish that in these intermediate dimensions, partitions asymptotically grow faster than MacMahon numbers. More specifically, assuming that the limits exist, we show: $\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-3/4}\log p_3(n) = 1.8196 \pm 0.0019$, $\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-4/5}\log p_4(n) = 1.7215 \pm 0.0045$, $\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-5/6}\log p_5(n) = 1.6521 \pm 0.0059$, and $\lim_{n\to\infty}\log n^{-6/7}p_6(n) = 1.652 \pm 0.021$ for partitions in dimensions $d=3,4,5,$ and $6$ respectively. These numbers are all larger than MacMahon leading order asymptotic coefficients of $1.7898, 1.6614, 1.5737,$ and $1.509$ respectively. Additionally, we also find estimates for some of the sub-leading asymptotic terms in $\log p_d(n)$ in each of the dimensions.

</details>


### [134] [Computational Algorithms for Invariant Reduction of Variational Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03197)
*Kostya Druzhkov,Alexey Shevyakov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了计算算法，将PDE的对称约简方法（如不变守恒律、变分结构和哈密顿结构）通过不变约简机制显式地从原始模型传递到约简模型。算法利用变分p-形式作为（度偏移的）切系统的守恒律这一解释，将摄动变量视为反交换变量，从而在分级交换框架下统一处理守恒律、变分1-形式和预辛结构的约简。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏微分方程（PDE）的对称约简比其他类型的约简继承了更多的几何结构，包括不变守恒律、变分结构和哈密顿型结构。然而此前缺乏将这些结构通过不变约简机制显式传递到约简模型的系统计算方法，需要开发具体的计算算法来实现这一过程。

Method: 将变分p-形式解释为扩展系统（即度偏移的切系统，由原始方程及其线性化组成，其中摄动变量被处理为反交换变量）的守恒律。在此框架下，利用守恒律理论的已知概念并自然地适配到分级交换（graded-commutative）设置中。文中提出了三种算法：(i) 基于同伦的约简算法，适用于演化方程组，已在Maple中实现；(ii) 下降约简算法，适用于一般ℓ-正规系统中p>0的情形；(iii) 在适当条件下适用于点对称性的简单约简算法，包括用较少独立变量的系统来描述约简。

Result: 守恒律、变分1-形式和预辛结构的约简被统一为一个单一的算法过程。示例涵盖一维和二维空间中的非线性演化方程、Laplace方程、不可压缩Euler方程以及Pavlov方程的余切系统。

Conclusion: 作者建立了一套统一的算法框架，将守恒律约简、变分1-形式约简和预辛结构约简纳入同一程序，并提供了适用于不同系统类型的多种算法实现，通过多个经典方程验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Symmetry reductions of partial differential equations (PDEs) inherit more geometric structures than other types of reductions: invariant conservation laws, variational structures, and, under suitable conditions, Hamiltonian-type structures of the original model descend to the reduced model through the mechanism of invariant reduction. This paper develops computational algorithms that carry out such reductions explicitly. We use the interpretation of variational $p$-forms as conservation laws of an enlarged system --- the (degree-shifted) tangent system --- consisting of the original equations together with their linearizations, in which the perturbation variables are treated as anticommuting. This allows us to formulate the algorithms using well-known concepts from the theory of conservation laws, naturally adapted to the graded-commutative setting. The reduction of conservation laws, variational $1$-forms, and presymplectic structures thereby becomes a single algorithmic procedure. We present (i) a homotopy-based reduction algorithm for systems of evolution equations, implemented in Maple; (ii) a descent reduction algorithm applicable to general $\ell$-normal systems for $p>0$; and (iii) a simple reduction algorithm available for point symmetries under suitable conditions, including the description of reductions in terms of systems involving fewer independent variables. Examples include nonlinear evolution equations in one and two spatial dimensions, the Laplace equation, the incompressible Euler equations, and the cotangent system of Pavlov's equation.

</details>


### [135] [Optimal bounds for embedded eigenvalues of one-dimensional discrete Schrödinger operators with decaying potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03224)
*Jifeng Chu,Wencai Liu,Kang Lyu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一维离散薛定谔算子在一类慢衰减实值势（V(n)=O(1)/(1+n)）作用下，嵌入特征值存在与不存在的临界相变条件，刻画了势的渐近系数的精确阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散薛定谔算子的嵌入特征值问题在数学物理中具有重要意义，特别是在慢衰减势（衰减阶为O(1/n)）情形下，其嵌入特征值的存在性缺乏精确刻画。本文旨在确定该临界衰减系数，填补这一精确相变结果的空白。

Method: 本文通过分析势V(n)的渐近系数的临界阈值，利用谱理论、散射理论或振荡积分等技术，对一维半直线上带自伴边界条件的离散薛定谔算子进行嵌入特征值的Sharp条件刻画。

Result: 给出了关于势的渐近系数在嵌入特征值存在性方面的精确（sharp）相变阈值，即当系数低于某临界值时不存在嵌入特征值，达到或超过该临界值时则存在。

Conclusion: 研究确立了慢衰减离散势条件下嵌入特征值存在性与不存在的精确临界分界，为离散薛定谔算子谱理论提供了严格的Sharp结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider one-dimensional discrete Schrödinger operators \begin{align}
  Hu(n)=(Δ+V)u(n)\nonumber \end{align}
  on $\ell^2(\mathbb{N})$ with a self-adjoint boundary condition at $n=0$, where $Δ$ denotes the discrete Laplacian and $V(n)$ is a real-valued perturbation satisfying
  $$V(n)=\frac{O(1)}{1+n}.$$ We determine the sharp transition for the asymptotic coefficient of \(V\) governing the existence and nonexistence of embedded eigenvalues.

</details>


### [136] [Controllability, returning waves and scattering without reverberation in 3D acoustic dynamic system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03536)
*Mikhail I. Belishev,Aleksei F. Vakulenko*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究三维动态声学散射系统$u_{tt}-Δu+qu=0$在存在束缚态时的可控性问题。当哈密顿量$H=-Δ+q$具有束缚态时，某些重新聚焦点$a$处系统会丧失可控性，导致有限能量波同时在过去和未来锥中消失、不留回声地离开非均匀区域，该效应与波前反转（时间反演镜）有相似性但数学上更有意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨具有紧支撑势的波方程系统中，势场的束缚态如何影响动态声学散射系统的可控性，揭示量子束缚态与经典波传播控制之间的深层联系，并发现一种新的物理效应。

Method: 采用无穷远源（控制）产生入射球面波的方法分析系统可控性。通过将波聚焦于$x=0$（t=0时刻充满全空间），研究当重新聚焦点变为其他位置$a$时，系统可控性是否保持。核心是利用哈密顿量$H=-Δ+q$的束缚态来分析可控性丧失的条件。

Result: 证明了当哈密顿量存在束缚态时，系统中会出现某些点$a$，使得系统在这些点重新聚焦时丧失可控性。由此导致物理效应：存在有限能量的波$u^f$同时在过去锥和未来锥中消失，即波在进入非均匀区域时不产生回响就离开。这一效应与波前反转（时间反演镜）有相似之处。

Conclusion: 束缚态的存在破坏了这类动态声学散射系统在某些空间点上的可控性，并引发一种独特的无回响传播效应。该效应在数学意义上比时间反演镜更为深刻，为理解势场散射系统中量子-经典对应提供了新视角。

Abstract: The dynamic acoustic scattering system is governed by the wave equation $u_{tt}-Δu+qu=0$ in $\Bbb R^3,\,\,\,-\infty<t<\infty$, with a compactly supported potential $q$ and infinitely distant sources (controls) $f$, which initiate incoming spherical waves $u=u^f(x,t)$ provided $u^f\big|_{|x|<-t,\,\,\,t<0}=0$. These waves are focused at $x=0$ and fill up the whole space at the moment $t=0$. The system is {\it controllable} if the set of waves $u^f(\cdot,0)$ produced by all finite energy controls $f$, covers the space $L_2(\Bbb R^3)$.
  As we show, if the Hamiltonian $H=-Δ+q$ has the bound states, then in the space the points $a$ appear such that the system, being refocused at $x=a$, loses controllability. The latter leads to a physical effect: the waves $u^f$ of finite energy appear, which vanish simultaneously in the past and future cones $|x|<\pm\, t$ and leave the region of inhomogeneity of $q$ without reverberation. This effect has some similarities with the wavefront reversal (Time Reversing Mirror), but is more meaningful from a mathematical point of view.

</details>


### [137] [The conductivity matrix at the topological phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03832)
*Simone Fabbri,Giovanna Marcelli,Alessandro Giuliani*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了二维非相互作用半金属晶格电子系统完整电导率矩阵的显式公式（基于欧几里得多体形式），发现纵向电导率仅取决于锥形带结构，而横向电导率同时由锥形结构和相邻拓扑相的性质决定。在旋转对称性假设下电导率矩阵恢复普适性，并通过多个量子霍尔流体模型展示了横向电导率分别为半整数量子化或依赖外部应变参数的连续非普适情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半金属相出现在两个Bloch带在费米能处以锥形交叉相接触处，通常发生在不同整数量子霍尔相之间的转变点。理解这一临界点的电导率矩阵的完整行为与普适性特征对于拓扑相变物理至关重要，但此前缺乏对整电导率矩阵的显式表述。

Method: 采用欧几里得多体形式（Euclidean many-body formalism）对二维非相互作用半金属晶格电子系统进行推导，获得整个电导率矩阵（含纵向和横向分量）的闭式表达式，并对多种物理相关的量子霍尔流体拓扑相变模型进行计算验证。

Result: 推导得到电导率矩阵的显式公式：纵向电导率仅依赖于费米面处锥形带的具体几何形状；横向电导率则由锥形结构及附近两个拓扑相的本质共同决定。一般情况下（无旋转对称性）电导率不在普适量子电导的整数倍；但在线性化哈密顿量涌现旋转对称性假设下，电导率矩阵恢复普适性。对具体模型计算展示了横向电导率可以是e²/h的半整数倍，也可连续依赖于外部应变参数（非普适）。

Conclusion: 半金属态的电导率矩阵的普适性依赖于系统具备涌现旋转对称性；否则横向电导率可表现出非普适（连续参数依赖）或半整数普适量子化的不同特征。该工作为理解量子霍尔拓扑相变临界点的输运性质提供了完整的理论框架。

Abstract: For a general class of two-dimensional non-interacting semi-metallic electron systems on a lattice, we derive an explicit formula for the whole conductivity matrix, by using Euclidean many-body formalism. The semi-metallic phase takes place whenever two Bloch bands touch at the Fermi energy with conical intersections and generally occurs at the transition between distinct integer quantum Hall phases. Unlike the longitudinal conductivity (which depends solely on the shape of the cones at the Fermi level), the transverse conductivity is remarkably determined both by the conical structures of the bands and by the nature of the two nearby topological phases. Generically, in the semi-metallic state, neither the longitudinal nor the transverse conductivities are quantized in integer multiples of a universal conductivity quantum; however, universality of the conductivity matrix is restored under the assumption of emergent rotational symmetry of the linearized Hamiltonian at the Fermi points. Using our formula, we compute the conductivity matrix for several physically relevant models of quantum Hall fluids at the topological phase transition, exhibiting cases where the transverse conductivity is quantized in half-integer multiples of $e^2/h$ and cases where it depends continuously on an external strain parameter, thus shedding light on its universality or non-universality features.

</details>


### [138] [Classification of abstract Bose field models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03863)
*Yasumichi Matsuzawa*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对量子场论中的一类抽象Bose场模型进行了酉等价分类，涵盖抽象自由Bose场模型、抽象van Hove-Miyatake模型以及红外重整化van Hove-Miyatake模型，并将该分类应用于二次相互作用模型的分类，解答了Arai提出的关于红外重整化van Hove-Miyatake模型分类的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子场论中，对各种模型进行酉等价分类是理解其基本结构和相互关系的核心问题。Arai此前提出了关于红外重整化van Hove-Miyatake模型分类的开放问题，本文旨在系统地解决更大一类抽象Bose场模型的分类问题。

Method: 本文采用算子理论方法，在量子场论框架下对抽象Bose场模型建立分类准则。通过刻画酉等价的不变量，将抽象自由Bose场模型、抽象van Hove-Miyatake模型和红外重整化van Hove-Miyatake模型纳入统一的分类框架，并将所得结果推广应用到二次相互作用模型的分类。

Result: 该文给出了一类抽象Bose场模型的完整酉等价分类，并作为应用对二次相互作用模型进行了分类，最终回答了Arai提出的关于红外重整化van Hove-Miyatake模型分类的开放问题。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一类广泛的抽象Bose场模型在酉等价意义下的完全分类理论，不仅覆盖了多个重要模型类，还成功解决了长期存在的问题，为量子场论中模型的结构分析提供了有力的分类工具。

Abstract: We classify a class of abstract Bose field models in quantum field theory up to unitary equivalence. The class includes abstract free Bose field models, abstract van Hove--Miyatake models, and infrared-renormalized van Hove--Miyatake models. Moreover, as an application of our classification, we classify quadratic interaction models. In particular, our results answer a question of A. Arai concerning the classification of infrared-renormalized van Hove--Miyatake models.

</details>


### [139] [Limits of Stochastic Semigroups and Block-Triangular Majorisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04057)
*Fabio Deelan Cunden,Jakub Czartowski,Giovanni Gramegna,Marilena Ligabò*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由不变分布参数化的随机矩阵半群在参数趋于无穷时的极限结构，发现该极限半群与保持极限分布的半群不同。对于吉布斯向量情形，极限半群为具有附加次随机性约束的块上三角随机矩阵，由此引出"块三角majorisation"概念，它在普通majorisation与上三角majorisation之间插值，并由有限族单调函数完全刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究依赖于参数的随机矩阵半群在参数趋于无穷时的行为，揭示"取极限"与"保持不变分布"这两个操作不可交换的现象，并为理解低温极限下（β→∞）马尔可夫过程的渐近结构提供统一框架。

Method: 作者引入基于不变分布γ(β)的随机矩阵半群的极限概念；利用块结构分解与技术分析，证明极限半群由块上三角随机矩阵构成并附带次随机性约束；通过刻画极值元素与诱导的概率向量预序，建立了块三角majorisation理论，并利用有限族单调函数给出完全刻画；最后分析了Rényi α-熵在极限下的行为。

Result: 证明了极限半群与保持极限分布的半群不一致；当不变分布为吉布斯向量时，极限半群为满足次随机约束的块上三角随机矩阵；完整刻画并枚举了其极值元素；建立了块三角majorisation，证明其可由有限族单调函数完全刻画；分析了Rényi熵的渐近行为。

Conclusion: 该工作为参数化随机矩阵半群的极限研究提供了通用框架，块三角majorisation作为介于普通majorisation与上三角majorisation之间的新序关系，具有独立的理论价值和应用前景，特别是在统计物理低温极限与马尔可夫链渐近分析方面。

Abstract: We investigate limits of semigroups of stochastic matrices defined by their invariant distribution. Given probability vectors $γ(β)$ depending on a parameter $β$, we introduce a notion of convergence as $β\to\infty$ for the corresponding semigroups of $γ(β)$-preserving stochastic matrices and investigate the structure of the resulting limit. In general, the limiting semigroup differs from the semigroup preserving the limiting distribution, showing that these two operations do not commute. We develop a general framework for such limiting semigroups and study in detail the case in which the invariant distributions are Gibbs vectors at the inverse temperature $β$. We show that the limiting semigroup consists of block-upper-triangular stochastic matrices subject to additional substochasticity constraints. We characterise and enumerate their extremal elements and determine the preorder on probability vectors induced by the action of the semigroup. The resulting notion of Block-Triangular majorisation interpolates between ordinary majorisation and upper triangular (aka unordered) majorisation. We show that it is completely characterised by a finite family of monotones and analyse the corresponding behaviour of Rényi $α$-entropies as $β\to\infty$.

</details>


### [140] [A simple derivation of the zero-temperature BCS functional from the reduced BCS Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04060)
*Christian Hainzl,Riccardo Panza*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在巨正则系综下，约化BCS哈密顿量的精确有限体积基态能量与对应的有限体积BCS泛函最小值之间的误差与体积无关，从而确立了热力学极限下BCS变分原理对基态能量密度的精确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证BCS理论在严格的数学意义上对基态能量密度的变分原理是否精确成立，这是凝聚态物理中BCS超导理论的严格化核心问题之一。

Method: 在全费米Fock空间上考虑巨正则约化BCS哈密顿量，配对吸引相互作用支撑在费米面附近的能量壳层内。对固定化学势，比较精确有限体积基态能量与有限体积BCS泛函的最小值，并证明二者之间的误差与体积无关（即误差有界且不随体积增大而发散）。

Result: 证明了精确有限体积基态能量与BCS泛函最小值之差为体积无关的量（即有界误差），进而推导出热力学极限下BCS变分原理对基态能量密度是精确的。

Conclusion: BCS变分原理在热力学极限下对基态能量密度具有严格精确性，为BCS理论提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: We consider a grand-canonical reduced BCS Hamiltonian on the full fermionic Fock space with an attractive pairing interaction supported in an energy shell around the Fermi surface. For fixed chemical potential, we prove that the exact finite-volume ground-state energy differs from the minimum of the corresponding finite-volume BCS functional by a volume-independent error. As a consequence, the BCS variational principle is exact for the ground-state energy density in the thermodynamic limit.

</details>

### [141] [Structural Compatibility and Uniform Stability of Temporally Degenerate Parabolic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03136)
*Amadou Cissé*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间退化抛物型分布参数系统的反馈设计问题。作者证明了此类系统允许的反馈互连完全由一个算子相容性条件所刻画，该条件将奇异反应算子与执行器及观测算子联系起来。该条件可去除闭环动力学中的奇异成分，将退化演化方程化为正则演化方程。基于此正则化公式，通过临界-残差分解获得指数稳定性证书，并经有限到无限提升定理推广到原无穷维演化系统。最终得到可构造的静态输出反馈综合方法，数值实验验证了正则化机制、稳定性证书及提升定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分布参数系统的反馈设计预设闭环动力学构成良定的演化问题，但这一预设对时间退化抛物型系统而言并不平凡——时间退化不仅影响演化方程的分析性质，还影响反馈互连本身的数学表述。因此需要为这类系统建立严格的反馈设计理论基础。

Method: 提出了算子相容性条件来刻画允许的反馈互连；通过去除闭环动力学中的奇异成分将退化系统化简为正则演化方程；利用临界-残差分解（critical-residual decomposition）产生一致指数稳定性证书；建立有限到无限提升定理将稳定性结论推广到原无穷维系统；最终获得构造性的静态输出反馈综合方案。

Result: 数值实验验证了正则化机制的合理性，确认了稳定性证书的有效性，并验证了有限到无限提升定理的正确性。

Conclusion: 该研究为时间退化抛物型分布参数系统的反馈设计提供了完整的理论框架，从反馈互连的良定性表征到稳定性分析再到输出反馈综合，形成了一条系统性的解决方案路径。

Abstract: Modern feedback design for distributed parameter systems presupposes that the closed-loop dynamics define a well-posed evolution problem. This presupposition becomes nontrivial for temporally degenerate parabolic systems, where temporal degeneracy affects not only the analytical properties of the evolution equation but also the mathematical formulation of the feedback interconnection itself. It is shown that admissible feedback interconnections for temporally degenerate parabolic systems are completely characterized by an operator compatibility condition linking the singular reaction operator with the actuator and observation operators. This characterization removes the singular component of the closed-loop dynamics and reduces the degenerate evolution equation to a regular evolution equation. Building upon this regularized formulation, a critical--residual decomposition yields a uniform exponential stability certificate, which is subsequently extended to the original infinite-dimensional evolution through a finite-to-infinite lifting theorem. A constructive static output feedback synthesis is finally obtained as a consequence of these results. Numerical experiments illustrate the regularization mechanism, validate the stability certificate, and confirm the finite-to-infinite lifting.

</details>


### [142] [Gradient-Free Optimization for Matrix functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03170)
*Sawyer Allen,Cash Cherry,Aidan Eck,Stephen Becker,Daniel McKenzie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在仅有方向导数（而非完整梯度）条件下优化矩阵变量的光滑非凸函数问题，该设置源于消费级硬件上微调大型神经网络时的内存限制。作者从信号处理角度将该问题建模为结构化恢复问题，提出了三种贡献：基于采样算子伪逆的替代梯度估计器、利用近似低秩梯度结构的矩阵感知估计器族、以及与谱下降（spectral descent）优化器结合实现成本摊销的策略。实验表明利用低秩特性可显著加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在消费级硬件上微调大型神经网络时，由于内存限制无法使用反向模式自动微分获取完整梯度，但可以通过前向模式获得方向导数。这一实际约束催生了在仅访问方向导数条件下优化矩阵变量函数的数学问题，需要设计高效的梯度估计方法。

Method: 1) 将梯度估计问题框架化为信号处理中的结构化恢复问题；2) 提出用采样算子的伪逆替代其伴随算子，构建替代随机梯度估计器；3) 当梯度满足近似低秩条件时，引入矩阵感知技术构建高精度梯度估计器族，可嵌入任意一阶方法；4) 利用梯度估计器输出的因子分解结果与谱下降等矩阵感知优化器结合，将投影步骤成本摊销为零额外开销。

Result: 通过两组关于近似低秩梯度合成函数的数值实验，证明利用低秩结构可获得远快于标准方法的收敛速度，达到良好的近似解。

Conclusion: 利用方向导数与近似低秩梯度结构相结合，可以在内存受限场景下实现高效的矩阵变量非凸优化，且与谱下降的结合提供了一条实际可行的计算路径。

Abstract: We consider the task of optimizing smooth, possibly non-convex functions of a matrix variable given access only to directional derivatives rather than full gradients. This setting arises when fine-tuning large neural networks on consumer-grade hardware: the network's weights are matrices, memory constraints rule out backward-mode automatic differentiation, but directional derivatives remain available through forward mode.
  We frame gradient estimation in this setting as a structured recovery problem, in the spirit of signal processing. From this perspective we provide three contributions. First, we introduce an alternative to the standard random gradient estimator; the difference corresponds to replacing the adjoint of the sampling operator with its pseudoinverse. Second, when the gradient satisfies an approximate low-rank condition, techniques from matrix sensing yield a family of highly accurate gradient estimators that drop into any first-order method. Third, we note that while the computational cost of such estimators is high, this can be amortized by combining them with a matrix-aware optimizer such as spectral descent. Specifically, the gradient estimator computes a factorization of the gradient, allowing for the projection step of spectral descent to be done at no extra cost.
  We demonstrate our findings with two careful numerical experiments on synthetic functions with approximately low-rank gradients. We show that by exploiting this low-rank property one obtains much faster convergence to good approximate solutions.

</details>


### [143] [An Exact Combinatorial Branch-and-Bound Algorithm for the Job Sequencing and Tool Switching Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03219)
*Alberto Locatelli,Jean-François Côté,Leandro C. Coelho*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对作业排序与刀具切换问题（SSP），提出了一种名为组合分支定界（C-B&B）的精确算法，整合了两个各具创新的分支定界算法及预处理阶段，成功解决了数十年来悬而未决的基准实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSP是柔性制造中的经典组合优化问题，尽管已有大量精确与启发式方法，但数十年前提出的仅含20个作业的基准实例至今仍未证明最优性，亟需更强大的精确算法来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: C-B&B算法整合三个核心组件：(1) 第一个分支定界算法采用新型分支方案以缩减隐式枚举树规模，并引入多个新的定界函数；(2) 第二个分支定界算法基于Laporte等人（2004）的分支方案，新增一个定界函数与两条支配规则加以强化；(3) 预处理阶段融入基于分支定界的启发式方法，可快速生成高质量初始可行解（上界）。

Result: 大量计算实验表明，C-B&B相比已有方法实现重大突破：以显著更少的计算代价证明更多实例的最优性，并关闭了多个公开数十年之久的基准实例。

Conclusion: C-B&B算法在处理SSP问题上展现出强大的求解能力，不仅超越了此前所有已发表方法的性能，还成功解决了长期未解的历史性开放实例，为该领域树立了新的基准。

Abstract: The Job Sequencing and Tool Switching Problem (SSP) is a well-known combinatorial optimization problem arising in the context of flexible manufacturing. Since the seminal work of Tang and Denardo (1988), the SSP has received significant attention in the literature, leading to the development of numerous exact and heuristic approaches. Despite these efforts, several benchmark instances proposed decades ago and containing only 20 jobs have remained unsolved to proven optimality. In this work, we propose an exact algorithm for the SSP, namely the Combinatorial Branch-and-Bound (C-B\&B) algorithm, which combines two distinct branch-and-bound algorithms, each introducing novel features compared with the existing literature. The former relies on a new branching scheme designed to reduce the size of the implicit enumeration tree, together with a collection of new bounding functions. The latter builds on the branching scheme introduced by Laporte et al. (2004) and strengthens it with a new bounding function and two dominance rules. Within C-B\&B, these exact algorithms are complemented by a preprocessing phase that incorporates a new branch-and-bound-based heuristic capable of rapidly generating a high-quality initial incumbent solution. Extensive computational experiments show that C-B\&B represents a strong breakthrough over previously published approaches, proving optimality for more instances with significantly less computational effort and closing several benchmark instances that have remained open for decades.

</details>


### [144] [Accelerated Prox-Level Methods for Unknown Piecewise-Smooth Optimization II: Function-constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03251)
*Zhenewei Lin,Zhe Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于凸函数约束优化的任意时间（anytime）且几乎无参数的算法——Restarted Penalty APEX（罚加速近端水平方法），适用于目标和约束函数为未知分段光滑函数的场景。该算法是首个在无需知道增长模量和精确罚系数的情况下，对满足二次增长条件的问题达到最优预言复杂度的算法，并能生成同时约束最优性间隙和约束违例的可验证证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的函数约束凸优化方法通常需要事先知道问题的结构参数（如增长模量或精确罚系数），这在实际场景中往往不可获得。此外，在目标和约束函数为分段光滑（piecewise-smooth）的情况下，设计一个既能达到最优复杂度又无需手工调参、同时能给出可验证收敛证书的算法仍是一个开放挑战。

Method: 提出的Restarted Penalty APEX算法基于罚方法框架，结合了加速束水平法（accelerated bundle-level method）。算法采用重启（restart）策略以自适应地处理二次增长性质，无需知道增长模量或精确罚系数。该算法还提供了可验证的最优性间隙和约束违例的联合证书，为任意时间（anytime）算法设计。

Result: 对于满足二次增长条件的函数约束优化问题，Restarted Penalty APEX是第一个在不需要知道增长模量或精确罚系数的前提下达到最优预言复杂度的算法。此外，该算法生成的既约束最优性间隙又约束约束违例的可验证证书在现有文献中似乎是全新的。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在分段光滑且参数未知的凸函数约束优化中同时实现了最优预言复杂度和可验证的自适应停止准则，所生成的最优性-可行性联合证书为文献中新增的贡献。

Abstract: We introduce an anytime, almost parameter-free algorithm for convex function-constrained optimization, in which the objective and constraint functions are unknown piecewise-smooth. Our algorithm, Restarted Penalty APEX (Penalty Accelerated Prox-level method for Exploring Piecewise Smoothness), is an accelerated bundle-level method based on the penalty approach. For problems satisfying quadratic growth, the proposed method is the first to achieve optimal oracle complexity without knowing the growth modulus or the exact penalty coefficient. Furthermore, Restarted Penalty APEX generates a verifiable certificate that bounds both the optimality gap and the constraint violation. This certificate also appears to be new to the literature.

</details>


### [145] [Mean-field optimal stopping with endogenous quantile cutoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03277)
*Erhan Bayraktar,Ibrahim Ekren,Xihao He*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有内生群体层面终止的均场最优停时问题：当存活质量跌破指定阈值时，所有剩余主体同时停止。文章将不连续目标重新表述为存活质量位于{0}∪[α,1]的奇异非凸约束，证明了强值与弱值的等价性、最优规则的存在性、动态规划原理，以及有限人口值向均场值的收敛性（在严格初始可行条件下），并分析了临界边界处的收敛失效现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有群体层面外生停止规则的均场最优停时问题中，当停止条件依赖于群体内生状态（如存活质量）时，目标函数呈现不连续性，传统方法难以直接处理。需要建立新的分析框架来刻画这类问题的最优解及其有限人口逼近行为。

Method: (1) 将不连续目标重新表述为存活质量处于{0}∪[α,1]的奇异非凸约束；(2) 通过逼近方法证明强值与弱值的等价性；(3) 利用紧致性和罚函数方法证明最优规则的存在性；(4) 建立动态规划原理；(5) 在严格初始可行条件下，通过紧性与恢复序列分析证明有限人口值的收敛性。

Result: 价值函数在临界边界之外连续，但在临界边界本身可能不连续。在严格初始可行条件下，有限人口值收敛到均场值，近优经验测度的分布在紧性意义下收敛，且每个均场最优者都存在恢复序列。然而在阈值处，有限人口收敛可能失效。

Conclusion: 本文为具有内生群体终止的均场最优停时问题提供了一个完整的理论框架，给出了值函数的正则性刻画和有限人口逼近的收敛性条件，并识别出临界边界处收敛失效的特殊现象，这对理解大规模主体系统中协同停止行为具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study a mean-field optimal stopping problem with an endogenous population-level shutdown. All remaining agents stop when the survival mass falls below a prescribed threshold. We recast the discontinuous objective as the singular, nonconvex constraint that the survival mass lie in $\{0\}\cup[α,1]$. We prove the equivalence of strong and weak values via an approximation and the existence of an optimal rule via compactness and penalization. We also prove a dynamic programming principle. The value is continuous away from the critical boundary but may be discontinuous at the boundary itself. Under strict initial feasibility, finite-population values converge to the mean-field value. In the same regime, the laws of near-optimal empirical measures are tight and every mean-field optimizer admits a recovery sequence. At the threshold, however, finite-population convergence may fail.

</details>


### [146] [Method of successive approximations for solving integral equations of actuarial mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03279)
*Bogdan Norkin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了经典保险风险过程（Cramer-Lundberg模型）的多种推广形式，包括变保费或随机保费、非泊松保费与索赔流、以及随机马尔可夫环境。针对这些推广模型推导了生存概率的积分方程及其方程组，建立了解存在唯一性的充要条件和充分条件，提出并验证了一种逐次逼近法及其收敛性，并开发了通过上下近似估计解精度的技术。该方法相比蒙特卡罗模拟等方法显著提高了精算计算的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Cramer-Lundberg风险过程假设保费流和索赔流均为泊松过程且环境静态，这与实际保险运营中保费可变化、索赔流非泊松、外部环境随机变化等现实情况不符。因此需要将这些理想化假设推广到更一般的模型，并发展相应的数值求解方法以提高破产概率计算的精度。

Method: 论文分别针对三类推广模型——（1）保费为确定性或随机可变的过程；（2）保费流与索赔流均为非泊松流的过程；（3）随机马尔可夫环境中的风险过程——推导出破产概率（或等价地生存概率）关于初始资本的积分方程。对于马尔可夫环境中的过程，建立了对应不同初始状态的生存概率积分方程组。随后对上述积分方程建立了解存在唯一性的一般充要条件及具体的充分条件，并提出逐次逼近法用于数值与解析求解，证明了其一致收敛性并给出了收敛速率。最后通过构造精确解的上下近似来估计近似解的误差范围。

Result: 理论结果表明逐次逼近法对上述各类推广模型的积分方程均一致收敛且具有可量化的收敛速率。数值实验验证了该方法的有效性，并与蒙特卡罗方法及已知的近似解进行了比较。所提出的方法能够以任意指定精度计算破产概率，可迭代地验证和改进经验近似值，并对蒙特卡罗模拟的精度与参数进行估计和修正，从而提高了精算计算的准确性。

Conclusion: 该研究为一般化保险风险过程中破产概率的计算提供了完整的理论框架与实用的数值方法。上下逼近技术使得误差可控，逐次逼近法兼具理论严谨性与工程实用性，是对蒙特卡罗方法的有力补充或替代，对精算实务中的风险管理具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: The dissertation considers various generalizations of the classical risk process describing the stochastic evolution of the capital of an insurance company (Cramer Lundberg model), including processes with variable deterministic or random premiums, non Poisson flows of premiums and claims, and a stochastic Markovian environment. Integral equations for the probability of nonruin as a function of initial capital are derived for these generalizations. For processes in a stochastic Markovian environment, systems of integral equations for nonruin probabilities corresponding to different initial states are obtained. General necessary and sufficient, as well as specific sufficient, conditions for the existence and uniqueness of solutions are established. A successive approximation method for numerical and analytical solution of the integral equations is theoretically and practically validated; its uniform convergence and rate of convergence are established. A technique for estimating the accuracy of approximate solutions is developed by constructing upper and lower approximations to the exact solution. The method is tested on numerical examples and compared with the Monte Carlo method and known solution approximations. The developed method increases the accuracy of actuarial calculations: it allows the probability of ruin to be calculated with any prescribed accuracy, empirical approximations to be verified and improved iteratively, and the accuracy and parameters of Monte Carlo simulations to be estimated and corrected.

</details>


### [147] [A Unified Theory of H-Duality in First-Order Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03281)
*Kevin Shu,Alex L. Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提供了两种互补的关于光滑强凸优化和收缩不动点问题中H-对偶性的解释。H-对偶指许多固定步长一阶方法（FSFOMs）的最终最坏情况性能恰等于其"时间反转"版本在配对性能设置下的最坏情况性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: H-对偶现象最初被用作自动将最终次优性保证的算法转换为最终梯度范数保证的算法，但尽管发现了更多H-对偶性能配对，其背后的统一解释一直缺失。

Method: 第一个解释：证明在具有极值曲率性质的一类实例上，FSFOMs产生的最终迭代点是最初迭代点的标量倍数，且该标量在时间反转下不变。第二个解释：利用性能估计证书概念，展示了一个显式变换可将算法收敛率的证书转换为其时间反转算法的证书。

Result: 两种互补的解释阐明了H-对偶产生的机制；作为应用，证明了ITEM的H-对偶的收敛保证，匹配了猜想的最优梯度到梯度保证。

Conclusion: H-对偶现象可以从极值曲率实例的标量不变性（几何角度）和性能估计证书的显式变换（代数角度）两方面得到统一解释。

Abstract: We provide two complementary explanations of H-duality in smooth strongly convex optimization and contractive fixed-point problems. H-duality refers to the phenomenon where the worst-case performance of many fixed-step first-order methods (FSFOMs) for a given performance setup are exactly equal to the worst-case performance of their "time-reversed" counterparts and a paired performance setup. This concept gained interest as a mechanism for automatically converting an algorithm with guarantees on the final suboptimality to algorithms with guarantees on the final gradient norm. Since its initial discovery, additional H-dual performance setup pairings have been discovered empirically, yet a general explanation for these phenomena remained elusive.
  Our first explanation of H-duality shows that, on a class of instances with extremal curvature properties, FSFOMs produce a final iterate that is a scalar multiple of the initial iterate, and that this scalar is invariant under the time reversal of the underlying FSFOM. This, together with the empirical observation that many FSFOMs have such an instance with extremal curvature as their worst case, gives one explanation for why H-duality often occurs. The second explanation is complementary and uses the notion of a performance estimation certificate of the convergence rate of a FSFOM. We exhibit an explicit transformation of such certificates that preserves many of the properties that define such a certificate, and we show that in many numerical and analytical examples, this transformation in fact transforms certificates of the convergence rate of an algorithm into certificates for its time-reversal. As an application, we prove a convergence guarantee for the H-dual of ITEM matching the conjectured optimal gradient-to-gradient guarantee.

</details>


### [148] [On the Complexity of Recognizing SDP Exactness for the Maximum Cut Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03508)
*Avinash Bhardwaj*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文解决了最大割问题（Max-Cut）半定规划（SDP）松弛精确性判定问题的计算复杂度。作者证明了对于加权图，判定SDP精确性是强NP难的（此前结果仅证明弱NP难），并进一步将此困难性扩展到简单无权重图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Delorme和Poljak的开创性工作证明了判定Max-Cut的SDP松弛是否精确（即最优值等于整数最大割）是NP难的，但其归约依赖于指数级缩放的边权，仅证明弱NP难，并明确留下了一个悬而未决的问题：对于简单无权重图，该问题的复杂度如何？本文旨在彻底解决这一复杂度问题。

Method: 作者采用两种构造性证明策略：(1) 对于加权图，通过构造一个具有多项式有界整数权重的平方和（sum-of-squares）对偶证书，将判定精确性归约为强NP难问题；(2) 对于简单无权重图，通过将受限Not-All-Equal 4-SAT问题几何嵌入到边不相交的团结构中，扩展困难性。两种证明均使用"几何锁定机制"（geometric locking mechanisms），迫使连续SDP松弛达到绝对全局最小值，从而将连续界的分析从底层组合困难性中解耦。

Result: 本文证明：(1) 加权图的SDP精确性判定问题是强NP难的（此前仅为弱NP难）；(2) 该困难性可延伸到简单无权重图的场景，从而完整解决了Delorme-Poljak遗留的复杂度开放问题。

Conclusion: 本文完全解决了最大割问题SDP松弛精确性判定问题的计算复杂度——即使在最简单的无权重图情况下，该判定问题也是NP难的。这排除了在一般情况下高效判定SDP精确性的可能性，对Max-Cut问题的理论和算法研究具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: The Semidefinite Programming (SDP) relaxation of the Maximum Cut (Max-Cut) problem is exact when its optimal value equals the integer maximum cut, geometrically corresponding to a rank-1 optimal solution. While the pioneering work of Delorme and Poljak established that recognizing exactness is NP-hard, their reduction relied on exponentially scaling edge weights. This established only weak NP-hardness and explicitly left open the complexity for simple, unweighted graphs. In this paper, we resolve the computational complexity of the exactness property. First, we prove that deciding SDP exactness for weighted graphs is strongly NP-hard by constructing a sum-of-squares dual certificate with polynomially bounded integer weights. Second, we extend this hardness to simple, unweighted graphs via a geometric embedding of restricted Not-All-Equal 4-SAT into edge-disjoint clique structures. Both proofs utilize geometric locking mechanisms that force the continuous SDP relaxation to an absolute global minimum, decoupling the continuous bounds from the underlying combinatorial hardness.

</details>


### [149] [Beyond Higher-Pulse Rectification: Operational Harmonic Coordination in Renewable P2H Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03531)
*Yangjun Zeng,Yiwei Qiu,Jie Zhu,Yi Zhou,Tao Wu,Xin Meng,Shi Chen,Buxiang Zhou,Kaigui Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种运行谐波协调方案，使低成本12脉波晶闸管整流器（TR）在可再生能源制氢（ReP2H）系统中满足并网谐波要求，替代高成本24脉波整流器，实现利润提升31%、整流级成本降低37.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶闸管整流器是ReP2H系统经济高效的电解电源，但其谐波可能违反电网规范。传统方案依赖更高脉波数的整流器（如24脉波）来抑制谐波，成本较高。因此需要一种协调运行方案，使低成本的12脉波整流器也能满足谐波要求，兼顾经济性与谐波合规性。

Method: 提出一个两层的运行协调框架：1）建立谐波模型，量化整流变压器（RCT）分接头位置和电解电流对谐波的影响，实现多个电解槽（ELZ）之间的谐波相互抵消；2）上层为小时级调度，在谐波可行域内利用随机规划和改进的渐进对冲算法确定ELZ启停，处理可再生能源不确定性；3）下层为分钟级调度，跟踪可再生能源出力并缓解谐波。此外提出分解算法，将生产调度与谐波抑制分离，提升计算效率。

Result: 基于实际工程案例的研究表明，所提方法相比仅电流调节方法利润提升31%。年度仿真进一步表明，协调的12脉波TR可通过增加RCT分接头动作以降低变压器投资，替代24脉波整流器实现谐波合规，将整流级成本降低37.5%。

Conclusion: 通过协调多个电解槽的运行与整流变压器分接头调节，低成本12脉波整流器可以替代高成本的24脉波整流器满足谐波合规要求，在保证电网谐波标准的同时显著提升系统经济性。

Abstract: Thyristor rectifiers (TRs) are cost-effective electrolysis power supplies for renewable power-to-hydrogen (ReP2H) systems, but their harmonics may violate grid-code limits. In contrast to conventional solutions that rely on higher-pulse (such as 24-pulse) rectifiers, this paper proposes an operational harmonic coordination scheme that enables low-cost 12-pulse TRs to meet harmonic requirements through coordinated operation. First, a harmonic model quantifies the effects of rectifier transformer (RCT) tap positions and electrolytic currents, enabling harmonic cancellation among multiple electrolyzers (ELZs). A two-layer framework then coordinates hydrogen production and harmonic mitigation. Hourly scheduling determines ELZ commitment within the harmonic feasible region under renewable uncertainty using stochastic programming and a modified progressive hedging algorithm, while minute-level dispatch tracks renewable power and mitigates harmonics. A decomposition algorithm separates production dispatch from harmonic mitigation to improve computational efficiency. Case studies based on real-life projects show that the proposed method increases profit by 31% over current-only regulation. Annual simulations further show that coordinated 12-pulse TRs can replace 24-pulse rectifiers for harmonic compliance by exchanging additional RCT tap actions for lower transformer investment, reducing rectification-stage cost by 37.5%.

</details>


### [150] [Well-posedness of Filtering Equations in Weighted Sobolev Spaces with Unbounded System Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03549)
*Zeju Sun,Songlin Zhou,Stephen S. -T. Yau*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在统一的缓冲加权Sobolev空间框架下，研究了连续时间非线性滤波中三个基本演化方程——鲁棒Duncan-Mortensen-Zakai (DMZ)方程、随机DMZ方程和Kushner-Stratonovich方程——的适定性问题。通过引入指数型权函数和相应的加权Sobolev空间，允许滤波系统系数具有无界多项式增长，在温和且易于验证的假设下建立了三个方程的弱解存在唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性滤波是现代控制理论的核心问题之一，其三个基本演化方程（鲁棒DMZ方程、随机DMZ方程和Kushner-Stratonovich方程）的适定性研究至关重要。然而，现有研究通常假设系统系数有界或满足特定增长约束，难以覆盖系数无界且呈多项式增长的一般非线性滤波系统。需要建立一种更一般的变分框架来统一处理这三个方程的适定性问题。

Method: 本文提出了一种统一的缓冲加权变分方法。具体而言：(1) 引入指数型权函数和相应的加权Sobolev空间，使得具有多项式增长的系数能够在变分框架下得到处理；(2) 在温和且易于验证的假设下，首先在加权空间中建立鲁棒DMZ方程弱解的适定性；(3) 利用规范（指数）变换及其逆变换，将鲁棒DMZ方程的适定性结果转移至随机DMZ方程和Kushner-Stratonovich方程，从而在缓冲加权Sobolev空间中统一证明三个方程解的存在唯一性。

Result: 在指数型加权Sobolev空间中，证明了鲁棒DMZ方程弱解的适定性；通过规范变换及其逆变换，将结果推广到随机DMZ方程和Kushner-Stratonovich方程，证明了这两个方程的解也在缓冲加权Sobolev空间中存在且唯一。同时总结了保证适定性的充分条件，验证了该框架对一般非线性滤波系统的广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 本文建立的统一缓冲加权框架成功地将三个滤波方程（鲁棒DMZ方程、随机DMZ方程和Kushner-Stratonovich方程）的适定性纳入统一的处理框架中，显著放松了对系统系数的限制条件（允许无界多项式增长），为更广泛的非线性滤波系统的理论分析提供了统一的数学工具。

Abstract: Nonlinear filtering problem is one of the core subjects in modern control theory. In this paper, we will study the well-posedness of the three fundamental evolution equations arising in continuous-time nonlinear filtering--the robust Duncan-Mortensen-Zakai (DMZ) equation, the stochastic DMZ equation, and the Kushner-Stratonovich equation--within a unified buffered weighted formulation. An exponential-type weight function and the corresponding weighted Sobolev spaces are introduced to enable a variational treatment of the filtering equations in a more general setting, in which the coefficients of the filtering system may be unbounded with polynomial growth. Under mild and easily verifiable assumptions, we first establish the well-posedness of the weak solution to the robust DMZ equation in these weighted spaces. Using the gauge (exponential) transformation and its inverse, these results are then transferred to the stochastic DMZ equation and the Kushner-Stratonovich equation, whose solutions are shown to exist and be unique in buffered weighted Sobolev spaces, yielding a unified treatment of all three filtering equations. Sufficient conditions for the well-posedness are also summarized, which illustrate the wide applicability of the proposed framework to general nonlinear filtering systems.

</details>


### [151] [The Traveling Tournament Problem: An Overview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03612)
*David Van Bulck,Fan Yang,Dries Goossens,Michael Trick*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对旅行商锦标赛问题（TTP）进行了全面的文献综述，涵盖其问题变体、基准实例、下界、近似保证、精确算法和启发式算法，并接替Michael Trick教授在RobinX项目中建立的下界与上界追踪仓库，同时提出了若干开放问题和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTP在过去25年已成为体育赛程安排中研究最广泛的优化问题之一。该问题源于美国职业棒球大联盟的赛程安排挑战，被认定为核心计算难题。作者旨在为该领域提供一份系统性的文献综述，并延续和更新已有的基准追踪工作，以支持该领域的持续发展。

Method: 本文采用文献综述的方法，系统梳理了TTP的主要问题变体、基准实例，总结了当前在下界、近似保证、精确算法和启发式优化算法方面的研究进展。同时，作者接管并维护了原由Prof. Michael Trick在RobinX体育赛程安排项目中建立的下界与上界追踪仓库，持续验证和更新相关的上下界结果。

Result: 作者提供了TTP研究领域的全面概述，包括问题变体、基准测试、下界技术和算法分类总结。此外，作者参与验证和追踪了TTP的上下界数据，继承了RobinX项目中Trick教授的存储库，为社区提供了更新的权威基准数据来源。

Conclusion: 通过系统性综述，本文确认了TTP在体育赛程优化领域的核心地位，识别了若干尚未解决的关键开放问题，并指出了有前景的未来研究方向，为该领域的后续研究提供了清晰的路线图。

Abstract: Over the past 25 years, the Traveling Tournament Problem (TTP) has become one of the most extensively studied optimization problems in sports scheduling. At its core, the TTP seeks to minimize the total travel distance incurred by teams that travel directly between opponents' venues during consecutive away games. The problem originated from the scheduling challenges faced by Major League Baseball, where it was identified as the central computational difficulty. This paper provides a comprehensive overview of the literature on the TTP. We review the principal problem variants and benchmark instances, and summarize the current state of the art in lower bounds, approximation guarantees, and exact and heuristic optimization algorithms. Moreover, we contribute to the continued development of the field by tracking and validating lower and upper bounds, while succeeding the repository originally established by Prof. Michael Trick as part of the RobinX sports timetabling project. Finally, we identify several open questions and outline promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [152] [Nash-Bargaining HalpernSGD via Limited-Memory MultiLRSGA: A Two-Phase Optimizer for Multi-Objective Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03616)
*Katherine Rossella Foglia,Francesco Sergio Pisani,Vittorio Colao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出NB-HalpernSGD优化器，通过LM-MultiLRSGA两阶段流程，先利用有限记忆变体近似Nash均衡作为参考点，再通过HalpernSGD求解Nash议价问题，实现多目标优化的Pareto解，在PINN模型中优于PCGrad、MultiAdam等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标优化问题需要同时平衡多个竞争性损失，现有方法如PCGrad、MultiAdam等在寻找Pareto最优解时缺乏原则性的参考点设定，且原始MultiLRSGA优化器内存开销过大，限制了其在大规模模型中的应用。

Method: 两阶段优化框架：(1)第一阶段采用LM-MultiLRSGA（MultiLRSGA的有限内存变体），通过将多目标问题转化为竞争博弈并逼近Nash均衡，获得Nash均衡诱导的分歧点作为参考点；(2)第二階段将原损失构造为Nash议价问题，将Nash积重写为对数最小化代理目标，使用HalpernSGD（锚定于计算的竞争性参考点）求解，最终导向Pareto解。

Result: 在PINN启发的神经模型上验证，NB-HalpernSGD在性能上优于PCGrad、MultiAdam和DualConeGD等已建立的多目标优化器。同时讨论了LM-MultiLRSGA阶段的收敛性和稳定性性质。

Conclusion: Nash均衡作为Nash议价过程的原理性参考点具有理论指导意义，所提出的两阶段方法有效结合了竞争博弈与议价建模的优点，且有限内存变体降低了计算开销，为多目标优化提供了新的可行方案。

Abstract: We propose NB-HalpernSGD via LM-MultiLRSGA, a two-phase optimizer for multi-objective optimization. The process starts with a competitive optimization phase by applying a limited-memory variant of MultiLRSGA, named LM-MultiLRSGA, designed to reduce the memory footprint of the original optimizer while preserving its rotational correction mechanism. It addresses multi-objective tasks by considering the competitive game associated with the original multi-objective problem, approximating a Nash equilibrium. This point defines a Nash-equilibrium-induced disagreement point: we formulate the Nash bargaining problem associated with the original losses and rewrite its Nash product as a logarithmic minimization surrogate. This minimization problem is solved using HalpernSGD, anchored at the computed competitive reference point. Therefore, the method uses the Nash equilibrium as a principled reference point for the bargaining stage and then moves toward a Pareto-oriented solution of the original multi-objective problem. We validate the proposed optimizer on a PINN-inspired neural model, where it outperforms established multi-objective optimizers, including PCGrad, MultiAdam, and DualConeGD. Finally, while the convergence properties of HalpernSGD have been extensively studied, we discuss the convergence and stability properties of the proposed LM-MultiLRSGA phase.

</details>


### [153] [On the $Ω(n)$ SDP relaxation gap for Submodular Box-Constrained Quadratic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03617)
*Junyu Zhang,Guanyi Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了子模盒约束二次规划（BCQP）问题中，带有限族线性割的半定规划（SDP）松弛的最坏情形松弛间隙。证明在维度n≥4时，任何带有限族线性割的SDP松弛都存在严格正的松弛间隙；进一步量化了该局限性的显式下界：含BQP有效割时松弛间隙为Ω(n)，含任意m个有效线性割时为Ω(n/m²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子模优化是一类具有全局保证的非凸问题，子模BCQP是其基本子类，在商业和管理场景（如定价）有直接应用。理解凸松弛方法（特别是SDP松弛）的局限性至关重要，因为松弛质量直接决定了能否获得强有效的下界或近似解。揭示这些松弛的理论间隙下界，可为设计更有效的凸松弛方法提供指导。

Method: 论文采用理论分析的方法。首先证明在n≥4维空间中，任何由有限族线性割构成的SDP松弛都无法完美求解所有子模BCQP实例（即存在严格正的松弛间隙）。然后，针对两类具体情形建立量化下界：(1) 仅含BQP有效割（包括McCormick和三角不等式）时，构造出松弛间隙为Ω(n)的实例；(2) 含任意有限m个有效线性割时，建立Ω(n/m²)的松弛间隙下界。通过构造特定反例实例进行最坏情形分析。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 维度n≥4时，任何带有限族线性割的SDP松弛对子模BCQP都存在严格正的松弛间隙；(2) 在归一化BCQP实例下，仅含BQP有效割的SDP松弛具有Ω(n)的松弛间隙下界；(3) 对任意m个有效线性割的SDP松弛，建立了Ω(n/m²)的松弛间隙下界。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了SDP松弛（尤其是带BQP有效割的SDP）在处理子模BCQP时的固有局限性，表明仅靠有限线性割无法消除松弛间隙。这为寻求更有效的凸近似方法指明了方向——可能需要引入超出线性割（如非线性割或特定非线性约束）的结构来提升松弛质量，从而更好地逼近子模BCQP问题的全局最优解。

Abstract: Continuous submodular optimization is an important class of nonconvex problems with global guarantees, for which the submodular box-constrained quadratic programming (BCQP) forms a fundamental subclass. It arises naturally as quadratic subproblems of general twice-differentiable submodular problems and also has direct business and management applications such as pricing. This paper studies the worst-case relaxation gap of semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxations with a finite family of valid linear cuts for submodular BCQP. We show that in dimension $n\geq4$, for any SDP relaxation formulated with a finite family of linear cuts, there always exists a submodular BCQP instance with a strictly positive relaxation gap. By restricting to the normalized BCQP instances that remove the problem's scaling factor, we further quantify this limitation with explicit lower bounds. In general dimension $n\geq4$, when the SDP relaxation is formulated with only Boolean-quadric-polytope (BQP) valid cuts, including the commonly used McCormick and triangle inequalities, we establish an $Ω(n)$ relaxation gap lower bound. For an arbitrary family of $m$ valid linear cuts, we establish an $Ω(n/m^2)$ lower bound. These results capture the fundamental limitations of SDP relaxations, especially SDP with BQP valid cuts, and provide guidance on what additional constraints may be needed for more effective convex approximations of submodular BCQP.

</details>


### [154] [Transversality Conditions for Boundary Constraints Defined by Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04084)
*Isaac M Ross*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对边界条件由微分方程（而非代数终点流形）定义的最优控制问题，推导出通用的初始时刻和终端时刻横截性条件，并引入了协调/非协调时钟时间与弱伴随余向量等新概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最优控制理论假设边界条件定义在终点流形上，然而N体系统中的轨迹优化问题（以及更一般的不可积动力系统）的边界条件由微分方程组及其附加条件定义，需要新的横截性条件理论支撑。

Method: 本文首先建立了微分边界条件的严格数学定义，作为理论基础；进而引入协调/非协调时钟时间及弱伴随余向量的新概念，在此基础上推导出通用的初始和终端时刻横截性条件，并证明了相应定理。

Result: 在非协调时钟时间情形下，新的横截性条件揭示了一个特殊情形：弱伴随余向量与边界微分方程的向量场正交，这与经典横截性条件中"余向量与终点流形正交"的表述有本质区别。

Conclusion: 推导出的定理具有通用性，可广泛适用于边界条件由微分方程定义的一类最优控制问题；其应用已在三体问题的多个案例中得到验证（详见其他论文）。

Abstract: What are the transversality conditions for an optimal control problem when the boundary conditions are defined by differential equations? This seemingly bizarre question is motivated by trajectory optimization problems in the $N$-body system. The question, however, is more fundamental and goes beyond problems in astrodynamics to nonintegrable dynamical systems in general. The main contribution of this paper is the development of generic initial- and final-time transversality conditions for optimal control problems whose boundary conditions are defined in terms of differential equations with side conditions. The mathematical definition of differential boundary conditions are part of the foundations developed in this paper. To support the new fundamentals, the concept of coordinated/uncoordinated clock times and weak adjoint covectors are introduced. In the case of uncoordinated clock times, the new transversality conditions reveal that there exists a special situation where a weak adjoint covector is orthogonal to the vector field of the boundary differential equation. This condition is sharply different from the classical statement of orthogonality with respect to the endpoint manifold. The theorems developed in this paper are generic. An application of the theorems to several cases in the three-body problem are described in separate papers.

</details>


### [155] [Bi-objective Two Phase Transportation Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03671)
*Prabhjot Kaur,Kalpana Dahiya,Vanita Verma*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个双目标两阶段运输问题（BTPTP），将源-目的地链路划分为两个不相交的阶段，要求第一阶段运输全部完成后才能进行第二阶段。论文提出了一种多项式时间的迭代算法BTPTP-Algorithm，通过求解受限最小成本运输问题来系统地找出所有非支配解，并提供了理论证明、数值算例及不同规模实例的CPU时间分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段运输问题中需要同时最小化两个相互冲突的目标（运输时间和运输成本），现有方法难以有效找到该问题的全部非支配解，因此需要开发一种系统且高效的算法来解决这一多目标优化挑战。

Method: 提出BTPTP-Algorithm，一种多项式时间迭代算法。在每次迭代中求解特定的受限成本最小化运输问题，并在前一阶段获得的Phase-I和Phase-II运输时间的基础上对相应源-目的地链路施加限制，从而系统地排除所有支配解并记录所有非支配解。算法附有完备性证明。

Result: 通过数值算例验证了理论结果，并对不同规模的BTPTP实例进行了计算实验，给出了以CPU时间衡量的算法计算性能表现。

Conclusion: BTPTP-Algorithm能够系统地找到两阶段双目标运输问题的全部非支配解，且算法的时间复杂度为多项式级别，计算行为在不同规模实例上表现良好。

Abstract: This paper discusses a bi-objective two-phase transportation problem (BTPTP) in which transportation takes place in two phases. The whole set of source-destination links is partitioned into two disjoint sets namely, Phase-I and Phase-II. The transportation among the source-destination links of Phase-II set is done only after the transportation among the source-destination links of Phase-I set is complete. The transportation among the source-destination links in each phase is done in parallel. The problem discussed in this paper concentrates on minimizing two objectives viz., sum of transportation times and sum of transportation costs of both the phases, simultaneously.
  Due to conflicting nature of the objectives, a polynomial time iterative algorithm named as BTPTP-Algorithm is developed that finds all of its non-dominated solutions. At each iteration, the BTPTP-Algorithm solves some specific restricted cost minimizing transportation problems and finds corresponding optimal feasible solutions. The restrictions imposed on the source-destination links of Phase-I and Phase-II sets, in each of these problems, depend upon their corresponding Phase-I and Phase-II transportation times obtained at the previous step. The algorithm is developed in such a way that it expels all the dominated solutions and records all the non-dominated solutions of the problem, systematically. A proof of the BTPTP-Algorithm's capability of recording all the non-dominated points is provided. Further, a numerical illustration is given in support of the theory and computational behavior of the BTPTP-Algorithm for various BTPTP instances of different sizes, is provided in terms of CPU time.

</details>


### [156] [Automated Priority Rule Design for the Resource-Constrained Project Scheduling Problem: A Large Language Model-Guided Population-Based Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03754)
*Jingyu Luo,Mario Vanhoucke,José Coelho*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）引导的群体搜索框架，用于自动化设计资源受限项目调度问题（RCPSP）的优先规则。LLM在离线搜索中生成并迭代修正候选规则，训练项目上的调度质量作为适应度反馈。实验表明，LLM设计的规则在测试集上优于传统单一规则和同等搜索努力下的GP设计规则，且在大规模高并行项目上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限项目调度问题（RCPSP）中，优先规则启发式因其低计算成本和显式决策逻辑被广泛使用，但传统规则在不同项目上表现不一致。虽然遗传规划（GP）超启发式是自动化规则设计的主流方法，但进化出高性能规则需要评估大量候选规则，计算代价高。近年来大语言模型的进展使得利用性能反馈生成和迭代修正优先规则成为可能，从而提供了一种更高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出一个LLM引导的群体搜索（population-based）框架用于自动化优先规则设计。在离线搜索阶段，LLM负责生成和修订候选规则；候选规则的适应度由其在训练项目上的调度质量决定，该质量反馈进一步引导后续的规则修订。搜索结束后，返回最优规则并直接应用于未见过的项目，无需额外搜索或调用LLM。

Result: 实验结果表明：LLM设计的规则在测试集上优于所有传统单一规则；在可比搜索努力下优于GP设计的规则；与大幅扩大搜索规模的GP结果相比仍具竞争力。在大规模高并行项目上，选定的LLM设计规则优于所有传统规则及两种遗传算法配置。消融研究揭示了主要搜索组件对性能的影响，规则分析描述了LLM设计规则的结构与决策行为。

Conclusion: LLM引导的方法为自动化优先规则设计提供了一种高效且有效的新途径，在低搜索代价下能够产生竞争力强甚至超越现有方法的调度规则，展现出替代传统GP超启发式方法的潜力。消融研究和规则分析为理解LLM辅助搜索的工作机制提供了深入见解。

Abstract: The objective of the resource-constrained project scheduling problem (RCPSP) is to minimize makespan while satisfying precedence and renewable-resource constraints. Priority-rule heuristics construct feasible schedules with low computational cost and explicit decision logic, making them widely used in practice and an attractive alternative to more computationally intensive methods. However, no traditional rule performs consistently well across projects, and researchers have therefore investigated automated priority-rule design. Genetic programming (GP) hyper-heuristics have been the predominant approach to this task, but evolving a high-performing rule may require evaluating many candidate rules on the training projects. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) make it possible to generate and iteratively revise priority rules using performance feedback.
  This paper presents an LLM-guided population-based framework for automated priority-rule design. During the offline search, an LLM generates and revises candidate rules, while schedule quality on training projects determines candidate fitness and guides subsequent revisions. At the end of the search, the best rule is returned and applied directly to unseen projects without further search or LLM calls.
  Experiments show that the LLM-designed rules outperform traditional single rules across the test sets and outperform GP-designed rules obtained under comparable search effort, while remaining competitive with rules obtained from a substantially larger GP search. On large projects, selected LLM-designed rules outperform all considered traditional rules and two genetic algorithm configurations on highly parallel projects. An ablation study examines how changes to the main search components affect performance, while rule analyses describe the structure and decision behavior of the LLM-designed rules.

</details>


### [157] [Lifetime Profit-Maximising Co-optimisation of Multi-Service Stacking for Battery Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03767)
*Yuxin Xia,Frederik Schiele,Yihong Zhou,Volkan Kumtepeli,David Howey,Iacopo Savelli,Thomas Morstyn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一个考虑电池老化的滚动时域框架，用于电网级储能的多服务叠加收益优化，并在英国市场（含Enduring Auction Capability平台）验证。结果表明考虑老化可将终身收入提升最多32%，高保真老化模型还可再提升16%，且随着电池老化应动态调整最优服务组合以最大化终身利润。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电网级电池储能可通过在电力和频率响应市场叠加服务创造收入，但如何在多个服务之间找到终身利润最大化的叠加策略仍然具有挑战性。服务之间通过共享电池容量耦合，受系统运营商能源管理规则约束，还受产品特定技术要求影响系统运行、老化和终身盈利能力，需要一个能联合优化多服务叠加的老化感知框架。

Method: 提出一个老化感知的滚动时域框架，协同优化多服务叠加策略。该框架显式考虑产品特定特征和能量状态（State-of-Energy）合规规则，并应用于英国市场，其中储能运营商可叠加电力交易与多种动态频率响应服务（经Enduring Auction Capability平台采购），且受国家能源系统运营商（NESO）的能源管理要求约束。

Result: 使用真实市场数据验证：（1）相对于不考虑老化的基准，考虑老化可将终身收入提升最多32%；（2）更高保真度的老化建模相较于简单公式可提供额外最多16%的收入提升；（3）较低贴现率倾向于平衡日历老化与循环老化的策略，而较高贴现率倾向于激进运行；（4）所有策略中，响应正频率偏差的服务始终优于响应负频率偏差的服务；（5）随着电池老化，应动态调整最优服务组合以最大化终身利润。

Conclusion: 电池老化建模、贴现率和电池老化状态是决定终身价值与最优叠加策略的关键因素。终身利润最大化需要将老化纳入优化框架，并在电池寿命周期内自适应地调整服务组合。

Abstract: Grid-scale battery energy storage can generate revenue by stacking services across electricity and frequency response markets, yet identifying the lifetime profit-maximising stacking strategy remains challenging. Decisions across services are coupled through shared battery system capacity, constrained by system operator energy management rules, and further shaped by product-specific technical requirements that govern system operation, degradation, and lifetime profitability. This paper presents an ageing-aware receding-horizon framework for co-optimising multi-service stacking that explicitly captures product-specific characteristics and state-of-energy compliance rules. The framework is applied to the Great Britain market, where storage operators can stack electricity trading with multiple dynamic frequency response services procured through the newly introduced 'Enduring Auction Capability' platform under energy management requirements imposed by the National Energy System Operator. Using real market data, we demonstrate that degradation modelling, discount rate, and battery ageing jointly govern both lifetime value and optimal stacking strategy. Accounting for ageing increases lifetime revenue by up to 32% relative to a degradation-agnostic benchmark, while higher-fidelity ageing modelling can provide a further revenue improvement of up to 16% over simpler formulations. Lower discount rates favour strategies that balance calendar and cycling ageing, while higher discount rates favour aggressive operation. Across all strategies, services responding to positive frequency deviations are consistently preferred over those responding to negative deviations. Lifetime profit is maximised by adapting the optimal service mix as the battery ages.

</details>


### [158] [Data-Driven Brownian Reflection Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03870)
*Guodong Pang,Dacheng Yao,Hao Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究布朗运动模型下漂移和波动率未知的数据驱动反射控制问题，提出三种算法：LTO（先学习后优化）、AU（自适应更新）和AU-FH（全历史自适应更新），分别实现O(√T)和O(log T)的期望遗憾界，并将遗憾分解为探索、瞬态和学习三部分进行分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际反射控制问题中，系统参数（漂移和波动率）通常是未知的，需要在控制过程中进行在线学习与优化权衡，因此需要设计数据驱动的算法来平衡探索与利用，实现有限时间内的最优遗憾保证。

Method: 首先提出LTO算法：在探索阶段估计策略相关参数并代入最优性方程得到策略；进一步提出AU和AU-FH算法，连续更新估计器和反射水平。遗憾分解为探索、瞬态和学习三部分：瞬态遗憾通过受控反射布朗运动半群到平稳性的时间积分偏差界来约束（结合Foster-Lyapunov不等式），学习遗憾通过估计器的一致性、均方误差界和局部光滑性来控制稳态成本差距，并利用移动边界Skorokhod映射的单调性通过路径比较导出AU算法切换状态的矩界。

Result: LTO算法实现O(√T)的有限时间期望遗憾界；AU和AU-FH算法实现改进的O(log T)遗憾界，其中AU-FH利用全部历史数据在数值模拟中表现更优。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了数据驱动反射控制问题的系统理论框架，证明了从简单的先学习后优化到自适应的连续更新算法可以有效缩小遗憾上界，同时揭示了探索、瞬态与学习三类误差源对总体遗憾的贡献结构。

Abstract: We study a data-driven reflection control problem for a Brownian model with unknown drift and volatility. We first propose a learn-then-optimize (LTO) algorithm: it estimates the policy-relevant parameter during exploration, plugs the estimate into the optimality equation, and exploits the resulting policy---achieving an $O(\sqrt{T})$ finite-time expected regret bound. We further propose two algorithms, adaptive-updating (AU) and full-history adaptive-updating (AU-FH), which continuously update the estimator and reflecting level, attaining an improved $O(\log T)$ regret bound. Notably, AU-FH algorithms leverages all historical data, yielding better performance in numerical simulations. Our analysis decomposes regret into exploration, transient, and learning components. Transient regret from nonstationarity is bounded by the time-integrated deviation of the transition semigroup from stationarity evaluated on the holding cost, which can be further bounded via a Foster-Lyapunov inequality for exponential convergence of the controlled reflected Brownian motion (RBM). For learning regret, we establish local regularity properties together with consistency and mean-squared error bounds for the estimator, which control the stationary cost gap between the learned and optimal reflection policies. In addition, we leverage the monotonicity of the moving boundary Skorokhod map to derive moment bounds for the AU algorithms' switching states, via pathwise comparison with fixed-boundary RBMs.

</details>


### [159] [Asymptotic Analysis of Gradient Mapping-type Stationarity Measure for the Sum of Nonconvex Nonsmooth Functions and Applications to Proximal Gradient-type Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03950)
*Keita Kume,Isao Yamada*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对两个可能非光滑非凸函数之和，提出了一种基于梯度映射类型的平稳性度量。在适当正则性假设下，证明了该度量沿某个收敛序列渐近消失可以刻画Fréchet或近端正平稳性，并建立了平滑化变体的渐近结果。作为应用，回答了Olikier-Waldspurger提出的开放问题，并提出了一个带非单调线搜索的邻近变量平滑算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非光滑非凸优化的平稳性表征是核心挑战之一，尤其是在处理两个函数之和（通常涉及光滑项加非光滑正则项或两个非光滑非凸项）时，缺乏统一的平稳性度量工具。此外，学界对近端梯度法产生的聚点是否为近端平稳点存在悬而未决的开放问题，需要系统性回答。

Method: 提出一种梯度映射型平稳性度量，结合Fréchet和近端平稳性的经典定义，在局部Lipschitz平滑、下半连续等正则性假设下证明度量渐近消失与平稳性刻画之间的等价性。同时发展该度量的平滑化变体，与现有的平滑技术（如变量平滑）相融合，并在此框架下提出带非单调线搜索的邻近变量平滑算法。

Result: （1）在局部Lipschitz光滑条件下，回答了Olikier-Waldspurger的开放问题：近端梯度法产生的每个聚点都是近端平稳点；（2）提出的邻近变量平滑算法（带非单调线搜索）在较低正则性和prox-正则条件下，保证满足平稳性度量消失的子序列的每个聚点都是Fréchet平稳点。

Conclusion: 本文构建了统一的平稳性度量框架，既提供了理论上的平稳性刻画工具，又推动了非光滑非凸优化算法的收敛性分析，填补了相关理论空白并解决了已有开放问题。

Abstract: We propose a gradient mapping-type stationarity measure for the sum of two possibly nonsmooth nonconvex functions. Under suitable regularity assumptions, we show that Fréchet or proximal stationarity can be characterized through asymptotic vanishing of the proposed measure along some convergent sequence. We also establish analogous asymptotic results for a smoothing-based variant of the measure, which enables us to combine the proposed framework with smoothing techniques developed for nonsmooth optimization. As an application of our analysis of the stationarity measure, we provide an affirmative answer to an open question raised by [Olikier-Waldspurger, SIAM J. Optim., 2025] on whether every cluster point of a sequence generated by a proximal gradient method is a proximal stationary point under local Lipschitz smoothness of one component of the cost function. As a second application, we propose a proximal variable smoothing algorithm with a nonmonotone linesearch for minimizing the sum of two nonsmooth nonconvex functions under lower regularity of one component and prox-regularity of the other. For the proposed algorithm, we show that every cluster point of a subsequence such that the stationarity measure vanishes is a Fréchet stationary point.

</details>


### [160] [Heavy-Tailed First-Order Optimization for Polyak-Łojasiewicz Condition: High-Dimensional Minimax Bounds, High-Probability Guarantee, and Fixed-Dimensional Improvements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03990)
*Weiming Ou,Xiao Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究光滑Polyak--Łojasiewicz（PL）优化问题，在条件无偏且满足α阶矩假设（1<α≤2）的随机梯度下，推导了噪声自适应的复杂度下界，并给出了匹配的截断镜像下降算法的高概率上界，同时刻画了固定维度下随机复杂度的精确特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的PL优化研究大多假设有界梯度或次高斯噪声，且未考虑维度与oracle预算的耦合关系。本文旨在建立仅依赖α阶矩（1<α≤2）的条件无偏随机梯度下的噪声自适应性下界与匹配算法，并厘清固定维度下随机项复杂度的精确行为，填补该领域的理论空白。

Method: （1）在维度可能依赖oracle预算的场景下，构造对抗性分布族证明噪声自适应下界，该下界在σ=0时退化为无噪声PL下界；（2）在适当的mirror-PL条件下，设计中心化截断镜像下降（centered-clipped mirror-descent）方法，在无有界域、无有界梯度、无次高斯假设下获得匹配的高概率上界（至多差对数因子）；（3）对固定维度d=1,2,3给出精确的随机项复杂度；对d>3，在α/(α-1)≥d-1时保持同样刻画，在互补区间给出含表面熵因子的上界并指出剩余间隙。

Result: 证明了噪声自适应下界T_ε=Ω_α[κ log(Δ_0/ε)+κ(σ²/(με))^{α/(2(α-1))}]；在mirror-PL条件下给出截断镜像下降算法达到匹配上界（差对数因子）；固定维度d=1,2,3以及d>3且α/(α-1)≥d-1时，随机项最优复杂度为Θ̃_α[(σ²/(με))^{α/(2(α-1))}]；其余情形给出带表面熵因子的上界并明确残余间隙。

Conclusion: 本文确立了PL优化在α阶矩（1<α≤2）噪声下的完备复杂度刻画，揭示了噪声项指数α/(2(α-1))的本质作用，表明即使在弱假设下（无有界梯度、无次高斯），截断技术也能达到近乎最优的收敛率。固定维度结果揭示了维度对随机复杂度的深刻影响（特别是d>3时的关键阈值α/(α-1)≥d-1），为后续研究留下明确的开放间隙。

Abstract: We study smooth Polyak--Łojasiewicz (PL) optimization with conditionally unbiased stochastic gradients satisfying \[ \mathbb E\!\left[ \|G_t-\nabla f(x_t)\|^α\mid\mathcal F_{t-1} \right]\le σ^α, \qquad 1<α\le2. \] When the dimension may depend on the oracle budget, we prove the noise-adaptive lower bound \[ T_ε=
Ω_α\!\left[ κ\log\frac{Δ_0}ε +
κ\left( \frac{σ^2}{με} \right)^{\fracα{2(α-1)}} \right], \] which recovers the noiseless PL lower bound when $σ=0$. Under the appropriate mirror-PL condition, we give a centered-clipped mirror-descent method attaining the matching high-probability upper bound up to logarithmic factors, without bounded-domain, bounded-gradient, or sub-Gaussian assumptions.
  We further characterize the stochastic complexity in prescribed fixed dimensions. For $d=1,2,3$, the optimal stochastic term is \[ \widetildeΘ_α\!\left[ \left( \frac{σ^2}{με} \right)^{\fracα{2(α-1)}} \right]. \] For every fixed $d>3$, the same characterization holds whenever \[ \fracα{α-1}\ge d-1. \] In the complementary regime, we provide an upper bound with an additional surface-entropy factor and explicitly identify the remaining gap.

</details>


### [161] [DREAM: Deep-Reparametrization of Adaptive Regularization Maps for Fast Zero-Shot Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04019)
*Thanh Trung Vu,Ander Biguri,Christoph Kolbitsch,Luca Calatroni,Kostas Papafitsoros,Andreas Kofler*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出DREAM框架，通过算法展开在零样本自监督设置下学习深度重参数化的自适应正则化映射（用于TV和TGV），无需大规模配对数据集。在二维图像去噪和动态MRI重建中验证，该方法收敛性质有理论保证、学习效率高（所需权重更新远少于SOTA方法）、CNN参数化提供隐式先验和降维，且自适应映射具有高度可解释性。在动态心脏MRI重建中，零样本训练性能可与监督模型匹敌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典变分重建方法具有可解释性和数学结构，但灵活性不足；端到端深度学习方法性能强但需要大规模配对数据集且缺乏理论保证。作者希望在保留变分方法可解释性和收敛性理论保障的同时，提升其灵活性，并解决参考数据难以获取时的训练问题。

Method: 采用算法展开（algorithm unrolling）技术，以原始-对偶（primal-dual）算法为构建模块，学习由CNN参数化的深度自适应正则化映射（用于TV和TGV）。在零样本、自监督设置下训练，无需配对数据集。CNN参数化既能充当隐式先验，也能提供有效的降维。

Result: 在二维图像去噪中验证了DREAM的有效性；在动态MRI重建（尤其心脏MRI）中，零样本训练能达到与监督模型相当的性能。相比SOTA方法，DREAM需要更少的权重更新即达到可比性能。CNN参数化优于直接优化自适应参数。与端到端深度学习方法相比，DREAM在监督与零样本训练之间的性能差距更小。

Conclusion: DREAM为难以获取参考数据的场景提供了一种有吸引力的替代方案：既保留了经典变分方法的理论收敛保证和可解释性，又通过深度重参数化的自适应正则化获得灵活性，且零样本训练可匹敌监督性能。自适应正则化映射的高可解释性是其相对纯深度学习方法的核心优势。

Abstract: Adaptive regularization is an effective means of improving the flexibility of classical variational reconstruction methods while retaining their interpretability and mathematical structure. In this work, we propose an approach for learning deep-reparameterized adaptive regularization maps (DREAM) for Total Variation (TV) and Total Generalized Variation (TGV) through algorithm unrolling in a zero-shot, self-supervised setting, requiring no access to large paired datasets. We first validate DREAM on a two-dimensional image-denoising problem and then apply it to large-scale dynamic MRI reconstruction. The resulting adaptive models are theoretically grounded, as they retain the convergence properties of the primal-dual algorithm used as the building block of the unrolled architecture. Our results show that the regularization parameter maps can be learned efficiently, requiring substantially fewer weight updates than state-of-the-art methods to achieve comparable performance. We further demonstrate that the CNN parametrization of the maps acts as an implicit prior and provides effective dimensionality reduction, outperforming direct optimization of the adaptive parameters. Across both applications, the performance gap between supervised and zero-shot training is smaller for DREAM than for the end-to-end deep-learning methods considered for comparison. Remarkably, in dynamic cardiac MRI reconstruction, zero-shot training matches the performance of the supervised model. Moreover, the learned adaptive maps are highly interpretable, providing a compelling alternative to purely deep-learning-based methods when reference data are difficult to obtain.

</details>


### [162] [Stronger Lower Bounds for (Non-)Anytime Acceleration of Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04032)
*Minchan Jung,Hanseul Cho,Chulhee Yun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究固定步长梯度下降法在L-光滑凸目标上的加速收敛问题，通过建立更强的下界来缩小现存上下界之间的差距：非任意时刻设置下界从Ω(N^{-1.635})提升到Ω(N^{-1.450})，任意时刻设置下界从Ω(N^{-1.241})提升到Ω(N^{-1.184})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管传统观点认为固定步长梯度下降的收敛率最优为Θ(N^{-1})，近期研究发现通过非恒定、非自适应、确定性的步长调度可以加速vanilla GD。然而，目前技术栈中上下界之间存在显著差距（非任意时刻：O(N^{-1.271}) vs Ω(N^{-1.635})；任意时刻：O(N^{-1.119}) vs Ω(N^{-1.241})），需要更强的下界来缩小这些间隔，从而更好地理解GD的收敛极限。

Method: 作者通过构造更精妙的"最坏情况"凸目标函数和特定的步长调度场景，分析GD在此类目标上的迭代轨迹，推导出更强的收敛率下界。在非任意时刻设置中建立了Ω(N^{-1.450})的下界，在任意时刻设置中建立了Ω(N^{-1.184})的屏障下界。

Result: 在非任意时刻（non-anytime）设置中，将GD收敛率的已知下界从Ω(N^{-1.635})提升至Ω(N^{-1.450})；在任意时刻（anytime）设置中，将已知下界从Ω(N^{-1.241})提升至Ω(N^{-1.184})，显著缩小了与已知上界之间的差距。

Conclusion: 通过更强的下界证明，本文为理解带确定性非恒定步长调度的GD在L-光滑凸优化中的收敛速率提供了更精确的刻画，进一步缩小了理论上下界之间的鸿沟，为未来寻找最优步长调度策略或更紧的上界分析提供了新的参考基线。

Abstract: The rate-optimal convergence rate of gradient descent (GD) with a fixed step-size is well known to be $Θ(N^{-1})$ for $L$-Lipschitz smooth convex objectives in the prior art in convex optimization. Surprisingly, several recent works show that we can accelerate vanilla GD by applying a nonconstant, nonadaptive, deterministic step-size schedule. The best-known upper bounds so far in the non-anytime & anytime setups are $O(N^{-1.271})$ [Altschuler and Parrilo, 2025, Grimmer et al., 2023] and $O(N^{-1.119})$ [Zhang et al., 2025], respectively. On the other hand, the best reported lower bounds (or barriers) up to date in the non-anytime & anytime setups are $Ω(N^{-1.635})$ and $Ω(N^{-1.241})$[Ye and Liu, 2026], respectively. We narrow these gaps by establishing stronger lower bounds for GD's convergence rate in both settings: $Ω(N^{-1.450})$ for the non-anytime rate bound and $Ω(N^{-1.184})$ for the anytime rate barrier.

</details>


### [163] [Factor-parity Hall sets and controllability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04056)
*Karine Beauchard,Frédéric Marbach*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类称为"因子奇偶 Hall 集"的 Hall 集类，其元素分为好括号和坏括号。作者证明了好括号可以在短时间内同步且任意地引导，从而为控制仿射系统的小时间局部可控性提供充分条件；同时证明每个坏括号都会产生可控性的障碍，构成必要条件的族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立控制仿射系统小时间局部可控性（STLC）的充要条件。现有工作（如 Kawski、Agrachev-Gamkrelidze 和 Krastanov）仅针对特定构造给出充分性证明，作者希望构建一个统一的 Hall 集理论框架，以系统化地刻画可控性的正反两方面条件。

Method: 作者引入因子奇偶 Hall 集（factor-parity Hall sets），将 Hall 括号分为好括号（good brackets）和坏括号（bad brackets）。基于 Kawski、Agrachev-Gamkrelidze 和 Krastanov 的构造方法，证明了所有好括号可以同时且任意地被"引导"（steered）到小时间状态空间中的期望方向，从而得到 STLC 的充分条件；反过来，对每个坏括号建立可控性障碍论证，推导出必要条件族。

Result: 建立了关于控制仿射系统小时间局部可控性的充分条件（基于好括号的可同时引导性）和必要条件（基于坏括号产生的障碍），两类条件分别由 Hall 集中括号的奇偶性结构决定。

Conclusion: 因子奇偶 Hall 集提供了统一的组合框架，将 STLC 的充分与必要条件分别与"好"和"坏"括号对应，推广并统一了已有文献中的构造结果，为控制仿射系统的可控性分析提供了系统化工具。

Abstract: We introduce a class of Hall sets, which we call factor-parity Hall sets, whose elements split into good and bad brackets. We prove that the good brackets can be steered simultaneously and arbitrarily in small time, which yields sufficient conditions for the small-time local controllability of control-affine systems. This positive result draws on constructions of Kawski, Agrachev-Gamkrelidze and Krastanov. Conversely, we prove that each bad bracket generates an obstruction to controllability, hence a family of necessary conditions.

</details>


### [164] [Zero sum two-player differential game under three regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04100)
*Brahim El Asri,Magnoudéwa Paka*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个具有三种不同机制（体现系统内机制切换动态）的两人零和随机微分博弈，通过粘性解方法推导显式解，分析Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman-Isaacs方程，并探究不同机制间相互作用对值函数结构的影响，最终在适当假设下给出值函数的显式表示及最优策略的系统性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际金融和工程系统中普遍存在机制切换（regime switching）现象，而现有零和随机微分博弈文献对此类多机制场景的显式解研究不足。作者旨在填补这一空白，为理解机制间交互如何影响值函数结构提供系统的理论框架。

Method: 采用粘性解（viscosity solutions）方法对三类不同机制的两人零和随机微分博弈进行分析。具体而言，研究与博弈相关的Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman-Isaacs (HJBI) 方程，考察不同机制之间的相互作用如何影响值函数的结构。作者进一步假设已知切换区域的定性结构，以此推导出值函数在各配置下的显式表达式。

Result: 在合适的假设条件下，获得了多种切换系统配置下的值函数显式解，系统性地刻画了两玩家的最优策略，并展示了在已知切换区域定性结构时如何推导相应的值函数。

Conclusion: 所提出的粘性解框架为多机制零和随机微分博弈提供了系统的显式求解途径，能够揭示机制间交互对值函数结构的影响，为后续研究更复杂的机制切换博弈问题奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper investigates a two player zero-sum stochastic differential game characterized by three distinct regimes, reflecting the regime switching dynamics within the system. We aim to derive an explicit solution for a number of configurations of the switching system by means of the viscosity solutions approach. In particular, we analyze the associated Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman-Isaacs equations and examine how the interactions between the different regimes affect the structure of the value function. The proposed framework provides a systematic approach to characterize the optimal strategies of the two players and to obtain explicit representations of the value function un der suitable assumptions. We also illustrate how to derive the value functions in case we know the qualitative structure of switching regions.

</details>


### [165] [A Stochastic Riemannian Alternating Descent Ascent Method for Nonsmooth Composite Expectation Optimization on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04116)
*Meng Xu,Bo Jiang,Ya-Feng Liu,Anthony Man-Cho So*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一类黎曼非光滑复合期望优化问题（出现在机器学习、信号处理和统计学中），提出了一种名为StoRADA-RM的随机黎曼交替下降上升法（带递归动量）。该方法每次迭代执行一步或多步黎曼随机梯度下降和一步近端梯度上升，并采用无需向量传输的递归动量估计器（每迭代仅需O(1)次随机梯度评估）。作者证明该方法能在O(ε⁻³)次迭代内返回ε-黎曼随机稳定点，SFO调用复杂度同样为O(ε⁻³)，均为该问题类别中的最优已知界，后者甚至匹配平滑非凸优化中随机一阶算法的理论下界。数值实验（稀疏主成分分析和坐标无关稀疏估计）验证了方法的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼非光滑复合期望优化问题广泛存在于机器学习和统计应用中，但现有算法在处理此类问题时要么复杂度不理想，要么需要高昂的黎曼运算成本。作者希望提出一种高效且具有最优复杂度保证的算法来求解这类结构化minimax重表述问题。

Method: StoRADA-RM算法：利用问题固有的minimax重表述结构，在每次迭代中交替执行黎曼随机梯度下降步（一步或多步）和近端梯度上升步。其核心创新是提出了一种无需向量传输（vector transport-free）的递归动量估计器来计算黎曼随机梯度，将每迭代的随机梯度评估量降至O(1)常数水平，降低了运算开销。

Result: StoRADA-RM在O(ε⁻³)次迭代内返回ε-黎曼随机稳定点，同时SFO调用复杂度为O(ε⁻³)。这两个复杂度界均为该类问题中目前已知最优的，且SFO复杂度匹配了平滑非凸随机一阶优化的最优下界。数值实验在稀疏PCA和坐标无关稀疏估计两个任务上展示了方法的优越性能。

Conclusion: StoRADA-RM为黎曼非光滑复合期望优化问题提供了理论与实际双重优越的解决方案：理论上达到当前已知最优的迭代复杂度和SFO复杂度（后者的界匹配下界），实践中通过向量传输自由的动量估计器显著提升计算效率，并在多个代表性应用任务中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider a class of Riemannian nonsmooth composite expectation optimization problems, which arises in various machine learning, signal processing, and statistics applications. Noting that these problems admit structured minimax reformulations, we propose an efficient algorithm, named stochastic Riemannian alternating descent ascent method with recursive momentum (StoRADA-RM), to tackle them. StoRADA-RM performs one or multiple Riemannian stochastic gradient descent steps and then a proximal gradient ascent step at each iteration. To compute the Riemannian stochastic gradient, we propose a vector transport-free recursive momentum estimator that requires only $O(1)$ stochastic gradient evaluations per iteration. We prove that StoRADA-RM returns an $ε$-Riemannian-stochastic-stationary point of a given problem in the said class in $O(ε^{-3})$ iterations while making $O(ε^{-3})$ calls to a stochastic first-order oracle (SFO). Both the iteration complexity and SFO complexity bounds are the best known in the literature for the said class of problems. The latter even matches the optimal lower bound for smooth nonconvex optimization with stochastic first-order algorithms. We then present numerical results on sparse principal component analysis and coordinate-independent sparse estimation to demonstrate the superior performance of our proposed method.

</details>


### [166] [Computation of Strong Solutions to Stochastic Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04188)
*Yao Ji,Guanghui Lan,Jason Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了单调变分不等式（VIs）的强解计算问题，基于累加正则化（AR）思想开发了通用框架，在随机或acles下实现了近最优的复杂度，并推广了状态相关噪声模型的分析范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在残差缩减方面的随机复杂度高达O(σ²/ε⁴)，远非最优；同时状态相关噪声分析此前仅局限于强单调设定。作者旨在开发一种通用框架，同时逼近理论下界并扩展噪声模型的适用范围。

Method: 基于累加正则化（AR）思想构建通用框架，利用无偏随机或acles（方差一致有界σ²）进行计算。对于状态相关噪声模型，引入增强的随机算子外推（SOE）方法配合AR使用。

Result: 在随机oracles下，AR以最多Õ(LD₀/ε + σ²/ε²·(log(LD₀/ε))³)次调用获得期望算子残差≤ε的解，相比现有O(σ²/ε⁴)有显著改进，且与下界仅差对数因子。强单调情形下，已知模量时达到最优复杂度；仅当作单调问题处理时同样近最优。状态相关噪声模型下达到近最优复杂度。

Conclusion: AR框架在残差缩减指标下实现了近最优随机复杂度，其对数因子改进将σ²/ε⁴降低至σ²/ε²级别；提出的状态相关噪声模型将分析扩展至一般单调VIs（含非唯一解情形），增强SOE方法进一步保障了此设定下的近最优性。

Abstract: This paper studies the computation of strong solutions of monotone variational inequalities (VIs) with Lipschitz continuous operators. Building on the idea of accumulative regularization, we develop a general framework for VIs, with particular emphasis on stochastic settings. Under unbiased stochastic oracles with uniformly bounded variance $ σ^2$, AR computes an approximate solution with expected operator residual bounded by $\varepsilon$ using at most $ \widetilde{O}\left(\tfrac{LD_0}{\varepsilon}+\tfrac{ σ^2}{\varepsilon^2}(\log\tfrac{LD_0}{\varepsilon})^3\right) $ stochastic oracle calls, where $L$ is the Lipschitz constant and $D_0$ bounds the initial distance to the solution. It substantially improves the existing $\mathcal{O}( σ^2/\varepsilon^4)$ complexity for residual reduction and matches the lower bound up to logarithmic factors. For strongly monotone VIs, measured by the distance to the solution, AR achieves the optimal oracle complexity when the strong monotonicity modulus is known. By treating the problem as merely monotone, AR still achieves nearly optimal complexity without knowledge of this modulus. We further introduce a state-dependent noise model applicable to general monotone VIs with potentially nonunique solutions, extending state-dependent noise analysis beyond the strongly monotone setting. Under this model, AR, when equipped with an enhanced stochastic operator extrapolation (SOE) method, achieves nearly optimal complexity with the stochastic term depending on the variance at a solution.

</details>

# q-bio.PE [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Architecture-dependent Effects of Trade-offs on Evolutionary Navigability and Epistasis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02961)
*Nandita Chaturvedi*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文研究了可调多层基因型-表型映射结构如何与适应度权衡相互作用，影响进化动力学的多个方面（如崎岖度、可导航性、上位性及高适应度群体的微观进化）。研究发现这些关系高度依赖映射架构，权衡在多架构下效应不同，且等位基因频率的时间协方差可源自基因型-表型映射的内部结构本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因型-表型映射的结构在决定群体如何穿越适应度景观方面起着核心作用，但人们对该结构如何与环境约束和适应度权衡相互作用知之甚少。作者希望填补这一认知空白，探究映射架构是否以及如何调节突变效应、权衡与进化结果之间的联系。

Method: 构建一种可调的多层基因型-表型映射：二进制基因型通过多个前馈层逐层转换，最后由表型级适应度函数评估。显式建模表型到适应度的转化，以比较突现权衡适应度与无权衡对照。通过调节基因型长度和层数，探索不同深度与宽度的映射架构，并分析景观崎岖度、可导航性、上位性以及高适应度群体的微观进化动态。

Result: 发现崎岖度与可导航性之间存在复杂且依赖架构的关系：宽且浅的映射即使有极少的局部极大值也可具低可导航性；宽且深的映射在平均崎岖度水平下可导航性仍然很高。权衡能提高宽浅映射的可导航性，但在其他架构下效应被削弱。权衡对上位性的影响取决于进化历史，上位性相互作用的背景程度由映射架构决定。此外，高适应度群体的等位基因频率时间协方差可由崎岖基因型-表型映射的内部结构产生，形成类似于短期交替选择的信号。

Conclusion: 映射架构是决定种群进化动态的关键调节因素，权衡的作用具有高度的架构依赖性。研究者应警惕将时间协方差等进化信号简单地归因于外部选择性压力，因为基因型-表型映射的内部拓扑也可能产生相似的假象性信号。该框架为连接基因型-表型映射结构、环境约束与宏观进化模式提供了统一的可调平台。

Abstract: The structure of the genotype-phenotype map plays a central role in determining how populations move through fitness landscapes, yet less is known about how this structure interacts with environmental constraints and fitness trade-offs. Here, we study evolution on a tunable multilayer genotype-phenotype map in which binary genotypes are transformed through successive feed-forward layers before being evaluated by phenotype-level fitness functions. We explicitly model the phenotype to fitness transformation in order to compare an emergent trade-off fitness with a no-trade-off control. We explore different map architecture as models of more and less complex genotype-phenotype relationships by tuning the genotype length and number of layers. We find that ruggedness and navigability have a complex, architecture-dependent relationship. Landscapes with few local maxima can still have low navigability for wide and shallow maps, and navigability is high even at mean values of ruggedness for wide and deep maps. Trade-offs increase navigability in wide and shallow maps, while their effect is muted in other architectures. The effect of trade-offs on epistasis depends on evolutionary history. While trade-offs change which mutational neighborhoods are sampled by evolution, the background degree of epistatic interactions is set by map architecture. Finally, we use our framework to investigate micro-evolution of high-fitness populations and show that temporal covariance in allele-frequency changes can arise from the internal structure of a rugged genotype-phenotype map, producing signatures that resemble short-term alternating selection.

</details>
